## 450229

## 450231

## 450232

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 450248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかってるよ…。　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>ガルドランは殺さない。<line-break>ガルドランが死んでも<line-break>僕、別にうれしくないから。<end>"

## 450249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それに、何が必要だったのか<line-break>見失ってた僕の目を<line-break>覚まさせてくれたのはガルドランだし。<end>"

## 450250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "む、何かは知らぬが、彼らも正義を　<line-break>愛する正しく、熱い魂らしい…。<line-break>　<line-break>どうやら、君たちのことを俺様は<line-break>誤解していたようだ…！　これからも<line-break>俺様を応援してくれ、正しき魂よ！<end>"
EN: "Hmm. I know not what that was, but they too appear to be righteous, passionate souls devoted to justice. It seems I misjudged you! Continue to cheer me on, O righteous souls!<end>"
KO: "음, 뭔지는 모르겠지만 그들도 정의를 사랑하는 올바르고 뜨거운 영혼인 모양이군…. 아무래도 내가 너희를 오해했던 것 같다…! 앞으로도 나님을 응원해 다오, 올바른 영혼들이여!<end>"

## 450251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様はこれからも俺様街道を伝説へと<line-break>走り続けるだろう！　君たちと俺様の<line-break>魂の輝きは誰にもとめられないのだ！<line-break>さらばだ！　熱くさまよえる魂よ！<line-break>応援ありがとう！<end>"
EN: "I shall keep racing down my own road toward legend! No one can halt the radiance of our souls! Farewell, you passionate, wandering souls! Thank you for your support!<end>"
KO: "이 몸은 앞으로도 이 몸만의 길을 전설로 만들며 달릴 것이다! 너희와 이 몸의 영혼의 빛은 누구도 막을 수 없다! 잘 있어라, 뜨겁게 방황하는 영혼들이여! 응원 고맙다!<end>"

## 450255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 450257

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 450262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつは俺にこんなケガさせたんだぞ？<line-break>あいつは俺のメンツをつぶしたんだ。<line-break><line-break>ナッジ、<value:$28>、<line-break>お前ら、俺の友達じゃないのかよ？<line-break>友達がこんな目にあっても、お前らは…<end>"

## 450263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、ヴァン、力には使い方がある。<line-break>そうしないと正しい力にならないんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>欲や、独りよがりな力の誇示や<line-break>メンツなんかのために<line-break>使われちゃダメなんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Listen, Van. Power has to be used the right way, or it isn't righteous power at all. It mustn't be used for greed, self-serving displays of strength, or something like saving face.<end>"
KO: "있지, 반. 힘에는 쓰는 방법이 있어. 그래야 올바른 힘이 되는 거야. 욕심이나 자기만족식 힘 자랑, 체면 따위를 위해 써서는 안 돼.<end>"

## 450264

KO: "체면 따위 때문에, 라고오!?<end>"

## 450265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前、友達である俺のメンツを、<line-break>メンツなんかのためにの一言で…！！<end>"
EN: "I'm your friend, and you dismiss my honor as “something like saving face”...?!<end>"
KO: "너, 친구인 내 체면을 '체면 따위 때문에' 한마디로…!!<end>"

## 450266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァン、僕ら、ヴァンのためにも<line-break>思いとどまったんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>ヴァンが僕らに誰かを殺してほしい<line-break>なんて思わないはずだろう？<end>"
EN: "Van, we held back for your sake too. You wouldn't really want us to kill someone for you, would you?<end>"
KO: "반, 우리는 너를 위해서라도 그만둔 거야. 네가 우리에게 누군가를 죽여 달라고 바라진 않을 거잖아?<end>"

## 450267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前ら…、俺のことなんか、<line-break>ホントはどうでも、いいんだな？<end>"
EN: "You... You don't really care about me at all, do you?<end>"
KO: "너희들… 사실은 나 같은 건 아무래도 상관없는 거지?<end>"

## 450268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違うよ、ヴァン。<line-break>ねえ、わかってるはずだろ？<end>"
EN: "That's not true, Van. Come on, you know that, don't you?<end>"
KO: "아니야, 반. 있잖아, 너도 알고 있잖아?<end>"

## 450269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もういい！　頼まねえ！！<line-break>俺ひとりでもガルドランを地獄に送る！<end>"
EN: "Forget it! I won't ask you again! I'll send Girldlan to hell myself!<end>"
KO: "됐어! 부탁 안 해!! 나 혼자라도 갈드란을 지옥으로 보내겠어!<end>"

## 450270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってよ！　ヴァン！<end>"
EN: "Wait, Van!<end>"
KO: "기다려! 반!<end>"

## 450271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…困ったなあ。<line-break>ヴァンたら、へそ曲げちゃって…。　　<line-break>　<line-break>自分だけ、のけ者にされた気分なんだ。<line-break>ガルドランなんて、もう、<line-break>どうでもいいはずなのに…。<end>"
EN: "This is a problem. Van's gone off in a huff... He feels as though he was the only one left out. Girldlan shouldn't even matter to him anymore...<end>"
KO: "…곤란하네. 반이 삐쳐 버려서…. 자기만 따돌림당한 기분인가 봐. 갈드란 따위는 이제 아무래도 상관없을 텐데….<end>"

## 450272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、<value:$28>、<line-break>一緒にヴァンを捜そうよ。<line-break>そうじゃないと、ヴァンは本当に…。<end>"
EN: "Come on, <value:$28>. Let's look for Van together. Otherwise, he really might...<end>"
KO: "저기, <value:$28>, 같이 반을 찾자. 안 그러면 반은 정말로….<end>"

## 450273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、一緒に冒険しながら<line-break>ヴァンの行方を捜そうね。<end>"
EN: "Then let's look for Van while we travel together.<end>"
KO: "그럼 같이 모험하면서 반의 행방을 찾아보자.<end>"

## 450274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、あんたが<value:$28>か。<line-break>ナッジが世話になってるみたいだな。<line-break>　<line-break>今日は親父もおふくろも<line-break>留守にしてるんだ。<line-break>あんた、運がいいぜ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, so you're <value:$28>. Sounds like you've been looking out for Nadge. My old man and my mom are both out today. Lucky you.<end>"
KO: "아, 네가 <value:$28>구나. 나지가 신세를 지고 있는 모양이네. 오늘은 아버지도 어머니도 집에 안 계셔. 너 운이 좋군.<end>"

## 450275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつは俺にこんなケガさせたんだぞ？<line-break>あいつは俺のメンツをつぶしたんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>ナッジ、お前、<line-break>俺の友達じゃないのかよ？<line-break>友達がこんな目にあっても、お前は…<end>"
EN: "He did this to me! He humiliated me. Nadge, aren't you my friend? Your friend gets treated like this, and you still...<end>"
KO: "그 녀석은 나한테 이런 상처를 입혔어. 내 체면을 짓밟았다고. 나지, 너 내 친구 아니야? 친구가 이런 꼴을 당해도 넌…<end>"

## 450276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 450278
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 450279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 450280
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おなかが…すいて…。<end>"
EN: "I'm... so hungry...<end>"
KO: "배가… 고파서….<end>"

## 450281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、飢えか！　よし、任せろ！！　<end>"
EN: "Gah, hunger! All right, leave it to me!!<end>"
KO: "큭, 굶주림인가! 좋아, 맡겨 둬!!<end>"

## 450282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よかろう！　勇者が胸元で暖めていた<line-break>ちょっと古めのパンを食え！<end>"
EN: "Very well! Eat this slightly stale bread that this Hero kept warm against his chest!<end>"
KO: "좋다! 용자가 품속에 데우고 있던 조금 오래된 빵을 먹어라!<end>"

## 450283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ありがとうございました。<line-break>これで元気が出ました。<end>"
EN: "Th-thank you. I feel better now.<end>"
KO: "아, 감사합니다. 이제 기운이 났어요.<end>"

## 450284
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勇者の熱き魂に触れる者<line-break>また、１人、か。<end>"
EN: "Another person touched by a Hero's fiery soul...<end>"
KO: "용자의 뜨거운 혼에 닿은 자가 또 한 명인가.<end>"

## 450285
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…しまった！　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break><line-break>俺様自身、何日も食ってなくて…<line-break>餓死寸前であのパンを拾ったのを<line-break>忘れていた…！<end>"
EN: "Oh no! I'd forgotten that I hadn't eaten in days myself... and that I found that bread when I was on the verge of starving to death...!<end>"
KO: "…아차! 나도 며칠이나 못 먹어서… 굶어 죽기 직전에 그 빵을 주운 걸 잊고 있었어…!<end>"

## 450286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへっ！　無様だな。<line-break>未来の勇者様よお！？　<line-break>　<line-break>人助けしたのはいいが<line-break>自分も救えないのか？<line-break>ハッ、ザマぁねえなあ！<end>"
EN: "Heh! What a pathetic sight, Future Hero! You helped someone, but you can't even save yourself? Ha! What a joke!<end>"
KO: "헤헷! 꼴사납군. 미래의 용사님이 말이야!? 남을 도운 건 좋은데 자기 자신도 못 구해? 하, 꼴 좋다!<end>"

## 450287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっ、熱き魂を持つ者は<line-break>…ち、違う…のだ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Those who possess a fiery soul... a-are different...<end>"
KO: "흥, 뜨거운 혼을 가진 자는… 다, 다르…군.<end>"

## 450288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当なら、お前らの小屋を焼いて<line-break>お前らをこの町に入れないところだ！<line-break>　<line-break>ボンガが、よそ者のくせに<line-break>みんなを説得するから、しぶしぶ、<line-break>町の出入りだけは許しているが…<end>"
EN: "If I had my way, I'd burn down your shack and bar you from this town! But Bonga, outsider that he is, talked everyone around, so we've grudgingly agreed to let you come and go...<end>"
KO: "원래라면 너희 오두막을 불태우고 이 마을에 발도 못 들이게 했을 거다! 본가가 외지인 주제에 모두를 설득해서 마지못해 마을 출입만 허락하고 있지만….<end>"

## 450289
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ご、ごめんなさい。<line-break>ちょっと、考えごとしてて…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, s-sorry. I was lost in thought...<end>"
KO: "아, 미, 미안해요. 잠깐 생각에 잠겨서….<end>"

## 450290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あれ？　君は前に…。<end>"
EN: "H-huh? You're the one from before...<end>"
KO: "아, 어라? 너 전에….<end>"

## 450291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>っていうんだね。<line-break>よろしく、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "So your name is <value:$28>. It's nice to meet you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>라고 하는구나. 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 450292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、もしよかったら…<line-break><value:$28>と一緒に<line-break>冒険させてもらえないかな？<end>"
EN: "Um, if you don't mind... could I come adventuring with you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "저기, 괜찮다면… <value:$28>와 함께 모험하게 해 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 450293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕、強くなりたいんだ…。<line-break>でも、ひとりだと、強さってなんなのか<line-break>見失ってしまいそうで…。<end>"
EN: "I want to become stronger... But if I'm alone, I feel like I might lose sight of what strength really means.<end>"
KO: "나, 강해지고 싶어…. 하지만 혼자 있으면 강함이 뭔지 잃어버릴 것 같아서….<end>"

## 450294
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、それじゃ、僕<line-break><value:$28>が<line-break>呼び出してくれるのを待ってるよ。<end>"
EN: "All right. I'll wait for you to call for me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "응, 그럼 난 <value:$28>가 불러 주길 기다릴게.<end>"

## 450295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、よろしくね。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Well then, I look forward to traveling with you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 450296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 450297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec045-part99.toml

## 460002

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 460003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、ヒドー兄弟も堕ちたもんだ。　　<line-break>犯罪結社ライギロアの下請けとは！<line-break><line-break>しかし、背に腹は代えられねえか。<line-break>…にしても、ハンサムな男をさらえって<line-break>いったい、なんでこんなことを…。<end>"
EN: "Damn, the Hido Brothers really have sunk low. Subcontractors for the criminal organization Raigiloa! Still, we don't have much choice. But why would anyone ask us to kidnap a handsome man...?<end>"
KO: "큭, 히도 형제도 추락했군. 범죄조직 라이기로아의 하청이라니! 그래도 먹고살려면 어쩔 수 없나. …그런데 잘생긴 남자를 납치하라니 대체 왜 이런 짓을….<end>"

## 460004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て！　悪人！<end>"
EN: "Halt, evildoer!<end>"
KO: "거기 서! 악당!<end>"

## 460005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "未来の勇者ガルドラン！<end>"
EN: "Girldlan, the Hero of the Future!<end>"
KO: "미래의 용사 갈드란!<end>"

## 460006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！　ガルドランだとォ！？<line-break>ガルドランを相手にするほど堕ちたのか？<line-break><line-break>なめやがって！　この俺が、俺が<line-break>悪者人生一筋のこの俺が、ここまで<line-break>堕ちるとは！！　ひどい！　ひどい！<end>"
EN: "G-Girldlan?! Have we sunk so low that we're taking on Girldlan now? You've got some nerve! Me―I've devoted my whole life to villainy―and I've been reduced to this?! It's awful! Just awful!<end>"
KO: "큭! 갈드란이라고!? 갈드란을 상대할 정도로 내가 추락했단 말인가? 얕보지 마! 악당 인생 외길인 이 내가, 내가 여기까지 추락하다니!! 너무해! 너무하다고!<end>"

## 460007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こうなれば、技の１号と呼ばれていた<line-break>昔の栄光の片鱗をたっぷり見せてやる。　<line-break>覚悟しろ！　小僧めが！<end>"
EN: "Then I'll show you a taste of my former glory, from the days when they called me Technique No. 1. Prepare yourself, brat!<end>"
KO: "이렇게 된 이상, 기술의 1호라 불리던 옛 영광의 편린을 실컷 보여 주마. 각오해라! 이 애송이 녀석아!<end>"

## 460008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この前、<value:$28>に<line-break>やられた腰が…！！！<end>"
EN: "My back! It's still hurt from what <value:$28> did to it last time...!!!<end>"
KO: "지난번에 <value:$28>한테 당한 허리가…!!!<end>"

## 460009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドラン伝説キーック！！<end>"
EN: "Girldlan Legendary Kiiick!!<end>"
KO: "갈드란 전설 킥!!<end>"

## 460010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪者め…、貴様も味わうがいい…。<line-break>俺様の愛と正義と伝説の肉体攻撃を！<end>"
EN: "Villain... now you too shall taste my love, my justice, and my legendary physical attack!<end>"
KO: "악당 놈… 네놈도 맛보아라…. 나의 사랑과 정의와 전설의 육체 공격을!<end>"

## 460011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひ、ひいいい！<line-break>ずらかれー！<end>"
EN: "E-eeek! Run for it!<end>"
KO: "히, 히익! 튀어!<end>"

## 460012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、未来の勇者にとっては他愛もない<line-break>敵だった…。<line-break><line-break>さぁ、来るんだお嬢さん！！<line-break>君の恋人はこの未来の勇者ガルドランが<line-break>助け出してあげたよおおおん！！<end>"
EN: "Hmph. No more than a trifling foe for the Hero of the Future. Now, come to me, young lady! Girldlan, the Hero of the Future, has rescued your beloved for yooouuu!<end>"
KO: "흥, 미래의 용사에겐 별것 아닌 적이었다…. 자, 이리 오시오 아가씨!! 당신의 연인은 이 미래의 용사 갈드란이 구해 냈소오오!!<end>"

## 460013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ブレンダ…来てくれたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Brenda... you came.<end>"
KO: "브렌다… 와줬구나.<end>"

## 460014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "コアン…無事でよかった。<end>"
EN: "Koan... thank goodness you're safe.<end>"
KO: "코안… 무사해서 다행이야.<end>"

## 460015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<line-break>おかげで助かりました！<end>"
EN: "Thank you so much. You saved me!<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 덕분에 살았습니다!<end>"

## 460016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハハ！　君も俺様の熱き魂を感じて<line-break>彼女にふさわしい男になるのだな！<end>"
EN: "Ha ha! Feel the fire of my soul, and become a man worthy of her!<end>"
KO: "하하! 너도 나의 뜨거운 혼을 느끼고 그녀에게 어울리는 남자가 되는구나!<end>"

## 460017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい！　でも、ガルドランさんには<line-break>とても女の子にしつこいって噂があるから…<line-break>私、どうしてもつきまとわれたら恐いから…！<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry! But Girldlan, I've heard you're terribly persistent with girls... and I'm afraid you'll start following me around!<end>"
KO: "미안해요! 하지만 갈드란 씨는 여자아이한테 아주 집요하다는 소문이 있어서… 혹시라도 계속 따라다니면 무서워서요…!<end>"

## 460018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "確かに…！！　あなたが、もしかしたら、<line-break>恩返しを盾に彼女に近づいたりして、<line-break>僕たちにとって驚異になるかもしれない！<end>"
EN: "That's true! You might use the debt she owes you as an excuse to get close to her―and become a threat to us!<end>"
KO: "확실히…!! 당신이 혹시 은혜를 갚는다는 구실로 그녀에게 접근해서 우리에게 위협이 될지도 몰라!<end>"

## 460019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめんなさい、ガルドランさん！<line-break>そうなる前に、あなたを殺して、<line-break>私たち、私たちの愛を貫きます！！<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, Girldlan! Before that can happen, we'll kill you and see our love through to the end!<end>"
KO: "미안해요, 갈드란 씨! 그렇게 되기 전에 당신을 죽이고 우리, 우리의 사랑을 지키겠어요!!<end>"

## 460020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたの貴い犠牲は無駄にはしません！！<line-break>僕たち、きっと幸せになります！！<line-break>だから、死んでいてください、それじゃ！<end>"
EN: "Your noble sacrifice will not be in vain! We promise we'll be happy! So please stay dead. Goodbye!<end>"
KO: "당신의 숭고한 희생은 헛되게 하지 않겠습니다!! 저희는 반드시 행복해질게요!! 그러니 죽어 있어 주세요, 그럼 이만!<end>"

## 460021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐふっ！<end>"
EN: "Guh!<end>"
KO: "크헉!<end>"

## 460022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへっ！　無様だな。　　　　　　　<line-break>未来の勇者様よお！？<line-break><line-break>人助けしたのはいいが<line-break>そいつらにハメられて、このザマか？<line-break>ま、これも日頃の行いだな？<end>"
EN: "Heh! Look at you, mighty Hero of the Future! You save someone, only to get taken in and end up like this? Guess that's what you get for the way you live.<end>"
KO: "헤헷! 꼴사납군. 미래의 용자님!? 남을 도운 건 좋지만 그놈들에게 속아서 이 꼴이냐? 뭐, 이것도 평소 행실 탓이지?<end>"

## 460023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…誰もが俺様のような熱き魂を<line-break>持っているわけではない…。<line-break><line-break>臆病なのは仕方ない…<line-break>だが、熱き魂を持つ者は<line-break>…ち、違う…のだ。<end>"
EN: "Not everyone possesses a fiery soul like mine... Cowardice is only natural. But one who possesses a fiery soul is... d-different.<end>"
KO: "…누구나 나처럼 뜨거운 영혼을 가진 건 아니다…. 겁이 많은 건 어쩔 수 없어… 하지만 뜨거운 영혼을 지닌 자는… 다, 다르다….<end>"

## 460024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ？　どう違うんだア？<end>"
EN: "Yeah? Different how?<end>"
KO: "어? 뭐가 다른데?<end>"

## 460025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じ、自分のためではなく<line-break>あ、愛のために力を使わねばならん。<end>"
EN: "Y-you must use your strength not for yourself, but for l-love.<end>"
KO: "자, 자신을 위해서가 아니라 사, 사랑을 위해 힘을 써야 한다.<end>"

## 460026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何、恥ずかしいこと言ってんだよ！<line-break>このタコ！！<end>"
EN: "What kind of embarrassing crap is that, you moron?!<end>"
KO: "뭘 부끄러운 소릴 하는 거야! 이 문어야!!<end>"

## 460027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様は、あるコーンスの少年と<line-break>その仲間たちにそれを、教わった。　　<line-break><line-break>俺様はコーンスの少年とその友人を<line-break>テラネで傷つけた。少年は復讐のため<line-break>俺様をねらっていた…はずだった。<end>"
EN: "A boy from Konsu and his companions taught me that. I hurt the boy and his friend in Terane. He was after me for revenge... or so I thought.<end>"
KO: "나님은 어떤 콘스 소년과 그 동료들에게 그걸 배웠다. 나님은 콘스 소년과 그 친구를 테라네에서 상처 입혔다. 소년은 복수하려고 나님을 노렸을… 터였다.<end>"

## 460028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、その少年は２度も、<line-break>俺様を窮地から救ってくれた…。<line-break>いや、こう言っては少し違うな…。　　<line-break>少年はその友人を守るためだけに<line-break>力を使おうとしたから<line-break>俺様を窮地から救う結果になったのだ。<end>"
EN: "Yet that boy saved me from dire straits twice. No―that is not quite right. He used his strength solely to protect his friend, and saving me merely happened as a result.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 소년은 두 번이나 나님을 위기에서 구해 줬다…. 아니, 이렇게 말하면 조금 다르군…. 소년은 그저 친구를 지키기 위해 힘을 썼고, 그 결과 나님을 구한 것이다.<end>"

## 460029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様は昔、帝都の鼻つまみ者だった。　<line-break>だが、俺様の親父だけは、俺が<line-break>偉大な人間になると言っていた。<line-break>俺様はそれが気に入らなかった。<line-break>人のよさそうな、貧弱そうな親父が<line-break>暴れ者の俺様をまともに説教できず…<end>"
EN: "I used to be a notorious troublemaker in the Imperial Capital. But my old man always said I would become a great man. I didn't like hearing that. He looked too gentle and weak to give a brute like me a proper lecture...<end>"
KO: "나는 예전에 제도에서 모두가 꺼리는 문제아였지. 하지만 아버지만은 내가 위대한 인간이 될 거라고 했다. 난 그게 마음에 들지 않았어. 사람 좋아 보이고 약해 보이는 아버지는 난폭한 나를 제대로 꾸짖지도 못하고….<end>"

## 460030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでも、俺様にまともな生活に<line-break>戻ってもらいたいと、心にもないことを<line-break>言っているのだ…そう思っていた。<line-break>そんな親父が死んだ。俺様は死に目に<line-break>居合わせなかった。が、親父は死の床で<line-break>そんな俺様を責めず、こう言った。<end>"
EN: "I thought he was saying things he didn't mean because he wanted me to return to a decent life. Then he died. I wasn't there at the end. But on his deathbed, rather than blame me, he said this:<end>"
KO: "그래도 이 몸에게 제대로 된 생활로 돌아오라고 마음에도 없는 소리를 하는 거라 생각했다…. 그런 아버지가 죽었다. 이 몸은 임종을 지키지 못했다. 하지만 아버지는 죽는 순간에도 이 몸을 탓하지 않고 이렇게 말했다.<end>"

## 460031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの子は未来の勇者として<line-break>人様のため、働いていて<line-break>ここにこられないのでしょう、とな。　<line-break>俺様は親父が本気で俺様を信じてたのを<line-break>そして、俺様自身がいかに臆病だったか<line-break>を知った。<end>"
EN: "“He must be too busy serving others as the Hero of the Future to come here.” That was when I realized my old man had truly believed in me―and how much of a coward I had been.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석은 미래의 용사로서 사람들을 위해 일하느라 여기 올 수 없는 거겠지, 라고 말이야. 나는 아버지가 진심으로 나를 믿고 있었다는 것과, 내가 얼마나 겁쟁이였는지를 깨달았다.<end>"

## 460032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様は自分が偉大な人間になれないと　<line-break>思っていたのだ。そうなろうとして<line-break>失敗するのを恐れていたのだ。<line-break>親父の信じる心が俺様の魂を熱き魂に<line-break>変えた。俺様は憶病さを捨てた。<line-break>俺様はそして未来の勇者となったのだ。<end>"
EN: "I believed I could never become a great man. I was afraid to try and fail. My old man's faith transformed my soul into a fiery one. I cast aside my cowardice and became the Hero of the Future!<end>"
KO: "난 내가 위대한 사람이 될 수 없다고 생각했어. 그렇게 되려다 실패하는 게 두려웠던 거지. 아버지의 믿음이 내 영혼을 뜨거운 영혼으로 바꿨다. 난 겁을 버렸고, 미래의 용자가 된 거야.<end>"

## 460033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、今のようにやることすべてが<line-break>空回りした。目標が見えなかった。<line-break>俺様も少しセンチになっていった…。　<line-break>そんなときに他人を守る力、<line-break>つまり、愛としての正しき力について<line-break>あの少年が教えてくれたのだ。<end>"
EN: "But everything I did went awry, just as it does now. I had no goal in sight, and I grew a little sentimental. That was when the boy taught me about righteous power used to protect others―the power of love.<end>"
KO: "하지만 지금처럼 하는 일마다 헛돌았어. 목표가 보이지 않았지. 이 몸도 조금 감상적이 되어 갔다…. 그럴 때 다른 사람을 지키는 힘, 즉 사랑으로서의 올바른 힘을 그 소년이 가르쳐 준 거다.<end>"

## 460034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、少年の友人を思う心が<line-break>俺様の熱き魂をさらに熱くたぎらせた。<line-break>そして、俺様は知った…。<line-break>熱き魂が正しき力を使うとき、<line-break>その魂が他人の魂をも、熱く燃やすと。<line-break>その少年や、俺様の親父のように…。<end>"
EN: "The boy's devotion to his friend made my fiery soul burn hotter still. And then I understood: when a fiery soul wields righteous power, it sets other souls ablaze as well. Just as that boy did―and my old man.<end>"
KO: "그리고 소년이 친구를 생각하는 마음이 이 몸의 뜨거운 영혼을 더욱 끓어오르게 했다. 그리고 이 몸은 알았다…. 뜨거운 영혼이 올바른 힘을 쓸 때 그 영혼은 타인의 영혼까지 뜨겁게 불태운다는 걸. 그 소년이나 이 몸의 아버지처럼….<end>"

## 460035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様…、<line-break>その少年のテラネの友人だな？<end>"
EN: "You... You're that boy's friend from Terane, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "네놈… 그 소년 테라네의 친구로군?<end>"

## 460036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ケガが治って…よかったな。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break><line-break>貴様は昔の俺様によく似ている…。<line-break>世の中の汚いものに反発しているが、<line-break>それが何か、どうすればいいかわからず<end>"
EN: "I'm glad your injuries have healed. You are much like the man I used to be. You rebel against all the filth in the world, but you do not know exactly what that filth is or what to do about it.<end>"
KO: "상처가 나아서… 다행이군. 너는 옛날의 나와 많이 닮았다…. 세상의 추악한 것에 반발하고 있지만 그게 무엇인지, 어떻게 해야 하는지 몰랐지.<end>"

## 460037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、世の中の何かを認めると<line-break>その気に入らない何かすら認めることに<line-break>なりそうで、すべてに反発している…。<end>"
EN: "You reject everything because accepting anything in this world would feel like accepting even the things you despise.<end>"
KO: "다만 세상의 무언가를 인정하면 마음에 들지 않는 것까지 인정하게 될 것 같아서 모든 것에 반발하고 있는 거야….<end>"

## 460038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、それはすべて憶病だからなのだ！<line-break>　<line-break><line-break>認めたいものは、全部認めるがいい！<line-break>貴様が認めたいと思った時点で<line-break>それは認めるに値するものだから！！<end>"
EN: "But that is all because you are afraid! Accept everything you wish to accept! The very fact that you wish to accept something makes it worthy of acceptance!<end>"
KO: "하지만 그건 전부 겁쟁이라서 그런 거다! 인정하고 싶은 건 전부 인정해라! 네가 인정하고 싶다고 생각한 시점에서 이미 인정할 가치가 있는 것이니까!!<end>"

## 460039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様は目標はまだ見えないが<line-break>今、立って歩いてゆける。<line-break>俺様の熱き魂に人を触れさせること…　<line-break>そうして、人にこの熱き魂を<line-break>わけていくことで、俺様は正しき力を<line-break>愛を、正義を、世界に押し広げるのだ。<end>"
EN: "My goal is still unclear, but now I can stand and walk forward. By letting others feel my fiery soul and sharing its fire with them, I will spread righteous power―love and justice―throughout the world!<end>"
KO: "나에게 아직 목표는 보이지 않지만 지금은 일어나 걸어갈 수 있다. 나의 뜨거운 영혼을 사람들에게 느끼게 하는 것…. 그렇게 이 뜨거운 영혼을 나눠 주며 나는 올바른 힘과 사랑과 정의를 세상에 넓혀 갈 것이다.<end>"

## 460040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様も、それができる。<line-break>まず、貴様はその少年を認めてやれ！　<line-break>かけがえのない、貴様の友達を…！<end>"
EN: "You can do the same. Start by recognizing that boy for what he is: your irreplaceable friend!<end>"
KO: "너도 그것을 할 수 있다. 우선 그 소년을 인정해 줘라! 무엇과도 바꿀 수 없는 네 친구를…!<end>"

## 460041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…けっ、ガルドランのくせに<line-break>人に説教しやがって…。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Girldlan, of all people, giving me a lecture...<end>"
KO: "…쳇, 갈드란 주제에 남한테 설교나 하고….<end>"

## 460042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…憶病にならなければ、<line-break>俺にも、できるのかな。　　　<line-break><line-break>こんな世界じゃない、<line-break>ナッジなんかが笑って暮らせる<line-break>新しい世界を作ること…。<end>"
EN: "If I stop being afraid... could I do it too? Create a new world―not one like this, but one where even someone like Nadge can live with a smile...<end>"
KO: "…겁먹지만 않는다면 나도 할 수 있을까. 이런 세상이 아니라, 나지 같은 녀석도 웃으며 살 수 있는 새로운 세상을 만드는 것…<end>"

## 460043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジ…！？<end>"
EN: "Nadge...?!<end>"
KO: "나지…!?<end>"

## 460044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、ヴァン！　何をしたの！！<line-break>僕、ヴァンのこと…心配して…なのに！<end>"
EN: "Van! What did you do?! After I was so worried about you...!<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 반! 무슨 짓을 한 거야!! 난 반이 걱정돼서… 그런데!<end>"

## 460045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ははっ、何マジ入ってんだよ。<line-break>俺がガルドランなんか相手にするかよ。<line-break><line-break>こいつは別口でおねんねしてるんだ。<line-break>…んなに騒ぐなよ、大丈夫だ。<line-break>自分で立って歩けるって言ってた。<end>"
EN: "Ha! Why are you getting so worked up? Like I'd bother fighting Girldlan. He's taking a little nap for an entirely different reason. Don't make such a fuss. He's fine―said he could stand up and walk on his own.<end>"
KO: "하하, 왜 그렇게 진지해져. 내가 갈드란 따위를 상대하겠냐. 이 녀석은 다른 일로 자고 있는 거야. …그렇게 호들갑 떨지 마. 괜찮아. 자기 발로 서서 걸을 수 있다고 했어.<end>"

## 460046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当？<end>"
EN: "Really?<end>"
KO: "정말?<end>"

## 460047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、奴の口から聞いた。<line-break>ところで、<value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Yeah. I heard it from his own mouth. Anyway, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "그래, 녀석 입으로 들었다. 그런데 <value:$28>….<end>"

## 460048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺も仲間に加えてくれ、<line-break>俺、お前らと一緒に旅がしたいんだ。<end>"
EN: "Let me join you. I want to travel with all of you.<end>"
KO: "나도 동료로 넣어 줘. 나, 너희와 함께 여행하고 싶어.<end>"

## 460049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>！<line-break>ヴァンには宿屋が…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! But Van has the inn...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>! 반에게는 여관이….<end>"

## 460050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいって、いいって。<line-break>じゃ、よろしくな、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "It's fine, it's fine. I'm counting on you, then, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아, 괜찮아. 그럼 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 460051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、本当にガルドランを<line-break>放って置いていいのかなあ。<end>"
EN: "Are you sure it's all right to leave Girldlan here?<end>"
KO: "있지, 정말 갈드란을 그냥 내버려 둬도 될까?<end>"

## 460052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいさ。あいつの熱き魂で<line-break>ナントカするんだろ。行こうぜ。<end>"
EN: "He'll manage somehow with that fiery soul of his. Come on, let's go.<end>"
KO: "됐어. 그 녀석의 뜨거운 혼으로 어떻게든 하겠지. 가자.<end>"

## 460053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、俺はこいつの面倒見るから。<line-break>あとで、一緒にな。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll look after this one. I'll catch up with you later.<end>"
KO: "그럼 난 이 녀석을 돌볼 테니까. 나중에 같이 가자.<end>"

## 460054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…親父。　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break><line-break>俺が帝都の鼻つまみだったころ、<line-break>ただひとり、俺に秘められた勇者の魂を<line-break>信じて疑わなかった親父…。<end>"
EN: "...Dad. Back when no one in the Imperial Capital could stand me, you were the one person who never doubted that the soul of a hero lay hidden inside me...<end>"
KO: "…아버지. 내가 제도의 골칫거리였을 때도 유일하게 내 안에 숨은 용자의 혼을 믿어 의심치 않았던 아버지…<end>"

## 460055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見てくれ。未来の勇者となった俺を…。<line-break>それに、俺の魂はこれからも高まる。<line-break>真の勇者になるのも時間の問題だ。<end>"
EN: "Look at me now―the hero of the future... And my soul will only keep soaring higher. It's just a matter of time before I become a true hero.<end>"
KO: "봐라. 미래의 용사가 된 나님을…. 게다가 나님의 영혼은 앞으로도 계속 높아질 것이다. 진정한 용사가 되는 것도 시간문제다.<end>"

## 460056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな俺を記念して<line-break>歌を作ったんだ、親父…。<end>"
EN: "So I wrote a song in my own honor, Dad...<end>"
KO: "그런 나를 기념해서 노래를 만든 거야, 아버지….<end>"

## 460057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっ、聞きたいって？<line-break>よせよ、照れるじゃねえか。　　<end>"
EN: "What, you want to hear it? Come on, you're embarrassing me.<end>"
KO: "응? 듣고 싶다고? 그만둬, 부끄럽잖아.<end>"

## 460058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…しょうがないな。聞かせてやるよ。<line-break>これも勇者の親孝行だ。<line-break>じゃ、いくぜ。<end>"
EN: "...Oh, all right. I'll sing it for you. A hero's got to do right by his dad. Here goes.<end>"
KO: "…어쩔 수 없군. 들려주지. 이것도 용사의 효도니까. 그럼 시작한다.<end>"

## 460059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪熱く燃える　世界一の魂～<line-break>♪煮えたぎる　悪党への怒り～<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "♪ The world's greatest soul, blazing hot～ ♪ Rage against villains, boiling over～<end>"
KO: "♪뜨겁게 타오르는 세계 제일의 영혼~ ♪끓어오르는 악당을 향한 분노~<end>"

## 460060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪この世の愛をおびやかす<line-break>♪よろこばしくない奴の影<line-break>♪俺がちぎっては投げするぜ～！<line-break>♪正義の熱い～　（けるなぐる～）<line-break>♪勇者の力～　　（槍で突く～）<line-break>♪悪を（悪を）悪をひねる光～！<end>"
EN: "♪ Threatening all the love in this world ♪ The shadow of a most unwelcome creep ♪ I'll send that creep flying～! ♪ Justice, burning hot～ (Kick and punch～) ♪ A hero's power～ (Thrust with a spear～) ♪ Evil! (Evil!) The light that twists evil～!<end>"
KO: "♪이 세상의 사랑을 위협하는 ♪달갑지 않은 녀석의 그림자 ♪내가 닥치는 대로 내던진다~! ♪정의의 뜨거운~ (차고 때리고~) ♪용사의 힘~ (창으로 찌르고~) ♪악을 (악을) 악을 비트는 빛~!<end>"

## 460061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "♪ガルドラン！　熱く正しい魂～<line-break>♪ガルドラン！　青く輝く正義～<line-break>♪未来の勇者　その名はガルドラン～！<end>"
EN: "♪ Girldlan! A burning soul, righteous and true～ ♪ Girldlan! Justice shining blue～ ♪ The hero of the future―his name is Girldlan～!<end>"
KO: "♪갈드란! 뜨겁고 올바른 영혼~ ♪갈드란! 푸르게 빛나는 정의~ ♪미래의 용사 그 이름은 갈드란~!<end>"

## 460062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだった、親父？<end>"
EN: "Well, Dad? What did you think?<end>"
KO: "어땠어, 아버지?<end>"

## 460063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやだなあ。<line-break>そんなにグッとくる曲だったかい。<line-break><line-break>ま、これも親孝行だからな。<end>"
EN: "Oh, come on. Was it really that moving? Well, it's all part of being a good son.<end>"
KO: "참, 그렇게 마음에 와닿는 곡이었나? 뭐, 이것도 효도니까.<end>"

## 460064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親孝行…。　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Being a good son...<end>"
KO: "효도….<end>"

## 460065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親父が生きてるうちに<line-break>やっときたかったぜ…。　　<end>"
EN: "I wanted to do right by you while you were still alive...<end>"
KO: "아버지가 살아 계실 때 해 두고 싶었는데….<end>"

## 460066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たくさん、たくさんなぁ…。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "A lot. A whole lot...<end>"
KO: "많이, 아주 많이….<end>"

## 460067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァン、僕、仇をとったんだ。<line-break>ガルドランを殺してきたよ。<end>"
EN: "Van, I got my revenge. I killed Girldlan.<end>"
KO: "반, 나 복수했어. 갈드란을 죽이고 왔어.<end>"

## 460068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぇ、ヴァン、僕、仇をとったんだ。<line-break>ガルドランを殺してきたんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Van, listen. I got my revenge. I killed Girldlan.<end>"
KO: "있지, 반. 나 복수했어. 갈드란을 죽이고 왔어.<end>"

## 460069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジ…俺、この前はじめて<line-break>親父について巡礼者を送ってきたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Nadge... The other day, I went with my father to escort some pilgrims. It was my first time.<end>"
KO: "나지… 나 지난번에 처음으로 아버지를 따라 순례자를 데려다주고 왔어.<end>"

## 460070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァン、どうしたの？<line-break>喜んでくれないの？<end>"
EN: "Van, what's wrong? Aren't you happy for me?<end>"
KO: "반, 왜 그래? 기쁘지 않아?<end>"

## 460071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…霊峰トールへの道は険しく、<line-break>途中、モンスターも多い。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>だけどモンスターよりも何よりも、<line-break>人間の敵は人間…なんだ。<end>"
EN: "The road to Sacred Mount Thor is treacherous, and there are plenty of monsters along the way. But worse than any monster, the enemy of man... is man.<end>"
KO: "…영봉 토르로 가는 길은 험하고 도중에 몬스터도 많아. 하지만 몬스터보다 더 무서운 인간의 적은 인간…이야.<end>"

## 460072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "霊峰トールへの巡礼は苦行だ。<line-break>巡礼者たちは、みんな、それぞれに、<line-break>いろいろな思いや悩みを抱えている。<line-break>自分の内面を見つめすぎた人たち<line-break>せっぱ詰まってここへ逃げてきた人たち<line-break>みんなそれぞれに救いを求めてた。<end>"
EN: "The pilgrimage to Sacred Mount Thor is an ordeal. Every pilgrim carries different thoughts and troubles. Some have looked too deeply into themselves. Some have fled here in desperation. Each of them came seeking salvation.<end>"
KO: "영봉 토르 순례는 고행이야. 순례자들은 저마다 여러 생각과 고민을 안고 있어. 자기 내면을 너무 들여다본 사람, 궁지에 몰려 이곳으로 도망쳐 온 사람…. 모두 각자의 구원을 찾고 있었지.<end>"

## 460073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな人たちがトールの難所を<line-break>越えて行くにつれ…落として行くんだ。<line-break>なんて言うか…心の垢みたいなのをさ。<line-break>俺の目にははじめ、あの巡礼客たちは<line-break>ただの暇人やうさん臭いじじい、<line-break>人生の負け犬にしか見えなかった。<end>"
EN: "But as they make their way through the difficult passes of Thor, they start shedding something. I don't know how to put it... the grime on their souls, maybe. At first, those pilgrims just looked like idlers, shady old men, and losers in life to me.<end>"
KO: "그 사람들이 토르의 험한 길을 넘으면서 뭔가를 떨어뜨리고 가더라고. 뭐랄까, 마음의 때 같은 걸. 처음 내 눈엔 순례자들이 그저 한가한 사람이나 수상한 노인, 인생의 패배자로만 보였어.<end>"

## 460074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それが、どこか神様じみてくるんだ。<line-break>顔からして輝き出すんだ。<line-break>俺も俺の…親父の仕事に誇りを持った。<line-break>だが、ある晩だ。護衛の冒険者が<line-break>巡礼の持つわずかな金を得るために<line-break>魂が輝きだした人たちを殺し始めた。<end>"
EN: "Then they begin to seem almost divine. Even their faces start to shine. I came to take pride in my... in my father's work. But then, one night, the adventurers serving as escorts began killing people whose souls had begun to shine, all for the little money the pilgrims carried.<end>"
KO: "그러다 어딘가 신처럼 보이기 시작해. 얼굴에서까지 빛이 나는 것 같았지. 나도 내… 아버지의 일에 긍지를 갖게 됐다. 그런데 어느 날 밤, 호위하던 모험자가 순례자의 얼마 안 되는 돈을 노리고 영혼이 빛나던 사람들을 죽이기 시작했어.<end>"

## 460075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は叫んだ。やめろ！<line-break>この人たちは救いを求めて来たんだ！<line-break>お前ら、命を何とも思わないのか！<line-break>冒険者たちは笑った。<line-break>俺たちはいつでも危険の中で生きてる。<line-break>いつでも命のやりとりをしてるんだ！<end>"
EN: "I shouted, “Stop! These people came here seeking salvation! Does life mean nothing to you?” The adventurers laughed. “We live in danger every day. Every day, we kill or get killed!”<end>"
KO: "나는 외쳤다. 그만둬! 이 사람들은 구원을 찾아온 거야! 너희는 목숨을 아무렇지도 않게 생각하냐! 모험가들은 웃었다. 우리는 언제나 위험 속에서 살아. 늘 목숨을 걸고 사는 거라고!<end>"

## 460076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みっともない話だが俺も親父も<line-break>逃げ去ることしかできなかった。　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I'm ashamed to admit it, but all my father and I could do was run.<end>"
KO: "꼴사나운 이야기지만 나도 아버지도 도망치는 것밖엔 할 수 없었어.<end>"

## 460077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦って力を得ていくうちに<line-break>人は命や他人の痛みに鈍感になる。<line-break>　<line-break>そうして、力を持った人間は<line-break>他人を傷つけなければ<line-break>生きていけないようになる。<end>"
EN: "As people fight and gain power, they grow callous about life and the pain of others. And once they have power, they reach a point where they can't live without hurting someone.<end>"
KO: "싸워 힘을 얻어 갈수록 사람은 생명과 타인의 고통에 둔감해져. 그렇게 힘을 가진 인간은 남을 상처 입히지 않고는 살 수 없게 돼.<end>"

## 460078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…親父がそう言ってたよ。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "That's what my father said.<end>"
KO: "…아버지가 그렇게 말했어.<end>"

## 460079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前、向こう側に行っちまったんだな。<line-break>ガルドランや、強盗たちの方へ…。　　<line-break>　<line-break>ナッジ、お前、何かの命を奪ったことを<line-break>喜々として話す人間じゃなかったのに…<line-break>俺が、俺が、バカだったばっかりに…。<end>"
EN: "You've gone over to the other side, haven't you? To Girldlan and the bandits... Nadge, you weren't the kind of person who'd talk so gleefully about taking a life. This is all because I... because I was such a fool...<end>"
KO: "너, 저쪽으로 가 버렸구나. 갈드란이나 강도들 쪽으로…. 나지, 넌 누군가의 목숨을 빼앗은 걸 기뻐하며 말하는 사람이 아니었는데… 내가, 내가 바보였던 탓에….<end>"

## 460080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまない。俺、お前と旅がしたかった。<line-break>でも、今、お前と行く気にはなれない。<line-break>　<line-break>俺は親父と一緒に<line-break>ここで巡礼客相手の宿屋をやる。<line-break>巡礼客をここで守る。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I wanted to travel with you. But now... I can't bring myself to go with you. I'm staying here to run an inn for pilgrims with my father. I'll protect them here.<end>"
KO: "미안하다. 난 너와 여행하고 싶었어. 하지만 지금은 너와 함께 갈 마음이 들지 않아. 난 아버지와 함께 여기서 순례객을 상대하는 여관을 할 거야. 순례객을 여기서 지키겠다.<end>"

## 460081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ナッジのこと、頼むぜ。<end>"
EN: "Take care of Nadge for me.<end>"
KO: "…나지 부탁한다.<end>"

## 460082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、僕、ヴァンと<line-break>違う世界の人間になっちゃったんだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, Van and I belong to different worlds now.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 나는 반과는 다른 세계의 사람이 되어 버렸어.<end>"

## 460083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕、ヴァンと違う世界の人間に<line-break>なっちゃんたんだね。<end>"
EN: "Van and I belong to different worlds now, don't we?<end>"
KO: "나, 반과는 다른 세계의 사람이 되어 버렸구나.<end>"

## 460084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よぉ、<value:$28>。<line-break>元気そうで何よりだ。<line-break>　<line-break>泊まっていくかい？<line-break>あんたらなら、タダでいいぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Glad to see you're doing well. Staying the night? You can all stay for free.<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>. 건강해 보여서 다행이다. 묵고 갈래? 너희라면 공짜로 해 줄게.<end>"

## 460085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "泊まる<end>"
EN: "Stay<end>"
KO: "묵는다<end>"

## 460086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "泊まらない<end>"
EN: "Don't stay<end>"
KO: "묵지 않는다<end>"

## 460087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい。<line-break>じゃ、ゆっくりしていってくれ。<end>"
EN: "All right. Then take it easy while you're here.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 그럼 푹 쉬다 가.<end>"

## 460088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "疲れはとれたかい？<end>"
EN: "Feeling rested?<end>"
KO: "피로는 좀 풀렸어?<end>"

## 460089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはよかった。　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Glad to hear it.<end>"
KO: "그건 다행이군.<end>"

## 460090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、俺にできることはこれくらいだ。<line-break>よかったら、また来てくれ。じゃな。<end>"
EN: "Well, that's about all I can do for you. Come back sometime, if you like. See you.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 내가 할 수 있는 건 이 정도다. 괜찮으면 또 와. 그럼.<end>"

## 460091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい。<line-break>じゃ、生きてたら、また顔出せよ。<end>"
EN: "All right. If you're still alive, come by again sometime.<end>"
KO: "그래. 그럼 살아 있으면 또 얼굴 비춰.<end>"

## 460092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生きてたら、また顔出せよ。<line-break>歓迎するぜ。<end>"
EN: "If you're still alive, come by again. You'll be welcome.<end>"
KO: "살아 있으면 또 들러. 환영할게.<end>"

## 460093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うちは巡礼客だけでいっぱいだ。<line-break>さあ、帰れ、この汚い冒険者め！<end>"
EN: "This place is full up with pilgrims as it is. Now get out, you filthy adventuring scum!<end>"
KO: "저희는 순례객만으로도 꽉 찼습니다. 자, 돌아가시오, 이 더러운 모험자야!<end>"

## 460094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親父、やめろ。<end>"
EN: "Dad, stop it.<end>"
KO: "아버지, 그만해.<end>"

## 460095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ヴァン。<end>"
EN: "...Van.<end>"
KO: "…반.<end>"

## 460096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親父は色眼鏡でものを見過ぎる。　<line-break>冒険者にもいろいろあるんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>頭っから、<line-break>そんな言い方をするものじゃない。<line-break>そうだろ、親父？<end>"
EN: "You're too quick to judge, Dad. Not all adventurers are alike. You shouldn't talk like that right from the start. Am I wrong?<end>"
KO: "아버지는 편견으로 보는 게 너무 심해. 모험자도 여러 종류가 있어. 처음부터 그렇게 말하면 안 되지. 그렇지, 아버지?<end>"

## 460097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァンが、そう言うなら…<line-break>私も、別に…。<end>"
EN: "If you say so, Van... I don't really...<end>"
KO: "반이 그렇게 말한다면… 나도 딱히….<end>"

## 460098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまなかったな。<line-break>イヤな気分にさせて。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、巡礼客でいっぱいなのも<line-break>事実なんだ。悪いが、泊められない。<line-break>また、来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "Sorry for making you feel unwelcome. But it is true that we're full up with pilgrims. I'm afraid we can't put you up. Come back another time.<end>"
KO: "미안했다. 기분 나쁘게 해서. 하지만 순례객으로 가득 찬 것도 사실이야. 미안하지만 묵게 해 줄 수 없어. 또 와.<end>"

## 460099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、冒険者なんて…！<end>"
EN: "Gah, adventurers...!<end>"
KO: "큭, 모험자 따위…!<end>"

## 460100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親父！<end>"
EN: "Dad!<end>"
KO: "아버지!<end>"

## 460101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァ、ヴァン…。<line-break>…すまん、さっきはひどいことを言った。<end>"
EN: "V-Van... I'm sorry. What I said before was awful.<end>"
KO: "바, 반…. …미안해, 아까는 심한 말을 했어.<end>"

## 460102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまないな。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "미안하군.<end>"

## 460103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、<value:$28>、<line-break>僕ら、ガルドランを捜してるんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>ガルドランはテラネから<line-break>出ていったばかりだよ。<line-break>別の町に行こうよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>, we're looking for Girldlan. He only just left Terane, so let's try another town.<end>"
KO: "있지, <value:$28>. 우리는 갈드란을 찾고 있어. 갈드란은 테라네를 막 떠났어. 다른 마을로 가자.<end>"

## 460104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ、<value:$28>。<line-break>まず、ギルドへ行ってみようよ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>ガルドランは冒険者だから<line-break>ギルドにいるかもしれないし、<line-break>何か情報が手に入るんじゃないかな？<end>"
EN: "Oh, right, <value:$28>. Let's try the Guild first. Girldlan's an adventurer, so he might be there, and we may be able to learn something.<end>"
KO: "그래, <value:$28>. 우선 길드에 가 보자. 갈드란은 모험자니까 길드에 있을지도 모르고 뭔가 정보를 얻을 수 있지 않을까?<end>"

## 460105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、ギルドは…<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あ、あの建物がそうだね。<line-break>行こう、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Now, where's the Guild...? Ah, that must be it. Let's go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 길드는… 아, 저 건물이구나. 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 460106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、<value:$28>、<line-break>ガルドランはここにはいなかったよ。<line-break>　<line-break>ねえ、でも、ついでだから<line-break>冒険者登録していこうよ。<line-break>今後のこともあるしさ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. Girldlan wasn't here. But since we're here anyway, let's register as adventurers. It'll come in handy later.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>. 갈드란은 여기 없었어. 그래도 온 김에 모험자 등록하고 가자. 앞으로 일도 있고 하니까.<end>"

## 460107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、冒険者登録をすましてから<line-break>行こうよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, let's finish your adventurer registration before we go.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 모험자 등록부터 마치고 가자.<end>"

## 460108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者登録かい。<line-break>ここに名前を記入してくれ。<end>"
EN: "Here to register as an adventurer? Write your name here.<end>"
KO: "모험자 등록인가. 여기에 이름을 적어 줘.<end>"

## 460109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "幻術計振れておらず<line-break>犯罪登録当てはまらず。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>おめでとう。<line-break>あんたも冒険者として<line-break>登録されたよ。<end>"
EN: "No reading on the illusion meter, and no match in the criminal registry. Congratulations. You're now registered as an adventurer.<end>"
KO: "환술계 수치도 없고 범죄 등록에도 해당하지 않는군. 축하해. 너도 모험자로 등록됐어.<end>"

## 460110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからは、バリバリ仕事して<line-break>名をあげていってくれよ。<line-break>がんばりな！<end>"
EN: "Now get out there, take on plenty of work, and make a name for yourself. Good luck!<end>"
KO: "이제부터 팍팍 일해서 이름을 날려 줘. 힘내!<end>"

## 460111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの、ガルドランっていう<line-break>冒険者を知りませんか？<end>"
EN: "Excuse me, do you know an adventurer named Girldlan?<end>"
KO: "저기, 갈드란이라는 모험자를 아세요?<end>"

## 460112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドランだって？<line-break>あのガルドランに用があるのかい？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Girldlan? You've got business with that Girldlan?<end>"
KO: "갈드란이라고? 그 갈드란에게 볼일이 있는 건가?<end>"

## 460113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知ってるんですか？<end>"
EN: "You know him?<end>"
KO: "알고 계신가요?<end>"

## 460114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "変な奴だからね。<line-break>有名人さ。<line-break>未来の勇者ガルドラン、だろ？　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "He's an odd one, so he's rather famous. Girldlan, the future Hero. That's the one, right?<end>"
KO: "이상한 녀석이잖아. 유명인이지. 미래의 용자 갈드란, 맞지?<end>"

## 460115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さっき、ここに現れてさ。<line-break>猫屋敷の話をしたら<line-break>俺様伝説が待っているとか言って出てったよ。<end>"
EN: "He showed up here a little while ago. When I told him about Cat Manor, he took off saying something about his legend waiting for him.<end>"
KO: "아까 여기 나타났어. 고양이 저택 이야기를 했더니 '나님의 전설이 기다린다'면서 나갔지.<end>"

## 460116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫屋敷ってのはな、謎の屋敷なんだ。<line-break>エンシャントの南東、賢者の森にあるといい、<line-break>そこには森の賢者がいるということだが…<line-break>今まで、誰も、そこにたどり着いた<line-break>冒険者はいないんだ。噂によると<line-break>ただ、運命に選ばれた者だけが入れるってさ。<end>"
EN: "Cat Manor is a mysterious mansion said to stand in Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient. They say the Sage of the Forest lives there, but no adventurer has ever managed to reach the place. According to rumor, only those chosen by fate can enter.<end>"
KO: "고양이 저택은 수수께끼의 저택이야. 엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲에 있다고 하고 그곳엔 숲의 현자가 있다지만… 지금까지 거기에 도착한 모험자는 아무도 없어. 소문으로는 오직 운명에 선택된 자만 들어갈 수 있다더군.<end>"

## 460117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、誰も見たことのないものがあるなんて<line-break>なんで、そんな噂が広まっているんだか…。<line-break>そっちの方が謎だよなあ。<end>"
EN: "But if no one's ever seen the place, how did a rumor like that get started? That's the real mystery.<end>"
KO: "하지만 아무도 본 적 없는 게 있다고 어떻게 그런 소문이 퍼진 건지…. 그쪽이 더 수수께끼네.<end>"

## 460118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ガルドランはその猫屋敷に向かうって<line-break>言ってたんですね。<end>"
EN: "So Girldlan said he was going to Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "갈드란이 그 고양이 저택으로 간다고 했던 거군요.<end>"

## 460119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。新たなる俺様伝説の幕開けだってさ。<line-break>奴は自分こそ、運命に選ばれた人間だと<line-break>思ってんだろうな。<end>"
EN: "Right. Said it would mark the dawn of a new legend starring himself. He must think he's the one chosen by fate.<end>"
KO: "그래. 새로운 나의 전설이 시작되는 거라나. 녀석은 자기가 운명에 선택받은 인간이라고 생각하고 있겠지.<end>"

## 460120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントの南東、賢者の森…。　<line-break>その奥にあるという猫屋敷。<line-break>　<line-break>今から行っても、間に合うかどうか<line-break>わからないけれど<line-break>行ってみようよ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Sage's Forest, southeast of Ancient... and Cat Manor, said to lie deep within it. I don't know if we can still make it in time, but let's go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "엔샨트 남동쪽, 현자의 숲…. 그 안쪽에 있다는 고양이 저택. 지금 가도 늦지 않을지 모르겠지만 가 보자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 460121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、町を出るときに合流しよう。<line-break>それじゃ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Then let's meet up when we leave town. See you there, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그럼 마을을 나갈 때 합류하자. 그럼, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 460122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、まずは冒険者登録をすませよう。<line-break>カウンターのおじさんに話しかけてよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, let's get you registered as an adventurer first. Go talk to the man at the counter.<end>"
KO: "저기, 우선 모험자 등록부터 끝내자. 카운터 아저씨한테 말 걸어봐.<end>"

## 460123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命！　これぞ運命！！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>エンシャント南東、賢者の森の奥、<line-break>猫屋敷…運命に選ばれた者のみが<line-break>入れるという。<end>"
EN: "Destiny! Now this is destiny!! Southeast of Ancient, deep within Sage's Forest, lies Cat Manor... They say only those chosen by destiny may enter.<end>"
KO: "운명! 이것이야말로 운명!! 엔샨트 남동쪽 현자의 숲 깊은 곳, 고양이 저택… 운명에 선택된 자만 들어갈 수 있다고 한다.<end>"

## 460124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命に選ばれた者といえば…俺様！<line-break>これはもう、行かざるをえまい！<line-break>ときめきながら待っていろ！　運命よ！<end>"
EN: "And who has been chosen by destiny...? Why, me! I have no choice but to go! Wait for me with your heart aflutter, O Destiny!<end>"
KO: "운명에 선택받은 자라면… 나님! 이건 이제 갈 수밖에 없군! 두근거리며 기다려라, 운명이여!<end>"

## 460125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この感覚…。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者…？<end>"
EN: "This presence... The Bearer of the Infinite Soul...?<end>"
KO: "이 감각…. 무한한 소울을 지닌 자…?<end>"

## 460126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃい。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>ようこそ猫屋敷へ。<line-break><line-break>私はここの主オルファウス。<line-break>立ち話もなんですから<line-break>中でゆっくりとお話ししませんか？<end>"
EN: "Welcome to Cat Manor. I am Orphaus, master of this house. Rather than stand out here talking, why don't we go inside and take our time?<end>"
KO: "어서 와요. 고양이 저택에 온 걸 환영합니다. 저는 이곳의 주인 올파우스. 서서 이야기하기도 그러니 안에서 천천히 이야기하지 않겠습니까?<end>"

## 460127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、ここには偶然来る、なんてことは<line-break>あり得ないんですよ。<line-break>　<line-break>私は運命なんて言葉は嫌いですが<line-break>たぶん、大事な巡り合わせです。<line-break>さ、どうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. No one ever comes here by chance. I dislike the word “fate,” but this is probably an important meeting. Please, come in.<end>"
KO: "후후, 여기는 우연히 올 수 있는 곳이 아니랍니다. 전 운명 같은 말은 싫어하지만 아마 중요한 인연이겠죠. 자, 들어오세요.<end>"

## 460128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの、オルファウス…さん、<line-break>ここにガルドランという冒険者は<line-break>来てませんか？<end>"
EN: "Um, excuse me, Orphaus... Has an adventurer named Girldlan been here?<end>"
KO: "저, 저기, 올파우스… 씨, 여기에 갈드란이라는 모험자가 오지 않았나요?<end>"

## 460129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ？　存じませんねえ。<line-break>人がここに来ること自体、<line-break>めったにありませんからね。<line-break>ですが、何かお話しできることは<line-break>あるかもしれません。<line-break>まあ、まずは中に入ってください。<end>"
EN: "Who can say? I don't know. People seldom come here at all. Still, there may be something I can tell you. For now, please come inside.<end>"
KO: "글쎄요? 모르겠네요. 사람이 여기 오는 것 자체가 드물거든요. 하지만 뭔가 이야기해 드릴 수 있는 건 있을지도 몰라요. 우선 안으로 들어오세요.<end>"

## 460130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ねえ、<value:$28>、<line-break>行ってみようか…。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>... Shall we go in?<end>"
KO: "…저기, <value:$28>, 가 볼까….<end>"

## 460131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なるほど、<line-break>だいたいのお話はわかりました。<line-break>　<line-break>友達の仇討ち…ですか。<end>"
EN: "I see. I believe I understand the gist of it. You want to avenge your friend... Is that it?<end>"
KO: "…그렇군요. 대강 사정은 알겠습니다. 친구의 복수…입니까.<end>"

## 460132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっしゃりたいことはわかります。<line-break>でも、…僕、くやしいんです。<line-break>　<line-break>大切な友達を助けられなかったこと。<line-break>僕に力が足りなかったこと…。<line-break>どうしようもなく…。<end>"
EN: "I understand what you're saying. But... I can't stand it. I couldn't save a dear friend. I wasn't strong enough... I just can't bear it.<end>"
KO: "무슨 말씀인지 압니다. 하지만… 저는 분해요. 소중한 친구를 구하지 못한 것. 제게 힘이 부족했던 것…. 정말 견딜 수 없을 만큼…<end>"

## 460133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど。<line-break>力が欲しいんですか…。<end>"
EN: "I see. So you want power...<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 힘이 필요한 겁니까….<end>"

## 460134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですが、力といっても<line-break>いろいろありますからねぇ。<line-break>　<line-break>こんな話があります。<line-break>闇の神器というものをご存じですか？<line-break>強大な力を秘めた１２の魔道器です。<end>"
EN: "But power comes in many forms. Let me tell you something. Have you heard of the Dark Artifacts? They are twelve magical artifacts containing immense power.<end>"
KO: "하지만 힘이라고 해도 여러 종류가 있으니까요. 이런 이야기가 있습니다. 어둠의 신기라는 걸 아십니까? 강대한 힘을 품은 열두 개의 마도기입니다.<end>"

## 460135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それらは闇の神器という名の通り<line-break>闇におちた破壊神、ウルグの復活に<line-break>用いられるといわれています。<end>"
EN: "As their name suggests, the Dark Artifacts are said to be used to resurrect Ulugh, the fallen God of Destruction.<end>"
KO: "그것들은 어둠의 신기라는 이름 그대로 어둠에 떨어진 파괴신 울그의 부활에 쓰인다고 합니다.<end>"

## 460136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近、何者かがウルグ復活のために、<line-break>古代の闇の怪物や魔人たちを<line-break>次々と復活させています。<end>"
EN: "Recently, someone has been resurrecting ancient creatures of darkness and demons, one after another, in an effort to revive Ulugh.<end>"
KO: "요즘 누군가가 울그 부활을 위해 고대의 어둠의 괴물과 마인들을 차례차례 부활시키고 있습니다.<end>"

## 460137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "先日も、闇の神器をまつっていた<line-break>ミイスの村が、魔人に焼き払われ、<line-break>神器と村人の命が奪われました。<end>"
EN: "Only recently, a demon burned Miis Village to the ground. The village had enshrined one of the Dark Artifacts, and both the artifact and the villagers' lives were taken.<end>"
KO: "얼마 전에도 어둠의 신기를 모시던 미이스 마을이 마인에게 불타 신기와 주민들의 목숨을 빼앗겼습니다.<end>"

## 460138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そんな時に、個人的な感情で<line-break>仇討ちなどやってる場合じゃない。<line-break>…そう言いたいんですか？<end>"
EN: "Are you saying this is no time to pursue a personal vendetta?<end>"
KO: "…이런 때 개인적인 감정으로 복수나 하고 있을 때가 아니다. …그렇게 말하고 싶은 건가요?<end>"

## 460139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、そうではありません。<line-break>ただ、ひとくちに力といっても<line-break>いろいろだということです。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Not at all. I'm merely saying that power comes in many forms.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그런 뜻은 아닙니다. 다만 힘이라고 해도 여러 종류가 있다는 뜻이지요.<end>"

## 460140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、その神器の話なんですが、<line-break>ロストールにあった闇の神器<line-break>禁断の聖杯も、盗まれてしまいました。<end>"
EN: "Now, about those artifacts. The Forbidden Grail, a Dark Artifact located in Rostorl, has also been stolen.<end>"
KO: "자, 그 신기에 대한 이야기인데요. 로스톨에 있던 어둠의 신기, 금단의 성배도 도둑맞고 말았습니다.<end>"

## 460141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "盗んだのは３匹のゴブリンです。<line-break>彼らは今、聖杯の力で知恵を得て<line-break>ゴブゴブ団と名乗って冒険しています。<end>"
EN: "The thieves were three Goblins. The Grail gave them intelligence, and now they call themselves the Gob-Gob Gang and go off on adventures.<end>"
KO: "훔친 건 고블린 세 마리입니다. 그들은 지금 성배의 힘으로 지혜를 얻어 고브고브단이라 자칭하며 모험하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 460142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけなら問題はないんですが…。<line-break>まずいことに、ある魔人が<line-break>聖杯をねらっているんですよ。<end>"
EN: "That alone wouldn't be a problem. Unfortunately, a certain demon is after the Grail.<end>"
KO: "그것뿐이라면 문제없지만…. 곤란하게도 어떤 마인이 성배를 노리고 있습니다.<end>"

## 460143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士<line-break>アーギルシャイアだ。<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire, one of Ulugh's Knights of the Round Table.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 아르길샤이어다.<end>"

## 460144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "猫がしゃべった！<end>"
EN: "The cat talked!<end>"
KO: "고양이가 말했다!<end>"

## 460145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は猫じゃねえ！<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士のひとりネモだ！　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I'm not a cat! I'm Nemo, one of the God of Destruction's Knights of the Round Table!<end>"
KO: "난 고양이가 아니야! 파괴신의 원탁기사 중 하나인 네모다!<end>"

## 460146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイアの奴、強い合成怪物を<line-break>作って、世界をおもしろ半分に<line-break>破壊しようと考えてやがる。<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire plans to create powerful hybrid monsters and tear the world apart just for the fun of it.<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어 녀석, 강한 합성괴물을 만들어 재미 삼아 세계를 파괴하려고 생각하고 있어.<end>"

## 460147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、彼女はそれに必要な知識を<line-break>禁断の聖杯から得ようと考え<line-break>ゴブリンたちを狙っているんです。<end>"
EN: "She is after the Goblins because she intends to obtain the knowledge she needs from the Forbidden Grail.<end>"
KO: "그리고 그녀는 필요한 지식을 금단의 성배에서 얻으려고 고블린들을 노리고 있습니다.<end>"

## 460150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、力っていろいろですねえ<line-break>ってことですよ。<line-break><line-break>ま、人の考え方によるんですけどね。<line-break>それって。<end>"
EN: "As I said, power comes in many forms. Though I suppose it all depends on how one looks at it.<end>"
KO: "그러니까 힘에도 여러 종류가 있다는 거죠. 뭐, 사람마다 생각하기 나름이지만요.<end>"

## 460151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "３匹のゴブリンどもは、聖杯の力で<line-break>言葉をしゃべれるようになってる。<line-break>今はエンシャント付近にいるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "The Grail gave those three Goblins the power of speech. They should be somewhere near Ancient now.<end>"
KO: "세 고블린은 성배의 힘으로 말을 할 수 있게 됐어. 지금은 엔샨트 근처에 있을 거야.<end>"

## 460152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。<end>"
EN: "Heh.<end>"
KO: "후훗.<end>"

## 460153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実は彼らに聖杯を盗ませたのは　<line-break>このバカネコなんです。<end>"
EN: "The truth is, it was this idiot cat who had them steal the Grail.<end>"
KO: "사실 그들에게 성배를 훔치게 한 건 이 바보 고양이입니다.<end>"

## 460154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯の力で元の姿に戻るはずが…<line-break>知恵を付けた彼らに見放されて、<line-break>今もこの格好なんですよ。<end>"
EN: "The Grail was supposed to restore his original form, but once the Goblins became intelligent, they abandoned him. He's been stuck like this ever since.<end>"
KO: "성배의 힘으로 원래 모습으로 돌아갈 줄 알았는데… 지혜를 얻은 그들에게 버림받아서 아직도 이 모습이랍니다.<end>"

## 460155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うるさい！　それより、このままだと<line-break>またアーギルシャイアの奴が<line-break>ミイスの時みたいなことをやりかねん。<end>"
EN: "Shut up! More importantly, if this goes on, Argyleshire may do the same thing she did at Miis.<end>"
KO: "시끄러워! 그것보다 이대로라면 아르길샤이어 녀석이 또 미이스 때 같은 짓을 할지도 몰라.<end>"

## 460156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうなんです。だからその前に、<line-break>なんらかの方法で禁断の聖杯を<line-break>守らなければなりません。<end>"
EN: "Precisely. We must find some way to protect the Forbidden Grail before that happens.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니다. 그러니 그 전에 어떤 방법으로든 금단의 성배를 지켜야 합니다.<end>"

## 460157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうでしょう？<line-break>彼らに会ってみませんか？<line-break>　<line-break>彼らも聖杯の持つ力に固執しています。<line-break>彼らと会えば、力について考える<line-break>いい参考になるのではないでしょうか？<end>"
EN: "What do you say? Would you like to meet them? They, too, are fixated on the power of the Grail. Meeting them may give you a useful perspective on power.<end>"
KO: "어떻습니까? 그들을 만나 보시겠습니까? 그들도 성배가 지닌 힘에 집착하고 있습니다. 만나 보면 힘에 대해 생각하는 데 좋은 참고가 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 460158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もし、聖杯を持ってきてくれたら<line-break>私が責任を持ってお預りします。<line-break>あ、これは別に強制はしません。<end>"
EN: "If you bring me the Grail, I will take responsibility for its safekeeping. Ah, but I'm not trying to force you.<end>"
KO: "만약 성배를 가져와 주시면 제가 책임지고 보관하겠습니다. 아, 강제하는 건 아닙니다.<end>"

## 460159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。そうですか。<line-break>では、見せたいモノがあります。<line-break>来てください。<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Very well. There is something I'd like to show you. Come with me, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후훗. 그렇군요. 그럼 보여 드리고 싶은 게 있어요. 와 주세요, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 460160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、行ってみるといいよ。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>僕はここで待ってるから。<end>"
EN: "Hey, why don't you go, <value:$28>? I'll wait here.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 가 보는 게 좋아. <value:$28>. 난 여기서 기다릴게.<end>"

## 460161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、ここまでちょっと一方的<line-break>でしたので、私からも微力ながら<line-break>お手伝いをさせてもらいます。<end>"
EN: "Things have been a little one-sided so far, so allow me to offer what little help I can.<end>"
KO: "자, 지금까지는 조금 일방적이었으니 저도 미력하나마 도와드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 460162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この子は転送機といいまして、<line-break>世界中のたいていの所から<line-break>仲間を呼び出せるんです。<end>"
EN: "This little device is called a teleportation machine. It can summon companions from almost anywhere in the world.<end>"
KO: "이 아이는 전송기라고 해서, 세계 대부분의 곳에서 동료를 불러올 수 있습니다.<end>"

## 460163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…あ、<value:$28>。<line-break>これって、いったい…？<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. What in the world is this...?<end>"
KO: "…아, <value:$28>. 이게 대체…?<end>"

## 460164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "逆に、別れたい仲間を、望む所に<line-break>送り出してやることもできます。<end>"
EN: "It works the other way, too: it can send companions you want to part ways with wherever they wish to go.<end>"
KO: "반대로 헤어지고 싶은 동료를 원하는 곳으로 보내 줄 수도 있습니다.<end>"

## 460165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たぶん、この子は役に立ちますよ。　　<end>"
EN: "This little device will probably come in handy.<end>"
KO: "아마 이 아이는 도움이 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 460166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが将来、旅の途中で人と出会い、<line-break>仲間を増やしたときなんかにね。<end>"
EN: "Once you start meeting people on your travels and gaining more companions, for instance.<end>"
KO: "나중에 여행 중 사람들을 만나 동료가 늘었을 때 같은 경우에 말이죠.<end>"

## 460167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ど、どうなってるの？<line-break>ねえ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "W-what's going on? <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "어, 어떻게 된 거야? 저기, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 460168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ、<line-break>驚かせてしまってすみません。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>今後は呼び出す前に<line-break>相手の方に都合を確認します。<line-break>もう、こんなことはありませんから。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. I'm sorry for startling you. From now on, I'll make sure it's convenient before summoning anyone. This won't happen again.<end>"
KO: "후후, 놀라게 해서 죄송합니다. 앞으로는 불러내기 전에 상대방의 사정을 확인하겠습니다. 이제 이런 일은 없을 겁니다.<end>"

## 460169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に旅する仲間を<line-break>変更したくなったら<line-break>遠慮なくここに来てください。<end>"
EN: "If you ever want to change which companions travel with you, please feel free to come here.<end>"
KO: "함께 여행하는 동료를 바꾸고 싶어지면 언제든 여기로 와 주세요.<end>"

## 460170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、何か質問があれば、<line-break>受け付けますよ。<end>"
EN: "Now then, if you have any questions, I'll be happy to answer them.<end>"
KO: "자, 질문이 있으면 받겠습니다.<end>"

## 460171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルとは<end>"
EN: "What is the Infinite Soul?<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울이란<end>"

## 460172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "転送機について<end>"
EN: "About the teleportation device<end>"
KO: "전송기에 대해<end>"

## 460173

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器について<end>"
EN: "About the Dark Artifacts<end>"

JP: "ネモについて<end>"
