KO: "네모에 대해서<end>"

## 460175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイアについて<end>"
EN: "About Argyleshire<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어에 대해서<end>"

## 460176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "質問はない<end>"
EN: "No questions<end>"
KO: "질문은 없다<end>"

## 460177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、それは秘密です。　　<line-break>私の口から教える事ではないですしね。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、心配しないでください。<line-break>あなたが冒険を続けていけば<line-break>きっと答えは見つけられるはずです。<end>"
EN: "Heh, that's a secret. It isn't for me to tell you. But don't worry. If you continue your journey, I'm sure you'll find the answer in time.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그건 비밀입니다. 제가 입으로 알려 드릴 일은 아니니까요. 하지만 걱정하지 마세요. 계속 모험하다 보면 분명 답을 찾을 수 있을 겁니다.<end>"

## 460178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まずは、ギルドで仕事をしてください。<line-break>そうして自分を高めていけば、いつか<line-break>それが何なのかわかりますよ。<end>"
EN: "For now, take on work at the Guild. Keep improving yourself, and one day you'll understand what it is.<end>"
KO: "우선 길드에서 일을 해 보세요. 그렇게 자신을 갈고닦다 보면 언젠가 그것이 무엇인지 알게 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 460179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将来、一緒に旅する仲間が増えた時、<line-break>その人たちを呼び出すための装置です。<line-break>また逆に、送り出す事もできます。<end>"
EN: "This device will let you summon any companions who join you on your travels. It can also send them away.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 함께 여행할 동료가 늘었을 때 그들을 불러내는 장치입니다. 반대로 내보내는 것도 가능합니다.<end>"

## 460180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に冒険をする仲間を変えたく<line-break>なったら、ここへ来てください。<end>"
EN: "If you ever want to change which companions travel with you, come back here.<end>"
KO: "함께 모험하는 동료를 바꾸고 싶어지면 여기로 오세요.<end>"

## 460181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの復活に必要とされる<line-break>魔道器です。全部で１２個あり、その<line-break>形は、槍や剣、水晶球とさまざまです。<end>"
EN: "They're arcane artifacts said to be necessary for the resurrection of Ulugh, the God of Destruction. There are twelve in all, taking various forms―spears, swords, crystal balls, and so on.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 부활에 필요하다고 전해지는 마도기입니다. 모두 열두 개이며 형태는 창, 검, 수정구 등 다양합니다.<end>"

## 460182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "基本的にウルグの円卓騎士が管理して<line-break>いますが、多くは大陸中に散らばり、<line-break>所在が不明になっているようです。<end>"
EN: "They're normally kept by Ulugh's Knights of the Round Table, but many have been scattered across the continent, and their whereabouts now appear to be unknown.<end>"
KO: "기본적으로 울그의 원탁기사가 관리하지만, 대부분 대륙 곳곳에 흩어져 소재가 불명확해진 모양입니다.<end>"

## 460183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにいるブサイクな猫ですね。<line-break>破壊神につかえる１２人の魔人、<line-break>ウルグの円卓騎士のひとりです。<end>"
EN: "The ugly cat right here. Nemo is one of the twelve demons who serve the God of Destruction, and one of Ulugh's Knights of the Round Table.<end>"
KO: "여기 있는 못생긴 고양이입니다. 파괴신을 섬기는 열두 마인, 울그의 원탁기사 중 하나입니다.<end>"

## 460184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かつて勇者ネメアに敗れて<line-break>猫の姿に封印されてしまってからは、<line-break>言葉を話すくらいしかできません。<end>"
EN: "Ever since the Hero Nemea defeated Nemo and sealed him in the form of a cat, he's been able to do little more than speak.<end>"
KO: "예전에 용사 네메아에게 패해 고양이 모습으로 봉인된 뒤로는 말을 하는 정도밖에는 할 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 460185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "円卓騎士には他に<line-break>アーギルシャイアなどがいますね。　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The other Knights of the Round Table include Argyleshire.<end>"
KO: "원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 아르길샤이어 등이 있습니다.<end>"

## 460186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウルグの円卓騎士のひとりで、ネモが<line-break>言うには残忍で妖艶な魔女だそうです。<line-break>　<line-break>究極の魔物を創り出すための知恵を<line-break>求めて、聖杯を探しています。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "She's one of Ulugh's Knights of the Round Table. According to Nemo, she's a cruel and seductive witch. She's searching for the Grail, hoping to gain the knowledge needed to create the ultimate monster.<end>"
KO: "울그의 원탁기사 중 한 명으로, 네모 말로는 잔혹하고 요염한 마녀라고 합니다. 궁극의 마물을 만들어 낼 지혜를 찾아 성배를 찾고 있습니다.<end>"

## 460187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、別の神器を手に入れるため、<line-break>辺境の村、ミイスを焼き尽くしました。<line-break>放っておけば他の地も危ないでしょう。<line-break>彼女の野望を阻止するため、是非とも<line-break>禁断の聖杯を手に入れてきてください。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "She also burned the remote village of Miis to the ground to obtain another sacred artifact. If left unchecked, other lands will be in danger as well. Please, you must find the Forbidden Grail and put an end to her ambitions.<end>"
KO: "또 다른 신기를 얻기 위해 변방 마을 미이스를 불태웠습니다. 내버려 두면 다른 곳도 위험하겠지요. 그녀의 야망을 막기 위해 꼭 금단의 성배를 손에 넣어 주세요.<end>"

## 460188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<end>"
EN: "I see.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까.<end>"

## 460189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そんな感じですかね。<line-break>わかっていただけましたか？<end>"
EN: "Well, that's about it. Does that all make sense?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 대충 그런 느낌이네요. 이해하셨습니까?<end>"

## 460190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聞きたいことがあれば答えますよ。<end>"
EN: "If you have any questions, I'll be happy to answer them.<end>"
KO: "궁금한 게 있으면 대답해 드리죠.<end>"

## 460191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "各地を歩き、多くの人に出会い、<line-break>世界のさまざまな姿に触れてください。<line-break>それが、冒険の旅というものです。<end>"
EN: "Travel from place to place, meet many people, and experience the world in all its variety. That's what an adventure is.<end>"
KO: "각지를 걷고 많은 사람을 만나며 세계의 다양한 모습을 접해 보세요. 그것이 모험 여행이라는 겁니다.<end>"

## 460192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その中でみなさんが、力について<line-break>何かを感じ取ってくれると<line-break>うれしいんですけどね。<end>"
EN: "Along the way, I hope you'll sense something about power.<end>"
KO: "그 과정에서 여러분이 힘에 대해 무언가를 느껴 준다면 기쁠 텐데요.<end>"

## 460193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "竜王が目覚め、闇の門が開き、<line-break>そして無限のソウルを持つ者が<line-break>ここに現れる…。<end>"
EN: "The Dragon King awakens, the Dark Gate opens, and the Bearer of the Infinite Soul appears here...<end>"
KO: "용왕이 깨어나고, 어둠의 문이 열리고, 그리고 무한의 소울을 지닌 자가 여기에 나타난다….<end>"

## 460194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また世界が震え始めますねえ…。<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The world will begin to tremble once more...<end>"
KO: "또 세계가 흔들리기 시작하겠군요….<end>"

## 460195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…結局、オルファウスさんは<line-break>僕たちに何を言いたかったんだろう？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "What was Orphaus trying to tell us in the end?<end>"
KO: "…결국 오르파우스 씨는 우리에게 무슨 말을 하고 싶었던 걸까?<end>"

## 460196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は禁断の聖杯を追うことで<line-break>オルファウスさんの言いたいことが<line-break>理解できるんだろうか…？<end>"
EN: "Will I understand what Orphaus meant if I keep pursuing the Forbidden Grail...?<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배를 쫓다 보면 오르파우스 씨가 말하려던 걸 이해할 수 있을까….<end>"

## 460197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ごめん。<line-break>また考えすぎのくせが出ちゃった。<line-break>行こう、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Sorry. I'm overthinking things again. Let's go, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "미안. 또 너무 깊이 생각하는 버릇이 나왔네. 가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 460198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "た～すけて～。　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Heeelp meee!<end>"
KO: "도~와줘~.<end>"

## 460199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやもう、ほ～んとうに<line-break>助けて欲しいんじゃね～、わし。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I really do need you to save me, I do.<end>"
KO: "아니, 정말로 좀 도와줬으면 하는 게야, 나 말이지.<end>"

## 460200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやもう、ほ～んとうに<line-break>助かったんじゃね～、わし。　　　　　<line-break><line-break>もう、な～んにち、逃げてたか<line-break>わからんもんね、わし。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you really did save me, you did. I don't even know how many days I've been on the run.<end>"
KO: "아니 정말, 나 살았네~. 며칠이나 도망다녔는지도 모르겠다니까.<end>"

## 460201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別に、あ～たま、ボケきっちゃったから<line-break>わからんわけじゃ～ないんじゃけどね。<line-break>ま、言葉のあ～やなんじゃけどね。<line-break>と～ころで、助けてもらったついでに<line-break>あ～んたのな～まえを<line-break>お～しえて欲～しいんじゃけどね。<end>"
EN: "It's not as if I've gone completely senile and that's why I don't know, mind you. It's just a figure of speech. Anyway, since you've already saved me, perhaps you'd tell me your name?<end>"
KO: "딱히 머리가 완전히 돌아버려서 모르는 건 아니고 말이지. 그냥 말의 표현이 그런 거야. 그나저나 구해 준 김에 자네 이름 좀 알려 주면 좋겠는데.<end>"

## 460202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ～か、な～んとか君て言うんじゃね。<line-break>次来るまでには覚えとくわね、わし。<line-break><line-break>ところで、あんた、強いみたいじゃね？<line-break>助けてもらったお礼に、この指輪、<line-break>も～らっちゃって欲しいんじゃけどね。<end>"
EN: "Ah, so it's What's-your-name, is it? I'll remember by the next time you come. By the way, you seem rather strong. As thanks for saving me, I'd like you to take this ring.<end>"
KO: "그~래, 뭐시기라고 했지. 다음에 올 때까지는 내가 기억해둘게. 그런데 자네 꽤 강한가 보군? 도와준 보답으로 이 반지, 받아줬으면 해서 말이야.<end>"

## 460203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もらう<end>"
EN: "Accept it<end>"
KO: "받는다<end>"

## 460204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "指輪について聞き返す<end>"
EN: "Ask about the ring<end>"
KO: "반지에 대해 되묻는다<end>"

## 460205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "断る<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "거절한다<end>"

## 460206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>ディードリングを手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> obtained the Deed Ring.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 디드 링을 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 460207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すぐはめて？　すぐはめて？　　<end>"
EN: "Put it on right away, will you? Right away?<end>"
KO: "바로 끼워 줘? 바로 끼워 줘?<end>"

## 460208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやん。薬指には～めるな～んて<line-break>わ～し、照～れちゃうんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, my. Putting it on your ring finger? Now you're making me blush.<end>"
KO: "어머나. 약지에 끼워 준다니, 나 부끄럽잖아.<end>"

## 460209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だめじゃね。他人に答えを聞くようでは<line-break>冒険者として恥～ずかしいんじゃね。<line-break><line-break>自分の手で真実をつかみ取る者、<line-break>そ～れが冒険者なんじゃね。<line-break>指輪をはめて、答えを得るんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "No, no. An adventurer ought to be ashamed to ask someone else for the answer. True adventurers seize the truth with their own hands. Put on the ring and find the answer for yourself.<end>"
KO: "안 되겠네. 남에게 답을 물어보는 건 모험가로서 부끄러운 일이네. 자기 손으로 진실을 붙잡는 자, 그게 바로 모험가인 게야. 반지를 끼고 답을 얻어 보게.<end>"

## 460210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はめた？　はめちゃった？<line-break>はめちゃったんじゃね？　ムフフ…。<end>"

KO: "꼈어? 끼워 버렸어? 진짜 끼운 거 아냐? 무후후….<end>"

## 460211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 460212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 460213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "이야~ 약지에 끼우다니, 나도 참 장난꾸러기라니까.<end>"

## 460214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 460215

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 460217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な～んと、説明も聞かずに<line-break>わかっちゃったんじゃね～！　　　　　<line-break><line-break>やっぱし、偉人と偉人は、何も言わずに<line-break>わかり合えちゃうんじゃね～。<line-break>わしらは同類なんじゃね～。ぽっ。<end>"
EN: "Well, well! You understood without even hearing the explanation! Great minds really can understand each other without saying a word. We're kindred spirits! Blush.<end>"
KO: "무려 설명도 안 들었는데 알아버린 거지~! 역시 위인끼리는 아무 말 없이도 통하는 거야~. 우리는 같은 부류인 거지~. 부끄.<end>"

## 460224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ディードリング、カッティング完了。<line-break>これであんたのディードは強化された。<line-break><line-break>こ～ゆ～芸当は、天才のわ～しじゃから<line-break>で～きるんじゃね～。<line-break>よい子はマネしちゃいか～んのじゃね。<end>"
EN: "...Deed Ring cutting complete. Your Deed has now been enhanced. Only a geeeenius like me could pull off a trick like this! Good children mustn't try it themselves.<end>"
KO: "…디드 링 커팅 완료. 이걸로 네 디드는 강화됐다. 이런 재주는 천재인 나니까 가능한 거지. 착한 아이는 따라 하면 안 된다네.<end>"

## 460225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml

KO: "자, 그럼 이번 강화에 대해 설~명해 볼까~.<end>"

## 460227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml

## 460228

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 460233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 460234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 460235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 460236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 460237

## 460238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%20アミラルのドラゴン祭りは<line-break>毎年、１２月の３１日なんじゃね。<end>ロストールのアイリーンのお母さんの<line-break>誕生日は４月の１５日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>神の手のロッシマの<line-break>誕生日は５月の７日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ツェラシェルの妹ヴァイライラの<line-break>誕生日は１月の１４日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ツェラシェルの妹ヴィアリアリの<line-break>誕生日は１月の１４日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>テラネの宿屋のおじさんの<line-break>誕生日は５月の１１日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>テラネの宿屋のおばさんの<line-break>誕生日は７月の１９日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ロストールの剣術指南オッシの<line-break>誕生日は８月の１２日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ウルカーンの長老シェムハザの<line-break>誕生日は１１月の４日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>フゴーの護衛兼鑑定人ダイダロの<line-break>誕生日は１０月の１０日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>フゴーの奥さん兼影武者のフゴー夫人の<line-break>誕生日は２月の１７日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>エルズのテレジアの<line-break>誕生日は１０月の１９日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ファーロス家当主、大公ノヴィンの<line-break>誕生日は５月の７日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ロストールの酒場のフェルムちゃんの<line-break>誕生日は５月の１１日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>わしの誕生日、１月１日じゃからね。<line-break>わしとフェルムちゃんの<line-break>相性はどうか知りたいんじゃね。<end>エルズの竜の座の女官ベモラベラモの<line-break>誕生日は１２月の１７日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>アミラルのロベルトの奥さんポーリアの<line-break>誕生日は７月の６日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ノーブルの悪代官ボルボラ兄弟の<line-break>誕生日は１２月の８日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>テラネの木こりボンガの<line-break>誕生日は１２月の１７日なんじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>テラネの木こりボンガの奥さんの<line-break>誕生日は５月の５日なんじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>アミラルの宿屋の娘ルフレンちゃんの<line-break>誕生日は２月の２４日なんじゃね。<line-break><line-break>わしの誕生日は１月１日じゃからね。<line-break>わしとルフレンちゃんの<line-break>相性はどうか知りたいんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%20Amiral's Dragon Festival is held every year on December 31st.<end>Irene's mother in Rostorl was born on April 15th. Sorry if you've never heard of her. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Rossima of the Hand of God was born on May 7th. Sorry if you've never heard of Rossima. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Zeraschel's younger sister Vailaila was born on January 14th. Sorry if you've never heard of her. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Zeraschel's younger sister Vialiali was born on January 14th. Sorry if you've never heard of her. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>The man at the inn in Terane was born on May 11th. Sorry if you've never heard of him. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>The lady at the inn in Terane was born on July 19th. Sorry if you've never heard of her. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Ossi, the swordsmanship instructor in Rostorl, was born on August 12th. Sorry if you've never heard of Ossi. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Shemhaza, the elder of Ulkarne, was born on November 4th. Sorry if you've never heard of him. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Daedalo, Hugo's escort and appraiser, was born on October 10th. Sorry if you've never heard of Daedalo. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Mrs. Hugo, Hugo's wife and double, was born on February 17th. Sorry if you've never heard of her. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Theresia of Elz was born on October 19th. Sorry if you've never heard of Theresia. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Grand Duke Novin, head of the Pharos family, was born on May 7th. Sorry if you've never heard of him. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Ferme from the tavern in Rostorl was born on May 11th. My birthday is January 1st, you see. I'd like to know how compatible Ferme and I are.<end>Bemoraberamo, a court lady of the Dragon's Seat in Elz, was born on December 17th. Sorry if you've never heard of her. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Polia, the wife of Roberto of Amiral, was born on July 6th. Sorry if you've never heard of her. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>The Bolbora brothers, Noble's corrupt magistrates, were born on December 8th. Sorry if you've never heard of them. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Bonga, the woodcutter in Terane, was born on December 17th. Sorry if you've never heard of him. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>The wife of Bonga, the woodcutter in Terane, was born on May 5th. Sorry if you've never heard of her. Not that knowing this will do you any good.<end>Lufren, the girl at the inn in Amiral, was born on February 24th. My birthday is January 1st, you see. I'd like to know how compatible Lufren and I are.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%20아미랄의 드래곤 축제는 매년 12월 31일이래.<end>로스톨 아이린 어머니의 생일은 4월 15일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>신의 손 롯시마의 생일은 5월 7일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>체라셸의 여동생 바일라이라의 생일은 1월 14일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>체라셸의 여동생 비아리아리의 생일은 1월 14일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>테라네 여관 아저씨의 생일은 5월 11일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>테라네 여관 아주머니의 생일은 7월 19일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>로스톨의 검술 사범 오시의 생일은 8월 12일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>울칸의 장로 셈하자 생일은 11월 4일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>휴고의 호위 겸 감정인 다이다로의 생일은 10월 10일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>휴고의 아내 겸 대역인 휴고 부인의 생일은 2월 17일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>엘즈의 테레지아 생일은 10월 19일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>파로스 가문 당주 대공 노빈의 생일은 5월 7일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>로스톨 술집의 페름 양 생일은 5월 11일이래. 내 생일은 1월 1일이니 페름 양과 궁합이 어떤지 궁금하네.<end>엘즈 용의 자리의 시녀 베모라베라모 생일은 12월 17일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>아미랄의 로베르토 아내 폴리아의 생일은 7월 6일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>노블의 악덕 관리 볼보라 형제 생일은 12월 8일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>테라네의 나무꾼 봉가 생일은 12월 17일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>테라네 나무꾼 봉가의 아내 생일은 5월 5일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>아미랄 여관집 딸 루프렌 양 생일은 2월 24일이래. 내 생일은 1월 1일이니 루프렌 양과 궁합이 어떤지 궁금하네.<end>"

## 460239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま～、今、ディードの研究を<line-break>鋭意、進めとるからね～。<line-break><line-break>ま～た、日をあらためて来～てくれたら<line-break>新し～い研究の成果を実験させて<line-break>もらうから、ま～た来るんじゃね～。<end>"
EN: "Well, I'm hard at work researching Deed right now. Come back another day, and I'll get to test the latest results of my research. So be sure to come back.<end>"
KO: "뭐~ 지금 디드 연구를 열심히 진행 중이라서 말이지~. 또 날짜를 바꿔서 와 주면 새로운 연구 성과를 실험하게 해 줄 테니, 또 오라고~.<end>"

## 460240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖杯を持つゴブリン…。<line-break>そんなのに会うことで、僕は<line-break>何か得ることができるのかなあ…？<end>"
EN: "A Goblin with the Forbidden Grail... I wonder if meeting such a creature would do me any good...<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배를 가진 고블린…. 그런 녀석을 만나서 나는 뭔가 얻을 수 있을까…?<end>"

## 460241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これがエンシャントの門かあ…。<line-break>さすがにテラネとは、けた違いだね。<line-break>　<line-break>こんなに近くだったのに、<line-break>僕たち、ここまで来るの<line-break>初めてなんだよね。<end>"
EN: "So this is the gate to Ancient... It's on a completely different scale from Terane. Even though it was so close, we've never come this far before, have we?<end>"
KO: "이게 엔샨트의 문인가…. 역시 테라네와는 차원이 다르네. 이렇게 가까웠는데도 우리 여기까지 온 건 처음이지.<end>"

## 460242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、中に入ろうか。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Well, shall we go inside, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그럼 안으로 들어갈까, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 460243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec046-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちゃんと話を理解したか？<line-break>わかんねえことがあったら<line-break>そこのオトコオンナによく聞いとけよ。<end>"
EN: "Did you follow all that? If there's anything you don't understand, make sure you ask that mannish woman over there.<end>"
KO: "이야기 제대로 이해했어? 모르는 게 있으면 저기 남자인지 여자인지 모를 녀석한테 잘 물어봐.<end>"

## 470001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、あ～んた、な～んとか君じゃね。<end>"
EN: "Well, if it isn't... er, What's-your-name.<end>"
KO: "어이쿠, 자네 그 뭐시기 군 아닌가.<end>"

## 470002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わし、あんたを、よ～く覚えとるのね。<line-break>ほ～とんど忘れとるんじゃけどね。<end>"
EN: "I remember you very well, you know. Mind you, I've forgotten most of it.<end>"
KO: "난 당신을 아주 잘 기억하고 있지. 거의 다 잊었지만 말이야.<end>"

## 470003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "と～にかく、世界を回って見聞を広め、<line-break>いろんな人と出会ってほしいんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, I want you to travel the world, broaden your horizons, and meet all sorts of people.<end>"
KO: "아무튼 세계를 돌며 견문을 넓히고 여러 사람을 만나 봤으면 좋겠구먼.<end>"

## 470004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、こ～こには、<line-break>ディードの説明を聞きに来たんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "You've come here for an explanation of Deed, haven't you?<end>"
KO: "당신, 여기에 디드 설명을 들으러 온 거지?<end>"

## 470005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その通り<end>"
EN: "That's right.<end>"
KO: "맞아<end>"

## 470006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや違う<end>"
EN: "No, that's not right.<end>"
KO: "아니, 틀려<end>"

## 470007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディードの使い方はわかっと～るのね。<end>"
EN: "So you know how to use a Deed, eh?<end>"
KO: "디드 사용법은 알고 있겠지.<end>"

## 470008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ～れなら、わしががかわいこちゃんを<line-break>紹介しちゃうんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "In that case, I'll introduce you to a pretty young thing.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 내가 귀여운 아가씨를 소개해 주지.<end>"

## 470009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アミラルの宿屋にルフレンちゃんて娘が<line-break>いるから、会いに行くといいんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "There's a girl named Lufren at the inn in Amiral. You ought to go and meet her.<end>"
KO: "아미랄 여관에 루프렌이라는 아이가 있으니 만나러 가 보면 좋겠구먼.<end>"

## 470010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディードの使い方はわかっと～るのね。<end>"
EN: "So you know how to use a Deed, eh?<end>"
KO: "디드 사용법은 알고 있겠지.<end>"

## 470011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それなら、ディードを使って大活躍して<line-break>わしの偉さを広めて欲しいんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "Then use Deeds to do great things and spread the word of my greatness!<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 디드를 써서 크게 활약하고 내 위대함을 널리 알려 줬으면 하네.<end>"

## 470012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティラの娘と呼ばれるいにしえの怪物、<line-break>アレを退治して、活躍してほしいのね。<end>"
EN: "I want you to make a name for yourself by defeating that ancient monster called the Daughter of Tira.<end>"
KO: "티라의 딸이라 불리는 고대의 괴물을 퇴치해서 활약해 줬으면 좋겠구먼.<end>"

## 470013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ～れじゃ、ロジクーニンの豆知識を　<line-break>ひ～とつ披露するのね。<end>"
EN: "Well then, let Rojikunin share one little tidbit with you.<end>"
KO: "그럼 로지쿠닌의 잡지식 하나를 알려 주지.<end>"

## 470014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%20アミラルのドラゴン祭りは<line-break>毎年、１２月の３１日なんじゃね。<end>テラネのボンガとロストールのオッシは<line-break>実は、知り合いだったりするんじゃね。<line-break><line-break>実は、結構、因縁深い２人なんじゃね。<line-break>２人は闘技場でつなっがと～るのね。<line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<end>神の手のロッシマの<line-break>誕生日は５月の７日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ツェラシェルの妹ヴァイライラの<line-break>誕生日は１月の１４日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ツェラシェルの妹ヴィアリアリの<line-break>誕生日は１月の１４日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>テラネの宿屋のおじさんの<line-break>誕生日は５月の１１日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>テラネの宿屋のおばさんの<line-break>誕生日は７月の１９日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ロストールの剣術指南オッシの<line-break>誕生日は８月の１２日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ウルカーンの長老シェムハザの<line-break>誕生日は１１月の４日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>フゴーの護衛兼鑑定人ダイダロの<line-break>誕生日は１０月の１０日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>フゴーの奥さん兼影武者のフゴー夫人の<line-break>誕生日は２月の１７日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>エルズのテレジアの<line-break>誕生日は１０月の１９日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ファーロス家当主、大公ノヴィンの<line-break>誕生日は５月の７日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ロストールの酒場のフェルムちゃんの<line-break>誕生日は５月の１１日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>わしの誕生日、１月１日じゃからね。<line-break>わしとフェルムちゃんの<line-break>相性はどうか知りたいんじゃね。<end>エルズの竜の座の女官ベモラベラモの<line-break>誕生日は１２月の１７日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>アミラルのロベルトの奥さんポーリアの<line-break>誕生日は７月の６日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ノーブルの悪代官ボルボラ兄弟の<line-break>誕生日は１２月の８日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>テラネの木こりボンガの<line-break>誕生日は１２月の１７日なんじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>テラネの木こりボンガの奥さんの<line-break>誕生日は５月の５日なんじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>アミラルの宿屋の娘ルフレンちゃんの<line-break>誕生日は２月の２４日なんじゃね。<line-break><line-break>わしの誕生日は１月１日じゃからね。<line-break>わしとルフレンちゃんの<line-break>相性はどうか知りたいんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%20Amiral's Dragon Festival is held every year on December 31.<end>Bonga of Terane and Ossi of Rostorl actually know each other. There's quite a history between those two, in fact. The arena is what connects them. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about.<end>Rossima of the Hand of God was born on May 7. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Zeraschel's younger sister Vailaila was born on January 14. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Zeraschel's younger sister Vialiali was born on January 14. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>The man at the inn in Terane was born on May 11. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>The woman at the inn in Terane was born on July 19. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Ossi, the swordsmanship instructor in Rostorl, was born on August 12. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Shemhaza, the elder of Ulkarne, was born on November 4. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Daedalo, Hugo's escort and appraiser, was born on October 10. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Mrs. Hugo, Hugo's wife and double, was born on February 17. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Terezia of Elz was born on October 19. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Grand Duke Novin, head of the Pharos family, was born on May 7. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Ferme from the tavern in Rostorl was born on May 11. My birthday is January 1, you know. I'd like to know how well Ferme and I would match.<end>Bemora Beramo, a court lady at the Seat of the Dragon in Elz, was born on December 17. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Porlia, Roberto's wife in Amiral, was born on July 6. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>The corrupt magistrates known as the Bolbora brothers of Noble were born on December 8. Sorry if you've no idea who they are. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Bonga, the woodcutter from Terane, was born on December 17. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>The wife of Bonga, the woodcutter from Terane, was born on May 5. Sorry if you've no idea who that is. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Lufren, the innkeeper's daughter in Amiral, was born on February 24. My birthday is January 1, you know. I'd like to know how well Lufren and I would match.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%20아미랄의 드래곤 축제는 매년 12월 31일이래.<end>테라네의 봉가와 로스톨의 오시는 사실 아는 사이래. 꽤 깊은 인연이 있고 둘은 투기장으로 이어져 있다나. 모르는 사람들이면 미안해.<end>신의 손 롯시마의 생일은 5월 7일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>체라셸의 여동생 바일라이라의 생일은 1월 14일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>체라셸의 여동생 비아리아리의 생일은 1월 14일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>테라네 여관 아저씨의 생일은 5월 11일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>테라네 여관 아주머니의 생일은 7월 19일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>로스톨의 검술 사범 오시의 생일은 8월 12일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>울칸의 장로 셈하자 생일은 11월 4일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>휴고의 호위 겸 감정인 다이다로의 생일은 10월 10일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>휴고의 아내 겸 대역인 휴고 부인의 생일은 2월 17일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>엘즈의 테레지아 생일은 10월 19일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>파로스 가문 당주 대공 노빈의 생일은 5월 7일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>로스톨 술집의 페름 양 생일은 5월 11일이래. 내 생일은 1월 1일이니 페름 양과 궁합이 어떤지 궁금하네.<end>엘즈 용의 자리의 시녀 베모라베라모 생일은 12월 17일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>아미랄의 로베르토 아내 폴리아의 생일은 7월 6일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>노블의 악덕 관리 볼보라 형제 생일은 12월 8일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>테라네의 나무꾼 봉가 생일은 12월 17일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>테라네 나무꾼 봉가의 아내 생일은 5월 5일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>아미랄 여관집 딸 루프렌 양 생일은 2월 24일이래. 내 생일은 1월 1일이니 루프렌 양과 궁합이 어떤지 궁금하네.<end>"

## 470015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま～、今、ディードの研究を<line-break>鋭意、進めとるからね～。<line-break><line-break>ま～た、日をあらためて来～てくれたら<line-break>新し～い研究の成果を実験させて<line-break>もらうから、ま～た来るんじゃね～。<end>"
EN: "Well, I'm hard at work researching Deeds at the moment. Come back another day and I'll have you help me test the latest results of my research. So do come again, eh?<end>"
KO: "뭐~ 지금 디드 연구를 열심히 진행 중이라서 말이지~. 또 날짜를 바꿔서 와 주면 새로운 연구 성과를 실험하게 해 줄 테니, 또 오라고~.<end>"

## 470016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫か！？<line-break>俺様が通りかかったからには<line-break>もう、助かったも同然だぞ！<end>"
EN: "Are you all right?! Now that I've come along, you're as good as saved!<end>"
KO: "괜찮나!? 이 몸이 지나가다 봤으니 이제 산 거나 마찬가지다!<end>"

## 470017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おなかが…すいて…。<end>"
EN: "I'm... hungry...<end>"
KO: "배가… 고파서….<end>"

## 470018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、飢えか！　<line-break>よし、任せろ！！　<end>"
EN: "Gah, you're starving! Very well―leave it to me!<end>"
KO: "큭, 굶주림인가! 좋아, 맡겨 둬!!<end>"

## 470019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、これだ！<line-break>この勇者の熱い魂のこもった<line-break>パンを食べるがいい！<end>"
EN: "Here it is! Eat this bread, infused with the fiery soul of the Hero himself!<end>"
KO: "자, 이거다! 이 용자의 뜨거운 혼이 담긴 빵을 먹어라!<end>"

## 470020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ありがとうございました。<line-break>これで元気が出ました。<end>"
EN: "Oh, thank you very much. I feel better already.<end>"
KO: "아, 감사합니다. 이제 기운이 났어요.<end>"

## 470021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勇者の熱き魂に触れる者<line-break>また、ひとり、か。<end>"
EN: "Another soul touched by the fiery spirit of a Hero...<end>"
KO: "용자의 뜨거운 혼에 닿은 자가 또 한 명인가.<end>"

## 470022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…しまった！　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>俺様自身、何日も食ってなくて…<line-break>餓死寸前であのパンを拾ったのを<line-break>忘れていた…！<end>"
EN: "Damn it! I haven't eaten in days myself... I'd forgotten I picked up that bread when I was on the brink of starving to death!<end>"
KO: "…아차! 나도 며칠이나 못 먹어서… 굶어 죽기 직전에 그 빵을 주운 걸 잊고 있었어…!<end>"

## 470023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐふっ！<end>"
EN: "Guh!<end>"
KO: "크헉!<end>"

## 470024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへっ！　無様だな。<line-break>未来の勇者様よお！？　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>調子に乗って人助けして<line-break>自分は行き倒れか？<line-break>とんだおもしろ怪物だな、お前は。<end>"
EN: "Heh! What a sorry sight you are, O future Hero! You got carried away helping someone, and now you're the one lying in the road? You're one hilarious monster.<end>"
KO: "헤헷! 꼴사납군. 미래의 용자님이시여!? 신나서 남을 돕더니 정작 자신은 길에서 쓰러졌나? 정말 웃기는 괴물이군, 너는.<end>"

## 470025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっ、熱き魂を持つ者は<line-break>その熱き魂を正しく燃やすために<line-break>日々戦わねばならないのだ…。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Those with fiery souls must fight each day to ensure that fire burns true...<end>"
KO: "후, 뜨거운 혼을 지닌 자는 그 뜨거운 혼을 올바르게 불태우기 위해 날마다 싸워야 하는 법이지….<end>"

## 470026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ？　いきなり何言い出すんだ？<end>"
EN: "Huh? What are you babbling about all of a sudden?<end>"
KO: "뭐? 갑자기 무슨 소리야?<end>"

## 470027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様……あのテラネの少年だな？　　　<line-break>貴様の友人たちが、<line-break>俺様に教えてくれたのだ。<line-break>彼らは、復讐のために<line-break>俺様をつけまわしていた…はずだった。<line-break>しかし、彼らは俺様を救ってくれた。<end>"
EN: "You... You're that boy from Terane, aren't you? Your friends taught me something. They had been following me to take revenge... or so I thought. Yet in the end, they saved me.<end>"
KO: "네놈… 테라네의 그 소년이군? 네 친구들이 나님에게 가르쳐 줬다. 그들은 복수를 위해 나님을 쫓아다니고… 있었어야 했다. 하지만 그들은 나님을 구해 줬지.<end>"

## 470028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、少し違うな。彼らは<line-break>自分の中の正しさのために力を使い、<line-break>その結果として俺様を救ったのだ。<line-break>俺様は震えた…。そして、知ったのだ。<line-break>熱き魂が正しき力を使うとき、<line-break>他人の魂をも、熱く燃やすと。<end>"
EN: "No, that's not quite it. They used their strength in service of their own sense of what was right, and as a result, they saved me. I trembled... and then I understood: when a fiery soul uses righteous power, it sets other souls ablaze as well.<end>"
KO: "아니, 조금 다르군. 그들은 자신 안의 올바름을 위해 힘을 썼고 그 결과 나를 구한 것이다. 나는 전율했다…. 그리고 깨달았지. 뜨거운 영혼이 올바른 힘을 쓸 때 다른 이의 영혼까지 뜨겁게 불태운다는 것을.<end>"

## 470029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺様と同じように、彼らもまた　　　　<line-break>未来をひらく熱き魂だったのだ！<line-break>　<line-break>…そして俺様は自分の道を確信した。<line-break>この熱き魂の燃えるがままに<line-break>生涯を戦い抜く、それが未来の勇者だ！<end>"
EN: "Like me, they too were fiery souls opening the way to the future! And with that, I became certain of my own path. To fight all my life as this fiery soul commands―that is what it means to be a future Hero!<end>"
KO: "나와 마찬가지로 그들 또한 미래를 여는 뜨거운 영혼이었던 것이다! …그리고 나는 내 길을 확신했다. 이 뜨거운 영혼이 타오르는 대로 평생 싸워 나가는 것, 그것이 미래의 용사다!<end>"

## 470030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "テラネの少年…、貴様もいつか<line-break>その魂を熱く燃やすのだろうな。<line-break>あの熱き魂たちの友なのだから…。<end>"
EN: "Boy from Terane... one day, your soul too will burn fiercely. You are a friend to those fiery souls, after all...<end>"
KO: "테라네의 소년이여… 너도 언젠가 그 영혼을 뜨겁게 불태우겠지. 저 뜨거운 영혼들의 친구이니 말이다….<end>"

## 470031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…けっ、ガルドランのくせに<line-break>偉そうに語りやがって…。<end>"
EN: "Tch... Girldlan, talking all high and mighty...<end>"
KO: "…쳇, 갈드란 주제에 잘난 척 떠들어 대긴….<end>"

## 470032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジ…！？<end>"
EN: "Nadge...?!<end>"
KO: "나지…!?<end>"

## 470033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、ヴァン！　何をしたの！！<line-break>僕、ヴァンのこと…心配して…なのに！<end>"
EN: "Van! What did you do?! After I was so worried about you...!<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 반! 무슨 짓을 한 거야!! 난 반이 걱정돼서… 그런데!<end>"

## 470034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ははっ、<line-break>俺がガルドランなんか相手にするかよ。<line-break>　<line-break>こいつは別口でおねんねしてるんだ。<line-break>…そんなに騒ぐなよ、大丈夫だ。<end>"
EN: "Ha! Like I'd bother fighting Girldlan. This guy's having a little nap for some other reason. Don't make such a fuss. It's all right.<end>"
KO: "하하, 내가 갈드란 따위를 상대하겠냐. 이 녀석은 다른 건으로 잠들어 있는 거야. …그렇게 소란 피우지 마. 괜찮아.<end>"

## 470035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当？<end>"
EN: "Really?<end>"
KO: "정말?<end>"

## 470036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、本当だって。<line-break>ところで、<value:$28>…。<end>"
EN: "Yeah, really. By the way, <value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "그래, 정말이라니까. 그런데 <value:$28>….<end>"

## 470037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺も仲間に加えてくれ、<line-break>俺、お前らと一緒に旅がしたいんだ。<end>"
EN: "Let me join you. I want to travel with all of you.<end>"
KO: "나도 동료로 넣어 줘. 나, 너희와 함께 여행하고 싶어.<end>"

## 470038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>！<line-break>ヴァンには宿屋が…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! But Van has the inn...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>! 반에게는 여관이….<end>"

## 470039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいって、いいって。<line-break>じゃ、よろしくな、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "All right, all right. I'm counting on you, then, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아, 괜찮아. 그럼 잘 부탁해, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 470040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、本当にガルドランを<line-break>放っておいていいのかなあ。<end>"
EN: "Do you really think we should just leave Girldlan here?<end>"
KO: "있지, 정말 갈드란을 그냥 내버려 둬도 되는 걸까?<end>"

## 470041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいさ。あいつの熱き魂とやらで<line-break>なんとかするだろ。行こうぜ。<end>"
EN: "He'll be fine. That fiery soul of his will see him through somehow. Come on, let's go.<end>"
KO: "됐어. 그 녀석의 뜨거운 영혼인가 뭔가로 어떻게든 하겠지. 가자.<end>"

## 470042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、俺はこいつの面倒みるから。<line-break>あとで、一緒にな。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll look after this guy. I'll join you later.<end>"
KO: "그럼 난 이 녀석을 돌볼게. 나중에 같이 가자.<end>"

## 470043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec047-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ハラが…減った…。<line-break>もう何も見えない…。<line-break>もう何も聞こえない…。　　　　　　<line-break>だが、そこの君よ、安心してくれ。<line-break>未来の勇者は必ずまた立ちあがる…。<end>"
EN: "I'm... hungry... I can't see anything anymore... I can't hear anything anymore... But you there, rest assured. The Hero of the future will rise again, without fail...<end>"
KO: "…배가… 고프다…. 이제 아무것도 안 보여…. 아무것도 안 들려…. 하지만 거기 자네, 안심해. 미래의 용자는 반드시 다시 일어난다….<end>"

## 480001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや、あ～んた、な～んとか君じゃね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, it's yooou, What-was-your-name.<end>"
KO: "어이쿠, 자네 그 뭐시기 군 아닌가.<end>"

## 480002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わし、あんたを、よ～く覚えとるのね。<line-break>ほ～とんど忘れとるんじゃけどね。<end>"
EN: "I remember you very well. Though I've forgotten almost everything about you.<end>"
KO: "난 당신을 아주 잘 기억하고 있지. 거의 다 잊었지만 말이야.<end>"

## 480003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そろそろ、立場みたいなのが決定されて<line-break>くるから、よく考えて行動せ～んとね。<end>"
EN: "Your place in things will start taking shape soon, so you'd best think carefully before you act.<end>"
KO: "슬슬 자기 입장 같은 게 정해질 테니 잘 생각하고 행동해야겠구먼.<end>"

## 480004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、こ～こには、<line-break>ディードの説明を聞きに来たんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "You've come here to hear about Deed, haven't you?<end>"
KO: "당신, 여기에 디드 설명을 들으러 온 거지?<end>"

## 480005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その通り<end>"
EN: "That's right.<end>"
KO: "맞아<end>"

## 480006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや違う<end>"
EN: "No, that's wrong.<end>"
KO: "아니, 틀려<end>"

## 480007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディードの使い方はわかっと～るのね。<end>"
EN: "You do know how to use Deed, don't you?<end>"
KO: "디드 사용법은 알고 있겠지.<end>"

## 480008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ～れなら、わしががかわいこちゃんを<line-break>紹介しちゃうんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "Then let me introduce you to a pretty young thing.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 내가 귀여운 아가씨를 소개해 주지.<end>"

## 480009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アミラルの宿屋にルフレンちゃんて娘が<line-break>いるから、会いに行くといいんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "There's a girl called Lufren at the inn in Amiral. You should go and see her.<end>"
KO: "아미랄 여관에 루프렌이라는 아이가 있으니 만나러 가 보면 좋겠구먼.<end>"

## 480010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディードの使い方はわかっと～るのね。<end>"
EN: "You do know how to use Deed, don't you?<end>"
KO: "디드 사용법은 알고 있겠지.<end>"

## 480011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それなら、ディードを使って大活躍して<line-break>わしの偉さを広めて欲しいんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "Then I want you to use Deed to perform great deeds and spread word of my greatness.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 디드를 써서 크게 활약하고 내 위대함을 널리 알려 줬으면 하네.<end>"

## 480012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティラの娘と呼ばれるいにしえの怪物、<line-break>アレを退治して、活躍してほしいのね。<end>"
EN: "I want you to make a name for yourself by slaying that ancient monster known as the Daughter of Tira.<end>"
KO: "티라의 딸이라 불리는 고대의 괴물을 퇴치해서 활약해 줬으면 좋겠구먼.<end>"

## 480013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディードの使い方はわかっと～るのね。<end>"
EN: "You do know how to use Deed, don't you?<end>"
KO: "디드 사용법은 알고 있겠지.<end>"

## 480014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたの仲間のセラ君は強いんじゃね。<line-break>彼と一緒に冒険して欲し～いんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "That companion of yours, Sera, is strong. I'd like you to go adventuring with him.<end>"
KO: "자네 동료 세라 군은 강하군. 그와 함께 모험했으면 좋겠구먼.<end>"

## 480015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ～うすればあんたも、大活躍で～きて<line-break>ディードも有名にな～れるんじゃね～。<end>"
EN: "Then you'll be able to achieve great things, and Deed will become famous too.<end>"
KO: "그러면 자네도 크게 활약하고 디드도 유명해질 수 있겠구먼.<end>"

## 480016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ～れじゃ、ロジクーニンの豆知識を　<line-break>ひ～とつ披露するのね。<end>"
EN: "Now then, allow me to share one little tidbit from Logikunin.<end>"
KO: "그럼 로지쿠닌의 잡지식 하나를 알려 주지.<end>"

## 480017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%20アミラルのドラゴン祭りは<line-break>毎年、○○月の○○日なんじゃね。<end>テラネのボンガとロストールのオッシは<line-break>実は、知り合いだったりするんじゃね。<line-break><line-break>実は、結構、因縁深い２人なんじゃね。<line-break>２人は闘技場でつなっがと～るのね。<line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<end>神の手のロッシマの<line-break>誕生日は５月の７日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ツェラシェルの妹ヴァイライラの<line-break>誕生日は１月の１４日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ツェラシェルの妹ヴィアリアリの<line-break>誕生日は１月の１４日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>古代の魔道士、封師レオニックが、<line-break>魔道技術を封じたレオニックの迷宮…<line-break><line-break>ロッシマはそのレオニックの迷宮に<line-break>挑んだこ～とがあ～るんじゃね。<line-break>…てことは彼なら場所を知っとるのね。<end>アミラルのロベルトと<line-break>神の手のロッシマは知り合いな～のね。<line-break><line-break>昔、彼らは一緒に旅をした仲間なのね。<line-break>ロッシマについて、知～りたかったら<line-break>ロベルトにきいてみたらいいんじゃね。<end>ロストールの剣術指南オッシの<line-break>誕生日は８月の１２日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>霊峰トールはだてに霊峰じゃないのね。<line-break>頂上にはちょっとした秘密があるのね。<end>フゴーの護衛兼鑑定人ダイダロの<line-break>誕生日は１０月の１０日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>フゴーの奥さん兼影武者のフゴー夫人の<line-break>誕生日は２月の１７日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>エルズのテレジアの<line-break>誕生日は１０月の１９日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ファーロス家当主、大公ノヴィンの<line-break>誕生日は５月の７日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>なんにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>ロストールの酒場のフェルムちゃんの<line-break>誕生日は５月の１１日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>わしの誕生日、１月１日じゃからね。<line-break>わしとフェルムちゃんの<line-break>相性はどうか知りたいんじゃね。<end>エルズの竜の座の女官ベモラベラモの<line-break>誕生日は１２月の１７日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>アミラルのロベルトの奥さんポーリアの<line-break>誕生日は７月の６日な～んじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>この世界には、い～たるところに　　　<line-break>運命の女神の刻印があ～るのね。<line-break><line-break>ちょ～っと、そういうと～ころに<line-break>目をつ～けてみると、お～もしろいかも<line-break>し～れないんじゃね。<end>テラネの木こりボンガの<line-break>誕生日は１２月の１７日なんじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>テラネの木こりボンガの奥さんの<line-break>誕生日は５月の５日なんじゃね。<line-break><line-break>知らない人の話だったらゴメンね。<line-break>それに、そんなこと知ってても、<line-break>な～んにもならなんじゃけどね。<end>アミラルの宿屋の娘ルフレンちゃんの<line-break>誕生日は２月の２４日なんじゃね。<line-break><line-break>わしの誕生日は１月１日じゃからね。<line-break>わしとルフレンちゃんの<line-break>相性はどうか知りたいんじゃね。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%20Amiral's Dragon Festival is held every year on the XXth day of XX.<end>Bonga of Terane and Ossi of Rostorl actually know each other. In fact, the two of them have quite a tangled history. Their connection goes back to the arena. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about.<end>Rossima of the Hand of God was born on May 7. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Zeraschel's younger sister Vailaila was born on January 14. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Zeraschel's younger sister Vialiali was born on January 14. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Leonique's Labyrinth, where the ancient mage and Seal Master Leonique sealed away magical technology... Rossima once ventured into that very labyrinth. Which means he must know where it is.<end>Roberto of Amiral and Rossima of the Hand of God know each other. Long ago, they traveled together as companions. If you want to know about Rossima, try asking Roberto.<end>Ossi, Rostorl's swordsmanship instructor, was born on August 12. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Sacred Mount Thor isn't called sacred for nothing. There's a little secret at its summit.<end>Daedalo, Hugo's escort and appraiser, was born on October 10. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Mrs. Hugo, Hugo's wife and double, was born on February 17. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Terezia of Elz was born on October 19. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Grand Duke Novin, head of the Pharos family, was born on May 7. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Ferme from the tavern in Rostorl was born on May 11. My birthday is January 1, you see. I'd like to know how compatible Ferme and I are.<end>Bemoraberamo, a lady-in-waiting at the Seat of the Dragon in Elz, was born on December 17. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Paulia, wife of Roberto of Amiral, was born on July 6. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>The marks of the Goddess of Fate can be found all over this world. It might be interesting to keep an eye out for places like that.<end>Bonga, the woodcutter from Terane, was born on December 17. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>The wife of Bonga, the woodcutter from Terane, was born on May 5. Sorry if you've no idea who I'm talking about. Not that knowing this does you any good.<end>Lufren, the innkeeper's daughter in Amiral, was born on February 24. My birthday is January 1, you see. I'd like to know how compatible Lufren and I are.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%20아미랄의 드래곤 축제는 매년 ○○월 ○○일이래.<end>테라네의 봉가와 로스톨의 오시는 사실 아는 사이래. 꽤 깊은 인연이 있고 둘은 투기장으로 이어져 있다나. 모르는 사람들이면 미안해.<end>신의 손 롯시마의 생일은 5월 7일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>체라셸의 여동생 바일라이라의 생일은 1월 14일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>체라셸의 여동생 비아리아리의 생일은 1월 14일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>고대 마도사 봉인사 레오닉이 마도 기술을 봉인한 레오닉의 미궁…. 롯시마는 거기에 도전한 적이 있대. 그렇다면 위치를 알겠지.<end>아미랄의 로베르토와 신의 손 롯시마는 아는 사이래. 옛날에 함께 여행한 동료였대. 롯시마가 궁금하면 로베르토에게 물어봐.<end>로스톨의 검술 사범 오시의 생일은 8월 12일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>영봉 토르는 괜히 영봉이 아니래. 정상에 작은 비밀이 있다나.<end>휴고의 호위 겸 감정인 다이다로의 생일은 10월 10일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>휴고의 아내 겸 대역인 휴고 부인의 생일은 2월 17일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>엘즈의 테레지아 생일은 10월 19일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>파로스 가문 당주 대공 노빈의 생일은 5월 7일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>로스톨 술집의 페름 양 생일은 5월 11일이래. 내 생일은 1월 1일이니 페름 양과 궁합이 어떤지 궁금하네.<end>엘즈 용의 자리의 시녀 베모라베라모 생일은 12월 17일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>아미랄의 로베르토 아내 폴리아의 생일은 7월 6일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>이 세계 곳곳에는 운명의 여신 각인이 있대. 그런 곳에 조금 눈을 돌려 보면 재미있을지도 몰라.<end>테라네의 나무꾼 봉가 생일은 12월 17일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>테라네 나무꾼 봉가의 아내 생일은 5월 5일이래. 모르는 사람이면 미안해. 알아도 별 쓸모는 없지만.<end>아미랄 여관집 딸 루프렌 양 생일은 2월 24일이래. 내 생일은 1월 1일이니 루프렌 양과 궁합이 어떤지 궁금하네.<end>"

## 480018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま～、今、ディードの研究を<line-break>鋭意、進めとるからね～。<line-break><line-break>ま～た、日をあらためて来～てくれたら<line-break>新し～い研究の成果を実験させて<line-break>もらうから、ま～た来るんじゃね～。<end>"
EN: "Well, I'm hard at work researching Deed at the moment. Come back another day, and I'll get to test the latest results of my research. So be sure to come back.<end>"
KO: "뭐~ 지금 디드 연구를 열심히 진행 중이라서 말이지~. 또 날짜를 바꿔서 와 주면 새로운 연구 성과를 실험하게 해 줄 테니, 또 오라고~.<end>"

## 480019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァン、僕、仇をとったんだ。<line-break>ガルドランを殺してきたよ。<end>"
EN: "Van, I got my revenge. I killed Girldlan.<end>"
KO: "반, 나 복수했어. 갈드란을 죽이고 왔어.<end>"

## 480020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねぇ、ヴァン、僕、仇をとったんだ。<line-break>ガルドランを殺してきたよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, Van. I got my revenge. I killed Girldlan.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 반. 나 원수를 갚았어. 갈드란을 죽이고 왔어.<end>"

## 480021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦って力を得ていくうちに<line-break>人は、命や他人の痛みに鈍感になる。<line-break>　<line-break>そうして、力を持った人間は<line-break>他人を傷つけなければ<line-break>生きていけないようになる。<end>"
EN: "As people fight and grow stronger, they become numb to the value of life and to the pain of others. In time, those with power can no longer live without hurting others.<end>"
KO: "싸우며 힘을 얻어 가다 보면 사람은 목숨이나 남의 아픔에 둔감해진다. 그렇게 힘을 가진 인간은 남을 상처 입히지 않고는 살아갈 수 없게 되지.<end>"

## 480023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ナッジ、お前、人の命を奪ったことを<line-break>笑って話すような奴じゃ<line-break>なかったのに…。<end>"
EN: "Nadge, you never used to be the kind of man who could laugh while talking about taking a life...<end>"
KO: "나지, 너 사람 목숨을 빼앗은 걸 웃으며 말하는 녀석은 아니었잖아….<end>"

## 480024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまない。俺、お前と旅がしたかった。<line-break>でも、今、お前と行く気にはなれない。<line-break>　<line-break>俺は親父と一緒に<line-break>この町で宿屋をやる。<line-break>決めたよ…、それが、俺の人生だ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry. I wanted to travel with you. But now, I can't bring myself to go with you. I'm going to run an inn here in town with my dad. I've made up my mind... That's the life I've chosen.<end>"
KO: "미안하다. 난 너와 여행하고 싶었어. 하지만 지금은 너와 갈 마음이 들지 않아. 아버지와 함께 이 마을에서 여관을 하기로 했어. 정했어… 그게 내 인생이야.<end>"

## 480025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ナッジのこと、頼むぜ。<end>"
EN: "...Take care of Nadge for me.<end>"
KO: "…나지 부탁한다.<end>"

## 480026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…、<line-break>僕、どこで変わっちゃったんだろう…。<line-break>　<line-break>ヴァン…、<line-break>僕はもう、君と一緒には<line-break>歩けないのかな…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... where along the way did I change? Van... maybe I can't walk beside you anymore.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…, 나 어디서부터 변해 버린 걸까…. 반…, 나는 이제 너와 함께 걸을 수 없는 걸까….<end>"

## 480027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>、<line-break>元気そうで何よりだ。<line-break>　<line-break>泊まっていくかい？<line-break>お前らなら、ただでいいぜ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Good to see you looking well. Staying the night? You lot can stay for free.<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>. 건강해 보여 다행이다. 묵고 갈래? 너희라면 공짜로 해 줄게.<end>"

## 480028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "泊まる<end>"
EN: "Stay<end>"
KO: "묵는다<end>"

## 480029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "泊まらない<end>"
EN: "Don't stay<end>"
KO: "묵지 않는다<end>"

## 480030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい。<line-break>じゃ、ゆっくりしていってくれ。<end>"
EN: "All right. Make yourselves at home.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 그럼 푹 쉬다 가.<end>"

## 480031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "疲れはとれたかい？<end>"
EN: "Feeling rested?<end>"
KO: "피로는 좀 풀렸어?<end>"

## 480032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それはよかった。　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Glad to hear it.<end>"
KO: "그건 다행이군.<end>"

## 480033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、俺にできることはこんなもんだ。<line-break>よかったら、また来てくれ。じゃな。<end>"
EN: "Well, that's about all I can do for you. Come back anytime. See you around.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 내가 할 수 있는 건 이 정도다. 괜찮으면 또 와. 그럼.<end>"

## 480034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうかい。<end>"
EN: "All right.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나.<end>"

## 480035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また、顔出せよな。<line-break>歓迎するぜ。<end>"
EN: "Come by again, all right? You'll be welcome.<end>"
KO: "또 얼굴 비춰. 환영할게.<end>"

## 480036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うちは巡礼客だけでいっぱいだ。<line-break>さあ、帰れ！<line-break>この汚い冒険者め！<end>"
EN: "We're full up with pilgrims. Now get out, you filthy adventurer!<end>"
KO: "우리는 순례객만으로 가득 찼다. 자, 돌아가! 이 더러운 모험자 놈!<end>"

## 480037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親父、やめろ。<end>"
EN: "Dad, stop it.<end>"
KO: "아버지, 그만해.<end>"

## 480038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ヴァン。<end>"
EN: "...Van.<end>"
KO: "…반.<end>"

## 480039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親父は色メガネでものを見すぎる。　<line-break>冒険者にもいろいろあるんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>頭っから、<line-break>そんな言い方をするもんじゃない。<line-break>そうだろ、親父？<end>"
EN: "You let your prejudices blind you, Dad. Adventurers aren't all the same. You shouldn't judge people like that from the start. Right, Dad?<end>"
KO: "아버지는 편견으로 사람을 너무 봐. 모험자도 여러 종류가 있어. 처음부터 그런 식으로 말하면 안 되지. 그렇지, 아버지?<end>"

## 480040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァンが、そう言うなら…<line-break>私も、別に…。<end>"
EN: "If you say so, Van... well, I suppose...<end>"
KO: "반이 그렇게 말한다면… 나도 딱히….<end>"

## 480041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまなかったな。<line-break>イヤな気分にさせて。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、今日は巡礼者でいっぱいなのも<line-break>事実なんだ。悪いけど、泊められない。<line-break>また、来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "Sorry I made you feel bad. But the place really is packed with pilgrims today. Sorry, but I can't put you up. Come back another time.<end>"
KO: "미안했어. 기분 나쁘게 해서. 그래도 오늘 순례자가 가득 찬 건 사실이야. 미안하지만 묵게 해 줄 수 없어. 또 와 줘.<end>"

## 480042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、冒険者なんて…！<end>"
EN: "Gah, adventurers...!<end>"
KO: "큭, 모험자 따위…!<end>"

## 480043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親父！<end>"
EN: "Dad!<end>"
KO: "아버지!<end>"

## 480044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァ、ヴァン…。<end>"
EN: "V-Van...<end>"
KO: "바, 반….<end>"

## 480045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec048-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すまん。<line-break>さっきはひどいことを言った。<end>"
EN: "...I'm sorry. I said some terrible things earlier.<end>"
KO: "…미안하다. 아까는 심한 말을 했다.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: ""

## 490001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まずはギルドへ行こう。<end>"
EN: "First, let's go to the Guild.<end>"
KO: "우선 길드로 가자.<end>"

## 490002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやじさんの荷物の後始末を　　　<line-break>しなくちゃ行けないからな。<line-break>　<line-break>それにこれからは、冒険者になる。<line-break>ギルドで冒険者登録を<line-break>しておかないと、な。<end>"
EN: "The old man's belongings still need sorting out. Besides, from now on, it's life as an adventurer. Better register with the Guild.<end>"
KO: "아저씨 짐을 정리해야 하거든. 그리고 이제부터는 모험자가 될 거야. 길드에 모험자 등록도 해 둬야지.<end>"

## 490003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスさんじゃないか。<line-break>最近、顔を見せなかったが、<line-break>どこをほっつき歩いていたんだい？<end>"
EN: "Xenetes! Haven't seen you around lately. Where've you been wandering off to?<end>"
KO: "제네테스 씨잖아. 요즘 얼굴을 안 보이더니 어디를 돌아다니고 있었어?<end>"

## 490004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、いろんなとこだ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, here and there.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 여러 곳이지.<end>"

## 490005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたじゃないと、<line-break>手に負えない依頼がたまってるんだよ。<line-break>ちょっと、片付けてくれないかい？<end>"
EN: "I've got a pile of jobs only you can handle. Think you could clear them out for me?<end>"
KO: "너 아니면 감당 못 할 의뢰가 쌓여 있어. 좀 처리해 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 490006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いんや。　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "Nope.<end>"
KO: "아니.<end>"

## 490007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今から俺はこいつの相棒になるんでな。<line-break>悪いが引き受けられない。<end>"
EN: "I'm partnering up with this one now. Sorry, but I can't take them.<end>"
KO: "이제부터 난 이 녀석의 동료가 될 거라서. 미안하지만 맡을 수 없어.<end>"

## 490008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつのギルド登録を<line-break>してやってくれ、おやじ。<end>"
EN: "Register this one with the Guild, will you, old man?<end>"
KO: "이 녀석 길드 등록 좀 해 줘, 아저씨.<end>"

## 490009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へえ。あんたかい。<line-break>ゼネテスさんが目を付けたんだ。<line-break>将来有望なんだろうね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, so it's you. If you've caught Xenetes's eye, you must have a promising future ahead of you.<end>"
KO: "오. 네가 그 사람이야? 제네테스 씨가 눈여겨봤다니 장래가 유망한가 보네.<end>"

## 490010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "犯罪登録なし。幻術計振れておらず。　　　<line-break>…はい、あなたは今日から<line-break>冒険者として登録されました。<line-break>頑張って仕事してくれよ。<line-break>あんたが有名になったら、そのギルド登録を<line-break>したのは俺だって鼻が高いからね。<end>"
EN: "No criminal record. No reading on the illusion gauge... All right. As of today, you're officially registered as an adventurer. Work hard out there. If you become famous, I'll be proud to say I was the one who registered you with the Guild.<end>"
KO: "범죄 등록 없음. 환술계도 이상 없음. …좋습니다, 오늘부터 모험자로 등록됐습니다. 열심히 일해 주세요. 당신이 유명해지면 그 길드 등록을 내가 했다고 자랑할 수 있으니까요.<end>"

## 490011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それから、こいつの馬車が<line-break>町の出口にとめてある。　　<line-break>　<line-break>その荷物を処分したいんだ。<end>"
EN: "There's one more thing. This one's wagon is parked near the way out of town. I want to get rid of the cargo.<end>"
KO: "그리고 이 녀석 마차가 마을 출구에 세워져 있어. 그 짐도 처분하고 싶어.<end>"

## 490012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これだけがこいつが抱えている、<line-break>商品の受注先のリストだ。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ついでだが、これだけのものを<line-break>責任持って、先方に届けて欲しい。<line-break>報酬は荷物を処分した分から払う。<end>"
EN: "This is the full list of customers waiting on the goods in that cargo. While you're at it, I want you to make sure all of it reaches the proper recipients. Your fee will be paid out of the proceeds from disposing of the cargo.<end>"
KO: "이게 이 녀석이 가지고 있는 상품 주문처 목록의 전부야. 그리고 이 물건들도 책임지고 상대에게 전해 줘. 보수는 짐을 처리한 몫에서 지급하지.<end>"

## 490013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おいおい、仕事の依頼を断った上に、<line-break>そっちから仕事をもってくるのかよ？<end>"
EN: "Hold on. First you turn down my jobs, and now you're bringing me one of your own?<end>"
KO: "이봐, 의뢰를 거절해 놓고 오히려 네 쪽에서 일을 가져오는 거야?<end>"

## 490014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大事なお客様ってわけだ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>それともうひとつ。<line-break>ツェラシェルに会いたい。<line-break>奴を呼んでくれ。<end>"
EN: "This is an important customer. One more thing: I want to see Zeraschel. Send for Zeraschel.<end>"
KO: "중요한 손님이라는 거군. 그리고 하나 더. 체라셸을 만나고 싶다. 녀석을 불러 줘.<end>"

## 490016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのかわり、一流だ。　　　　　　　<line-break>奴の腕が必要なんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>ま、そーゆーわけで早急に金もいる。<line-break>以上２件、いつまでにできる？<end>"
EN: "But Zeraschel is first-rate. I need Zeraschel's skills. And that's why I need the money quickly, too. When can you have both done?<end>"
KO: "대신 일류야. 그 녀석 솜씨가 필요해. 뭐, 그런 이유로 급히 돈도 필요하고. 이상 두 건, 언제까지 가능해?<end>"

## 490017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん。<line-break>少なくとも、１週間は<line-break>見てもらわんとなあ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. You'll have to give me at least a week.<end>"
KO: "으음. 적어도 일주일은 잡아야겠군.<end>"

## 490018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった。<end>"
EN: "All right.<end>"
KO: "알았어.<end>"

## 490019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、あと１週間も<line-break>ぶらぶらしてるのもなんだしな。<line-break>　<line-break>仕事するか？<line-break><value:$28>？<end>"
EN: "Well, no sense in loafing around for a week. Want to do some work, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "그럼 앞으로 일주일을 빈둥대기도 그렇고. 일할래, <value:$28>?<end>"

## 490020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、お前さんが仕事を取ってこい。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>まず、カウンターのギルドの親父に<line-break>話しかけるんだ。<line-break>そのあとは親父が説明してくれる。<end>"
EN: "Then you go pick up a job. Start by talking to the Guildmaster at the counter. He'll explain the rest.<end>"
KO: "그럼 네가 일을 받아 와. 먼저 카운터의 길드 아저씨에게 말을 걸어. 그다음은 그 사람이 설명해 줄 거야.<end>"

## 490021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者の基本は、こうやって　　　　　<line-break>ギルドの仕事をやって<line-break>地道に経験を重ねていくことだ。<line-break>ま、もちろん、世界を巡り<line-break>自由に探検するのも大事だがな。<line-break>んじゃ、町から出るときに合流しよう。<end>"
EN: "The foundation of adventuring is taking Guild jobs like this and steadily building experience. Of course, traveling the world and exploring freely matters too. I'll meet you when you're ready to leave town.<end>"
KO: "모험가의 기본은 이렇게 길드 일을 하면서 착실히 경험을 쌓는 거야. 물론 세계를 돌아다니며 자유롭게 탐험하는 것도 중요하지만. 그럼 마을에서 나갈 때 합류하자.<end>"

## 490022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと。<line-break>世界に出るより、まずはギルドだ。<line-break>　<line-break>おやじさんの荷物の処分も<line-break>しなくちゃ行けないし、<line-break>お前さんも今日から冒険者だ。<end>"
EN: "Wait. Before we head out into the world, we need to visit the Guild. We still have to dispose of the old man's belongings, and as of today, you're an adventurer too.<end>"
KO: "잠깐. 세상으로 나가기 전에 우선 길드부터야. 아버지 짐도 처분해야 하고, 너도 오늘부터 모험자잖아.<end>"

## 490023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギルドで冒険者登録は<line-break>済ませとかないと困るぜ？　　　　<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "You'll need to register as an adventurer at the Guild, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "길드에서 모험자 등록은 해 둬야 한다고, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 490024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、待ってたぜ。<line-break>まずは、金を受け取ろうや。<end>"
EN: "There you are. I've been waiting. First, let's collect the money.<end>"
KO: "여어, 기다렸어. 우선 돈부터 받자고.<end>"

## 490025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "金の方は用意できている。<line-break>商品の方の配送も終わった。<line-break>　<line-break>大変だったぜ。<line-break>いきなりあんだけの仕事が<line-break>舞い込んだんだからな。<end>"
EN: "The money's ready, and all the goods have been delivered. It was a real job, having that much work dropped on us without warning.<end>"
KO: "돈은 준비돼 있다. 상품 배송도 끝났고. 고생 좀 했지. 갑자기 그렇게 많은 일이 몰려들었으니까.<end>"

## 490026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっちが、<line-break>あんたの荷物や馬車を処分したり、<line-break>商品の代金を集めたりしてできた金。　<line-break>こっちが<line-break>冒険者を雇った人件費、その他<line-break>もろもろの諸経費。確認してくれ。<end>"
EN: "This is what we made from selling off your belongings and carriage and collecting payment for the goods. And this is what we spent hiring adventurers, along with all the other expenses. Check it over.<end>"
KO: "이쪽이 네 짐과 마차를 처분하고 상품 대금을 모아 만든 돈. 이쪽이 모험자를 고용한 인건비와 그 밖의 여러 경비야. 확인해 줘.<end>"

## 490027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これを差し引きして…、残りはちょうど５０００だ。はいよ。<end>"
EN: "Deducting all that... leaves exactly 5,000. Here you go.<end>"
KO: "이걸 빼고 나면… 남은 건 정확히 5000이다. 자.<end>"

## 490028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "５０００か…。ツェラシェルを雇うには<line-break>少し足りないな。<end>"
EN: "5,000... That's a little short of what we'd need to hire Zeraschel.<end>"
KO: "5000인가…. 첼라셸을 고용하기엔 조금 부족하군.<end>"

## 490029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "少し？　ぜんぜん足りないぞ。<line-break>どうするんだ？<end>"
EN: "A little? It's nowhere near enough. What are you going to do?<end>"
KO: "조금? 전혀 부족해. 어쩔 거야?<end>"

## 490030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、その辺はなんとかするさ。<end>"
EN: "Well, I'll work something out.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그건 어떻게든 되겠지.<end>"

## 490031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親父、ツェラシェルの方は<line-break>どうなってる？<end>"
EN: "Old man, any word from Zeraschel?<end>"
KO: "아버지, 체라셸 쪽은 어떻게 됐어?<end>"

## 490032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日にも来ると思うが…。<end>"
EN: "Should be here sometime today...<end>"
KO: "오늘쯤 올 거라고 생각하지만….<end>"

## 490033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、来たら<line-break>酒場で待ってると伝えてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Then when Zeraschel arrives, say I'll be waiting at the tavern.<end>"
KO: "그럼 오면 술집에서 기다린다고 전해 줘.<end>"

## 490034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃな。俺は酒場で待ってる。<line-break>お前さんもすぐ来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "All right, then. I'll be waiting at the tavern. You come straight over too.<end>"
KO: "그럼 이따 보자. 난 술집에서 기다릴게. 너도 곧 와 줘.<end>"

## 490035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それと、今手にいれた５０００ギアは<line-break>手をつけないで別にしといてくれ。<line-break>ちょっと、使い道があるんでな。<end>"
EN: "One more thing. Don't touch that 5,000 Gea we just got. Set it aside. I've got a use for it.<end>"
KO: "그리고 방금 손에 넣은 5000기어는 손대지 말고 따로 놔 둬. 쓸 데가 좀 있거든.<end>"

## 490038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、待ってたぜ。　<end>"
EN: "Hey. I've been waiting for you.<end>"
KO: "여어, 기다리고 있었어.<end>"

## 490039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからツェラシェルという<line-break>冒険者に会う。<line-break>色々と問題はあるが、腕は一流の男だ。<line-break>そいつから情報を仕入れて<line-break>俺たちは<line-break>ルルアンタを追いかける。<end>"
EN: "We're about to meet an adventurer named Zeraschel. He's got his problems, but his skill is first-rate. Once we've got some information from him, we'll go after Lulluantha.<end>"
KO: "이제 체라셸이라는 모험자를 만날 거야. 여러 문제가 있지만 솜씨는 일류인 남자지. 그에게서 정보를 얻어 루루안타를 쫓는다.<end>"

## 490040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分のつらさを乗り越えて、お前さんに<line-break>優しい手を差し伸べてくれた女の子に、<line-break>今度はお前さんが手を差し出すんだ。<line-break>…っと、来たみたいだな。<end>"
EN: "That girl overcame her own pain to offer you a helping hand. Now it's your turn to offer her one. Ah, looks like he's here.<end>"
KO: "자기 고통을 이겨내고 네게 다정한 손을 내밀어 준 여자아이에게 이번엔 네가 손을 내미는 거야. …어이쿠, 온 것 같군.<end>"

## 490041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "久しぶりだな、<line-break>イカサマ野郎。<end>"
EN: "Long time no see, you cheating bastard.<end>"
KO: "오랜만이군, 사기꾼 자식.<end>"

## 490042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前の依頼内容は大体わかっている。　<line-break>そこの子がエリスの手の者に<line-break>狙われる可能性はあるか。<line-break>それともうひとつ。ルルアンタとかいう<line-break>リルビーの娘の行方。<line-break>この２つを知りたいんだろ？<end>"
EN: "I have a fair idea what you want. First, whether that child there is likely to be targeted by Eris's people. Second, the whereabouts of a Lilubee girl called Lulluantha. Those are the two things you want to know, right?<end>"
KO: "네 의뢰 내용은 대충 알고 있어. 저 아이가 에리스 쪽 사람들에게 노려질 가능성이 있는지, 그리고 루루안타라는 릴비 여자아이의 행방. 이 두 가지를 알고 싶은 거지?<end>"

## 490043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な？<line-break>問題はあるけど一流だろ？<end>"
EN: "See? He's got his problems, but he's first-rate.<end>"
KO: "그렇지? 문제는 있어도 일류잖아?<end>"

## 490044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "で、どうだった？<line-break>お前のことだ、結構な情報を<line-break>仕入れてきてくれたんだろ？<end>"
EN: "So, what did you find out? Knowing you, you must've dug up plenty.<end>"
KO: "그래서 어땠어? 너라면 꽤 쓸 만한 정보를 알아 왔겠지?<end>"

## 490045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まず金だ。軽妙な会話で<line-break>俺がうっかり情報をたれ流すと思うか？<line-break>ここから先は、有料の会話だ。<end>"
EN: "Money first. Did you think a little witty banter would make me carelessly spill everything? From here on, the conversation costs you.<end>"
KO: "우선 돈부터. 가벼운 대화로 내가 무심코 정보를 술술 흘릴 것 같아? 여기서부터는 유료 대화다.<end>"

## 490046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "５０００ギア。<end>"
EN: "Five thousand Gea.<end>"
KO: "5000기어.<end>"

## 490047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "話にならん。<end>"
EN: "Out of the question.<end>"
KO: "말도 안 되는군.<end>"

## 490048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ更に、今までの賭けの俺の勝ち分<line-break>全部チャラ。これでどうだい？<line-break>１００００ギアはいくだろ？<end>"
EN: "Then I'll throw in all my winnings from our past bets. Wipe the slate clean. How's that? It must come to ten thousand Gea.<end>"
KO: "그럼 거기에 더해서 지금까지 내기에서 내가 딴 몫도 전부 없던 걸로 하지. 이 정도면 어때? 10000기어는 되겠지?<end>"

## 490049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふざけるな。お前のイカサマに<line-break>金を払う義務も必要もない。<end>"
EN: "Don't be absurd. I have neither reason nor obligation to pay for your cheating.<end>"
KO: "웃기지 마. 네 사기에 돈을 낼 의무도 필요도 없어.<end>"

## 490050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちぇ。じゃあ、ここでまたひとつ　　<line-break>俺と勝負といこうや。<line-break>イカサマは、なしだ。<line-break>俺が勝ったら今の条件で情報をもらう。<line-break>お前が勝ったら今の条件分、全部<line-break>お前が持って帰っていい。どうだ？<end>"
EN: "Tch. Then let's have one more wager, right here. No cheating. If I win, you give me the information for what I've just offered. If you win, you can take the whole amount home with you. What do you say?<end>"
KO: "쳇. 그럼 여기서 또 한 번 나와 승부하자. 사기는 없다. 내가 이기면 지금 조건대로 정보를 받는다. 네가 이기면 지금 조건에 걸린 걸 전부 네가 가져가도 돼. 어때?<end>"

## 490051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいだろう。<line-break>何で勝負だ？<end>"
EN: "Very well. What are we wagering on?<end>"
KO: "좋아. 뭘로 승부하지?<end>"

## 490052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この酒場に次に来る客は<line-break>男か、女か。これを当てる。<line-break>さあ、どっちだい？<end>"
EN: "We guess whether the next customer through the door is a man or a woman. So, which will it be?<end>"
KO: "이 술집에 다음으로 들어올 손님이 남자인지 여자인지 맞히는 거야. 자, 어느 쪽일까?<end>"

## 490053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "女、だ。<line-break>俺の耳は今、ここに向かって歩く<line-break>女の足音をしっかりととらえている。<end>"
EN: "A woman. I can clearly hear a woman's footsteps approaching this tavern.<end>"
KO: "여자다. 내 귀에는 지금 이쪽으로 걸어오는 여자의 발소리가 똑똑히 들리고 있어.<end>"

## 490054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん、女か。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>この酒場はそこにいるべっぴんさん<line-break>目当ての客が多いらしいんだがな。<line-break>ま、女でいいんだな？<end>"
EN: "Hmm, a woman? I'd heard a lot of this tavern's customers come to see that beauty over there. Still, you're sure about a woman?<end>"
KO: "흐음, 여자라고. 이 술집은 저기 있는 미인을 보러 오는 손님이 많다던데. 뭐, 여자로 하면 되는 거지?<end>"

## 490055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、いや待て。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>……男だ。男でいく。<end>"
EN: "N-no, wait. A man. I'll choose a man.<end>"
KO: "아, 아니 잠깐. ……남자다. 남자로 간다.<end>"

## 490056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった。男だな。<end>"
EN: "All right. A man, then.<end>"
KO: "알겠어. 남자구나.<end>"

## 490057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんにちはフェルムちゃん。<line-break>この前お願いしたお酒、ある？<end>"
EN: "Hello, Ferme. Do you have that drink I asked for the other day?<end>"
KO: "안녕, 페름. 전에 부탁한 술 있어?<end>"

## 490058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、おばあちゃん、こんにちは。<line-break>あのお酒、ありますよ。<line-break>はい、これですよね？<end>"
EN: "Oh, hello, ma'am! Yes, I have it. Here you are. This is the one, right?<end>"
KO: "아, 할머니, 안녕하세요. 그 술 있어요. 네, 이거 맞죠?<end>"

## 490059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ、これこれ。<line-break>うちの人が飲みたがってねえ。<line-break>じゃ、ありがとうね。フェルムちゃん。<end>"
EN: "Yes, that's it. My husband has been wanting some. Thank you, Ferme.<end>"
KO: "그래, 이거야. 우리 양반이 마시고 싶어 해서 말이지. 그럼 고마워, 페름.<end>"

## 490060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。<line-break>また来てくださいね、おばあちゃん。<end>"
