KO: "네. 또 오세요, 할머니.<end>"

## 490061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "女、だったな…。<end>"
EN: "That was a woman.<end>"
KO: "여자였지….<end>"

## 490062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、<line-break>かなり歳をくってはいるが<line-break>女だった…。<line-break>………………。<end>"
EN: "Yes. A very old woman, but a woman nonetheless...<end>"
KO: "그래, 나이는 꽤 들었지만 여자였어…. ……………….<end>"

## 490063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、残念だったな。<line-break>そんじゃ、<line-break>情報、聞かせてもらおうか。<end>"
EN: "Too bad. Now then, let's hear that information.<end>"
KO: "이런, 아쉽군. 그럼 정보를 들려주겠나.<end>"

## 490064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "……ちっ。<line-break>まず、最初のほうだが、あんた、<line-break><value:$28>、だっけ？<line-break>エリスは結構、あんたのことを<line-break>気に入っているようだ。<line-break>殺されることはまずないな。<end>"
EN: "Tch. First, about you. It was <value:$28>, wasn't it? Eris seems rather fond of you. You almost certainly won't be killed.<end>"
KO: "……쳇. 우선 첫 번째인데, 당신 <value:$28> 맞지? 에리스는 꽤 당신을 마음에 들어 하는 모양이야. 죽을 일은 거의 없겠군.<end>"

## 490065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとの方だが、ルルアンタは<line-break>旅芸人の一座にくっついて行った。<line-break>が、これが問題含みだ。<line-break>最近起こっている誘拐事件、あれに<line-break>関わっている盗賊団が、その一座を<line-break>隠れみのに使っている。<end>"
EN: "As for the other matter, Lulluantha tagged along with a traveling troupe. That's where the trouble starts. The bandits behind the recent kidnappings are using the troupe as cover.<end>"
KO: "뒤쪽 이야기인데, 릴비는 떠돌이 공연단을 따라갔어. 그런데 이게 문제야. 요즘 일어나는 유괴 사건에 관련된 도적단이 그 공연단을 은신처로 쓰고 있거든.<end>"

## 490066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まずいな。<line-break>ルルアンタの性格なら、まず確実に<line-break>面倒事に首を突っ込むだろうな。<end>"
EN: "That's bad. Knowing Lulluantha, she's almost certain to stick her nose into the whole mess.<end>"
KO: "큰일이군. 루루안타 성격이라면 틀림없이 골칫거리에 끼어들 거야.<end>"

## 490067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、なら<line-break>生きて再会したければ<line-break>早く追いかけることだな。　　<line-break>その一座はこれから<line-break>リベルダムをまわって<line-break>エンシャントへ向かうそうだ。<end>"
EN: "Then if you want to see her alive again, you'd better hurry after her. I hear the troupe is making the rounds through Liberdam before heading to Ancient.<end>"
KO: "그래, 그렇다면 살아서 다시 만나고 싶으면 빨리 뒤쫓아라. 그 일행은 이제 리벨덤을 거쳐 엔샨트로 향한다더군.<end>"

## 490068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうよ、ツェラシェル。<line-break>ついでに頼めるかな？<end>"
EN: "Thanks, Zeraschel. Mind doing me one more favor?<end>"
KO: "고맙군, 체라셸. 내친김에 하나 더 부탁해도 될까?<end>"

## 490069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あーいい、言うな。<line-break>言おうとしてる事はわかるし、<line-break>断らせないつもりなのもわかる。<line-break>エリスあたりに時間稼ぎして、<line-break>その子に呼び出しがかかるのを<line-break>遅らせればいいんだろ？<end>"
EN: "Oh, spare me. I know what you're about to ask, and I know you have no intention of letting me refuse. You want me to stall Eris and delay that child's summons, right?<end>"
KO: "아, 됐어. 말하지 마. 무슨 말을 하려는지 알고, 거절 못 하게 할 생각인 것도 알아. 에리스한테 시간을 좀 끌게 해서 그 아이에게 소집 명령이 가는 걸 늦추면 되는 거지?<end>"

## 490070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがだな。よろしく頼む。<line-break>感謝してるぜ。<end>"
EN: "I knew I could count on you. Take care of it for me. I appreciate this.<end>"
KO: "역시 대단하군. 잘 부탁한다. 고맙게 생각해.<end>"

## 490071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "感謝より金だ。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>だいたい、こんなこと、<line-break>本当ならお前がやればいいことだ。<end>"
EN: "I'd rather have money than gratitude. Besides, this is something you ought to be doing yourself.<end>"
KO: "감사보다 돈이지. 애초에 이런 일은 원래 네가 했어야 하는 거잖아.<end>"

## 490072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうもいかないのさ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>さて、と。<end>"
EN: "That's not an option. Well, then.<end>"
KO: "그럴 수도 없지. 자, 그럼.<end>"

## 490073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな、お前さんの言うとおり、　<line-break>まずはリベルダムだ。<line-break>急がないと、ルルアンタが危ない。<line-break>…と、その前に旅の支度をしてこい。<line-break>街の門で合流しようぜ。<line-break>じゃあな。<end>"
EN: "You're right. Liberdam first. Lulluantha will be in danger if we don't hurry. But before that, go get ready for the journey. We'll meet at the city gate. See you there.<end>"
KO: "그래, 네 말대로 우선 리벨덤이야. 서두르지 않으면 루루안타가 위험해. …그 전에 여행 준비부터 하고 와. 도시 문에서 합류하자. 그럼 이따 보자.<end>"

## 490074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？<line-break>さっき負けたらどうするつもり<line-break>だったかって？<line-break>なに、さっきの勝負に<line-break>負けは無かったのさ。<end>"
EN: "Hm? What would I have done if I'd lost that wager? Simple. There was no chance of me losing.<end>"
KO: "응? 아까 졌으면 어쩔 생각이었냐고? 뭐, 애초에 아까 승부엔 패배란 없었지.<end>"

## 490075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつの五感はすごいからな。<line-break>はじめに奴が言ったろ？　女って。　　<line-break>それが正解さ。<line-break>あとはそれを撤回するように<line-break>ゆざぶってやればいい。<line-break>やっぱり奴は賭け事に向いていないな。<end>"
EN: "His five senses are extraordinary. Remember what he said first? A woman. That was the right answer. All I had to do was rattle him until he changed it. He really isn't cut out for gambling.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석 오감은 대단하니까. 처음에 그 녀석이 말했지? 여자라고. 그게 정답이야. 이제 그 말을 철회하도록 흔들어 주면 돼. 역시 그 녀석은 도박에 안 맞아.<end>"

## 490090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、どうしたのじゃ。<line-break>港へ行くのではなかったのか？<end>"
EN: "What's the matter, <value:$28>? Weren't you going to the harbor?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 무슨 일이냐. 항구로 가는 게 아니었느냐?<end>"

## 490091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主もじゃ。<line-break>いつまでもこんな所で<line-break>油を売ってていいのか？<end>"
EN: "And what about you? Can you afford to stand around here all day?<end>"
KO: "그대도 마찬가지다. 언제까지 이런 곳에서 빈둥거리고 있어도 되는가?<end>"

## 490092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んー、あ、ああ。<line-break>ま、すぐ行くさ。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Oh, right. I'll be along shortly.<end>"
KO: "음, 아, 그래. 뭐, 금방 갈게.<end>"

## 490093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>か。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>こいつはアンギルダン、<line-break>尻を落ち着けることを知らなくて、<line-break>一生を棒に振った老傭兵だ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. This is Angeerdan, an old mercenary who never learned to sit still and wound up wasting his whole life.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>인가. 이쪽은 앙길단. 엉덩이를 붙이고 사는 법을 몰라 인생을 날린 늙은 용병이지.<end>"

## 490094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハハ、失礼な奴じゃな。<end>"
EN: "Ha! How rude.<end>"
KO: "하하, 무례한 녀석이군.<end>"

## 490095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、事細かに紹介しようか？<line-break>このアンギルダンのとっつぁんはだな。<line-break>　<line-break>昔はバロルのもとでディンガル統一の<line-break>ために戦った名将だ。しかしバロルが<line-break>魔王になると、決別して帝国を出奔。<end>"
EN: "Would you prefer a full introduction, then? Old Angeerdan here was once a renowned commander who fought under Balor to unite Dyneskal. But when Balor became the Demon King, Angeerdan broke with him and fled the Empire.<end>"
KO: "그럼 하나하나 자세히 소개해 줄까? 이 앙길단 영감은 말이지. 예전엔 발로르 휘하에서 딘갈 통일을 위해 싸운 명장이야. 하지만 발로르가 마왕이 되자 결별하고 제국을 떠났지.<end>"

## 490096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その後、世界中を傭兵として<line-break>渡り歩いたが、<line-break>結局、どこにも落ち着かずじまい。<end>"
EN: "After that, he roamed the world as a mercenary and never did settle down anywhere.<end>"
KO: "그 뒤 세계 곳곳을 용병으로 떠돌았지만 결국 어디에도 정착하지 못했어.<end>"

## 490097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔王バロルが倒されたあと、<line-break>実力と声望を買われて<line-break>帝国の将軍に返り咲いたんだが…。<line-break>今度は皇帝エリュマルクの<line-break>ネメアへのいじめじみた振る舞いを<line-break>バカ正直に堂々と批判。<end>"
EN: "After Balor, Demon King, was defeated, Angeerdan's skill and reputation won him a return to the Empire as a general. But then he had the foolhardy honesty to openly criticize Emperor Elmark's bullying treatment of Nemea.<end>"
KO: "마왕 발로르가 쓰러진 뒤 실력과 명망을 인정받아 제국 장군으로 복귀했지만…. 이번엔 황제 에류마르크가 네메아를 괴롭히듯 행동하는 걸 고지식하게 정면으로 비판했지.<end>"

## 490098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んで皇帝の反感を買って<line-break>処刑されそうになってるところを脱走。<line-break>今や、お尋ね者のご老体だ。<line-break>な？　こんな風に長々と語るより、<line-break>落ち着きがなくて、一生を棒に振った<line-break>老傭兵っつった方がわかりやすいだろ？<end>"
EN: "That put him on the emperor's bad side, so when they were about to execute him, he escaped. Now the old man's a wanted fugitive. See? Isn't it clearer just to call him an old mercenary who could never sit still and squandered his whole life, instead of dragging out the whole story?<end>"
KO: "그러다 황제의 미움을 사서 처형될 뻔하다 탈주. 지금은 수배자 신세인 노인이지. 봐, 이렇게 길게 말하는 것보다 성질 급하게 살다 인생 말아먹은 늙은 용병이라고 하는 게 이해하기 쉽잖아?<end>"

## 490099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっはっはっ、<line-break>それはお主の説明の仕方が悪いんじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha! Only because you tell it so poorly.<end>"
KO: "하하하, 그건 네 설명 방식이 나빴던 게야.<end>"

## 490100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしはアンギルダン。<line-break><value:$28>、今後よろしく頼む。<end>"
EN: "I'm Angeerdan. A pleasure to meet you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "나는 앙길단이다. <value:$28>, 앞으로 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 490101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、ゼネテスとも話しておったのだが…<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>そのルルアンタとかいう　　　　　　　<line-break>リルビーの踊り子らしい少女は、<line-break>確かに、その一座の中におったぞ。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes and I were just discussing this. That girl called Lulluantha, apparently a Lilubee dancer, was definitely with the troupe.<end>"
KO: "방금 제네테스와도 이야기하고 있었는데… 그 루루안타라는 릴비 무희로 보이는 소녀는 확실히 그 일행 안에 있었다.<end>"

## 490102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、もう、エンシャントへ<line-break>行ってしもうた。<line-break>　<line-break>先に言うておいてくれたら、<line-break>わしが奴らを<line-break>ふん縛っておいたものを。<end>"
EN: "But they've already left for Ancient. If only you'd told me sooner, I would have tied the lot of them up.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이미 엔샨트로 가 버렸어. 미리 말해 줬다면 내가 녀석들을 꽁꽁 묶어 뒀을 텐데.<end>"

## 490103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスから事情は聞いた。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>お主が追いかけて、<line-break>お主がそのリルビーを<line-break>救ってやるのじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes told me what happened. Go after them and save that Lilubee girl.<end>"
KO: "제네테스에게 사정은 들었다. 네가 쫓아가서 그 릴비를 구해 주는 거다.<end>"

## 490104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあ、とっつぁん。　<end>"
EN: "Say, old man.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 아저씨.<end>"

## 490105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴らは馬脚を現すようなことは<line-break>なかったんだな。<end>"
EN: "They never let the mask slip, did they?<end>"
KO: "녀석들이 본색을 드러낼 만한 짓은 없었던 거군.<end>"

## 490106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うむ。<line-break>怪しい素振りはまったくなかった。<line-break>　<line-break>あったら、わしが成敗しておるわい。<line-break>はっはっは！<end>"
EN: "No. They did nothing suspicious at all. Had they tried, I would have dealt with them myself. Ha ha ha!<end>"
KO: "음. 수상한 움직임은 전혀 없었다. 있었다면 내가 벌써 처단했지. 하하하!<end>"

## 490107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…てことは、まだ、ルルアンタは<line-break>奴らの正体に気づいていないんだな。<end>"
EN: "Then Lulluantha still doesn't know who they really are.<end>"
KO: "…그 말은 아직 루루안타가 놈들의 정체를 눈치채지 못했다는 거군.<end>"

## 490108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな。エンシャントへ行こう。<line-break>急げば間に合う。<line-break>　<line-break>準備ができたら、<line-break>港へ行って、乗船しよう。<line-break>俺も港にすぐに行く。<end>"
EN: "Right. Let's go to Ancient. If we hurry, we can still catch them. Once you're ready, head to the harbor and we'll board the ship. I'll be at the harbor shortly.<end>"
KO: "그래. 엔샨트로 가자. 서두르면 맞출 수 있어. 준비되면 항구로 가서 배를 타자. 나도 곧 항구로 갈게.<end>"

## 490109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺もすぐに行く。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>お前さんも準備ができ次第、<line-break>港に向かってくれ。<end>"
EN: "I'll head there now. Come to the harbor as soon as you're ready.<end>"
KO: "나도 곧 갈게. 너도 준비되는 대로 항구로 와 줘.<end>"

## 490110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "時間的には、一歩違いって奴だな。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>どうした？　シルヴァ村で、<line-break>あのノエルとかいう子を助けたこと、<line-break>後悔しているのか？<end>"
EN: "We're only one step behind them. What's wrong? Do you regret saving that girl Noel in Silva Village?<end>"
KO: "시간상으로는 한발 차이였군. 왜 그래? 실바 마을에서 그 노엘이라는 애를 도운 걸 후회하나?<end>"

## 490111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "後悔している<end>"
EN: "Have regrets<end>"
KO: "후회하고 있다<end>"

## 490112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "後悔していない<end>"
EN: "Have no regrets<end>"
KO: "후회하지 않는다<end>"

## 490113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そりゃ、残念だな。<end>"
EN: "That's a shame.<end>"
KO: "그거 참 아쉽군.<end>"

## 490114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それに、たとえ後悔するにしたって<line-break>まだ早すぎると思うぜ。<end>"
EN: "Besides, even if you're going to regret it, it's far too soon for that.<end>"
KO: "그리고 설령 후회하게 된다 해도 아직 너무 이르다고 생각해.<end>"

## 490115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急げばエンシャントで<line-break>まだ、追いつける。違うか？<end>"
EN: "Hurry, and there's still time to catch up in Ancient. Am I wrong?<end>"
KO: "서두르면 엔샨트에서 아직 따라잡을 수 있어. 그렇지?<end>"

## 490116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ。<value:$28>、<line-break>お前ならそういうと思ってた。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お前の判断が間違っていなかったって、<line-break>運命の女神に見せてやろうぜ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. I knew you'd say that, <value:$28>. Let's show the Goddess of Fate that you made the right choice.<end>"
KO: "후후. <value:$28>, 너라면 그렇게 말할 줄 알았어. 네 판단이 틀리지 않았다는 걸 운명의 여신에게 보여 주자고.<end>"

## 490117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エンシャントへ行って<line-break>ルルアンタを助け出して、な。　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Go to Ancient and rescue Lulluantha, all right?<end>"
KO: "엔샨트로 가서 루루안타를 구해 줘.<end>"

## 490118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺もすぐに行く。<end>"
EN: "I'll be there soon.<end>"
KO: "나도 곧 갈게.<end>"

## 490119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんも準備ができ次第、<line-break>港に向かってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Once you're ready, head for the harbor.<end>"
KO: "너도 준비가 되는 대로 항구로 가 줘.<end>"

## 490120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "成功を祈っておるぞ！<end>"
EN: "I'll be praying for your success!<end>"
KO: "성공을 빌고 있겠다!<end>"

## 490121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来たか。<line-break>待ってたぜ<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "There you are. I've been waiting for you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "왔군. 기다렸어, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 490122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょうどいいタイミングだ。<end>"
EN: "Your timing couldn't be better.<end>"
KO: "딱 좋은 타이밍이군.<end>"

## 490123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "例の旅芸人の一座は、どうやら<line-break>もうリベルダムを出たようだな。<line-break>急がないとルルアンタが危ないか…。<end>"
EN: "It seems that traveling troupe has already left Liberdam. If we don't hurry, Lulluantha could be in danger.<end>"
KO: "그 유랑극단은 아무래도 벌써 리벨덤을 떠난 모양이군. 서두르지 않으면 루루안타가 위험하겠어….<end>"

## 490124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、お前さんの言う通りだ。<line-break>急いで内海を渡って<line-break>エンシャントへ行こう。<end>"
EN: "You're right. Let's hurry across the Inland Sea to Ancient.<end>"
KO: "그래, 네 말이 맞아. 서둘러 내해를 건너 엔샨트로 가자.<end>"

## 490125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう船番に話はつけてある。<line-break>声をかければ乗船させてくれるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "I've already made arrangements with the boat keeper. Just ask, and you should be allowed aboard.<end>"
KO: "이미 뱃사공과 이야기는 해 뒀다. 말을 걸면 배에 태워 줄 거야.<end>"

## 490126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうした？　船番が恐いのか？<line-break>大丈夫だ。なりは大きいが話は通じる。<line-break>いきなりとって食われたりはしないさ。<end>"
EN: "What's wrong? Afraid of the boat keeper? Don't worry. The boat keeper may be huge, but is reasonable enough. You won't just get gobbled up.<end>"
KO: "왜 그래? 배 지키는 녀석이 무서워? 괜찮아. 덩치는 커도 말은 통하거든. 다짜고짜 잡아먹진 않아.<end>"

## 490127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あの男の連れか？<line-break>…チッ、乗んな。<end>"
EN: "You with that man? Tch. Get aboard.<end>"
KO: "그 남자의 동료냐? …쳇, 타.<end>"

## 490128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやあ、悪いな。<line-break>じゃ、よろしく頼むわ。<end>"
EN: "Sorry about this. I'll leave it to you, then.<end>"
KO: "아, 미안하네. 그럼 잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 490129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うるせえ。<line-break>賭けの負けは負けだ。<line-break>とっとと乗れ。<end>"
EN: "Shut up. A lost bet's a lost bet. Now get aboard.<end>"
KO: "시끄러워. 내기에서 진 건 진 거야. 얼른 타.<end>"

## 490130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　ああ、いや、さっきな。<line-break>この男前と軽く勝負したんだよ。<line-break>船賃を賭けてな。<end>"
EN: "Hm? Oh, that. This handsome fellow and I had a little contest earlier. We wagered the fare.<end>"
KO: "응? 아, 아니, 방금 이 잘생긴 남자와 가볍게 승부했어. 뱃삯을 걸고 말이지.<end>"

## 490131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあまあ、細かいこといいなさんな。<line-break>ほら、早くしないと船が出ちまうぜ。<end>"
EN: "Now, now, don't fuss over the details. Come on, the boat will leave without us if we don't hurry.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 사소한 건 됐어. 자, 빨리 안 가면 배가 떠나 버린다고.<end>"

## 490139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、と。着いたな。　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>帝都エンシャント。<line-break>ディンガル帝国の首都。<line-break>大陸北部最大の都市だ。<end>"
EN: "Well, we're here. Ancient, the Imperial Capital. It's the capital of the Dyneskal Empire and the largest city in the northern part of the continent.<end>"
KO: "자, 도착했군. 제도 엔샨트. 딘갈 제국의 수도이자 대륙 북부 최대 도시다.<end>"

## 490140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、情報収集だ。<line-break>またあとでおちあおうぜ。<end>"
EN: "Time to gather information. Let's meet up again later.<end>"
KO: "자, 정보 수집이다. 나중에 다시 만나자.<end>"

## 490141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、旅芸人の一座だろ。来てたよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you mean the traveling performers? Yes, they were here.<end>"
KO: "아아, 유랑 예인단 말이지. 왔었어.<end>"

## 490142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、リルビーの女の子の踊り子？<line-break>見なかったようだが…。<end>"
EN: "What, a little Lilubee girl who dances? Can't say I saw her...<end>"
KO: "뭐, 릴비 여자아이 무희? 못 본 것 같은데….<end>"

## 490143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>、つけられたな。<end>"
EN: "...<value:$28>, we've been followed.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, 미행당했군.<end>"

## 490144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…連中はまだエンシャントにいるんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ま、これからは、あんまり大きい声で<line-break>聞きまわらないようにしなくちゃ、な。<end>"
EN: "So they're still in Ancient. Well, we'd better stop asking questions where everyone can hear us.<end>"
KO: "…놈들은 아직 엔샨트에 있어. 뭐, 이제부터는 너무 큰 소리로 캐묻지 않도록 해야겠지.<end>"

## 490145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様ら、何をかぎまわっている？<end>"
EN: "What are you snooping around for?<end>"
KO: "네놈들, 뭘 캐고 다니는 거냐?<end>"

## 490146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まず、旅の一座に隠れみのにしている　<line-break>誘拐組織のこと。<line-break>　<line-break>そして<line-break>その秘密を知って悪者に追われている、<line-break>かわいいリルビーの女の子のことさ。<end>"
EN: "First, a kidnapping ring using a troupe of traveling performers as a front. Then there's the cute little Lilubee girl who learned their secret and now has the villains after her.<end>"
KO: "먼저 여행 악단을 위장막으로 삼은 유괴 조직 이야기. 그리고 그 비밀을 알고 악당들에게 쫓기는 귀여운 릴비 여자아이 이야기지.<end>"

## 490147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけ事が悟られていては<line-break>死んでもらうしかないな。<end>"
EN: "If you know that much, we'll have to kill you.<end>"
KO: "그 정도로 사정을 알아 버렸다면 죽어 줘야겠군.<end>"

## 490148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せっかく向こうさんから<line-break>おいでくださったんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>とっ捕まえて<line-break>お話を伺おうぜ、<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "They were kind enough to come to us. Let's catch them and ask a few questions, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "모처럼 저쪽에서 와 주셨잖아. 붙잡아서 이야기를 들어 보자고, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 490149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うう…、まさか、この俺が…。<end>"
EN: "Ugh... Me, of all people...<end>"
KO: "으으…, 설마 이 내가….<end>"

## 490150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、と。<line-break>ルルアンタはどうした？<end>"
EN: "Now, then. What happened to Lulluantha?<end>"

## 490151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひ、秘密を知られたんで<line-break>け、賢者の森で<line-break>仲間が始末することになっている。<end>"
EN: "S-she found out our secret, s-so our people in Sage's Forest are going to dispose of her.<end>"

## 490152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前らザコを<line-break>どうこうしたってはじまらん。<line-break>　<line-break>縁があったら、<line-break>お前らのボスの所に行って、<line-break>きっちりあいさつさせてもらう。<end>"

## 490153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "消えろ！　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Get lost!<end>"
KO: "꺼져!<end>"

## 490154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな。賢者の森へ急ごう。<end>"
EN: "Right. Let's hurry to Sage's Forest.<end>"
KO: "그래. 현자의 숲으로 서두르자.<end>"

## 490155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、どんなぐあいだ？<end>"

KO: "여어, 상태는 어때?<end>"

## 490156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の方はらちがあかないんで、<line-break>ちょっと、連中をおびき寄せた。<end>"

KO: "내 쪽은 진전이 없어서 녀석들을 조금 유인해 냈어.<end>"

## 490157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "せっかく、向こうさんから<line-break>おいでくださったんでな。<line-break>　<line-break>こいつらを叩きのめして、<line-break>お話を伺おうと思うわけだ。<line-break>手伝ってくれ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "They were kind enough to come to us. I thought we'd beat them senseless and ask a few questions. Give me a hand, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "모처럼 저쪽에서 직접 찾아와 주셨으니 말이야. 이놈들을 때려눕히고 이야기를 들어보려고 한다. 도와줘, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 490158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あるものさ。何気なく…<line-break>本当に何気なくしたことが…　<line-break>　<line-break>もう二度と<line-break>取り返しの着かないことに、<line-break>なってしまうこと、てのは…。<end>"
EN: "It happens. Something you do without thinking―truly without giving it a thought―can turn into something that can never be undone...<end>"
KO: "그런 일이 있지. 아무 생각 없이… 정말 무심코 한 일이… 다시는 되돌릴 수 없는 일이 되어 버리는 것….<end>"

## 490159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、いいのか？<end>"
EN: "Are you finished?<end>"
KO: "이제 괜찮아?<end>"

## 490160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな…葬って…やろう。<end>"
EN: "Yeah... Let's... give a proper burial.<end>"
KO: "그래… 묻어… 주자.<end>"

## 490161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このまま、ルルアンタを<line-break>放ってくのか？<end>"

KO: "이대로 루루안타를 내버려둘 거야?<end>"

## 490162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだな…葬ってやるべきだ。<end>"
EN: "Yeah... A proper burial is only right.<end>"
KO: "그래… 묻어 줘야겠어.<end>"

## 490163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ、ドジっちまったぜ。<line-break>ここまで連れてきて<line-break>逃げられるとはな。<end>"

KO: "쳇, 실수했군. 여기까지 데려와 놓고 도망치게 하다니.<end>"

## 490164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、リルビーのガキひとり、<line-break>こんな森の中で迷ったら、<line-break>モンスターに食われておしまいさ。<end>"
EN: "Still, one little Lilubee brat lost in a forest like this will end up monster food.<end>"
KO: "하지만 릴비 꼬맹이 혼자 이런 숲에서 길을 잃으면 몬스터한테 먹혀 끝장이야.<end>"

## 490165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、だが<line-break>念には念をいれとかねえとな。<line-break>　<line-break>…誰だ！<end>"
EN: "Yeah, but we'd better make sure. Who's there?!<end>"
KO: "그래, 하지만 만전을 기해야지. …누구냐!<end>"

## 490166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何！？　お前ら、帝都で俺たちを<line-break>かぎまわってた奴らか！<line-break>ちっ、向こうもドジりやがったのか。<end>"

KO: "뭐!? 너희, 제도에서 우리를 캐고 다니던 녀석들이냐! 쳇, 저쪽도 실수한 모양이군.<end>"

## 490167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、そんなとこだな。<line-break>さて、俺たちの用件だ。<line-break>ルルアンタはどうした？<end>"
EN: "Something like that. Now, down to business. What happened to Lulluantha?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 그런 셈이지. 자, 이제 우리 용건이다. 루루안타는 어떻게 됐지?<end>"

## 490168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁな。<line-break>モンスターにでも食われてて<line-break>くれるといいんだがな。<end>"
EN: "Who knows? I hope some monster's eaten her by now.<end>"
KO: "글쎄. 몬스터한테라도 잡아먹혔으면 좋겠는데.<end>"

## 490169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫。<line-break>ちゃんとお前らもここで殺して<line-break>心配なんかしなくてよくしてやるぜ！<end>"

KO: "괜찮아. 너희도 여기서 확실히 끝내서걱정할 일 없게 해 줄게!<end>"

## 490170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなに気を遣うなよ。<line-break>こっちはこっちで<line-break>好きなようにさせてもらうからよ。<end>"
EN: "No need to go to all that trouble. We'll just do as we please.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 신경 쓰지 마. 우리는 우리대로 하고 싶은 대로 할 테니까.<end>"

## 490171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Though it looks like that won't satisfy you.<end>"
KO: "…라고 해도 그쪽은 납득 못 하는 모양이군.<end>"

## 490172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺たちの正体を知った奴は<line-break>誰だろうとぶっ殺す！<line-break>野郎ども、いくぜ！！<end>"
EN: "Anyone who learns who we really are gets butchered! Come on, boys!<end>"
KO: "우리 정체를 안 녀석은 누구든 죽여 버린다! 얘들아, 간다!!<end>"

## 490173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちっ、おぼえてやがれ！<end>"
EN: "Damn it! Remember this!<end>"
KO: "쳇, 두고 보자!<end>"

## 490174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、ここは結界を張っています。<end>"
EN: "Heh. A barrier surrounds this place.<end>"
KO: "후후, 여기는 결계를 쳐 두었습니다.<end>"

## 490177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "運命が選んだ人だけが、<line-break>結界を抜けてここに入ってくるのです。<end>"
EN: "Only those chosen by fate can pass through the barrier and enter.<end>"
KO: "운명이 선택한 사람만 결계를 뚫고 이곳에 들어옵니다.<end>"

## 490178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですから、あなたほどの冒険者でも<line-break>ここに来たことがないんですよ。<end>"
EN: "That is why even an adventurer of your caliber has never been here before.<end>"
KO: "그러니 당신 정도의 모험자라도 여기에 온 적은 없는 겁니다.<end>"

## 490179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いらっしゃい。<line-break>お待ちしていましたよ。<end>"

KO: "어서 오세요. 기다리고 있었습니다.<end>"

## 490180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私はオルファウス。<line-break>この屋敷の主です。<end>"

## 490181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490183

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml

## 490186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "루루안타 씨라면 안에 계십니다.<end>"

## 490187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いきなり質問攻めですか？<end>"

## 490188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、ここで立ち話もなんですし、<line-break>中でお茶にしませんか？<end>"

KO: "후후, 여기서 서서 이야기하기도 뭐하니 안에서 차라도 마실까요?<end>"

## 490189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うんにゃ。信用していい。<end>"
EN: "Nah. We can trust him.<end>"
KO: "아니. 믿어도 돼.<end>"

## 490190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつには俺たち２人まとめても、<line-break>息も切らさずに始末できる実力がある。<end>"
EN: "He could finish both of us without even breaking a sweat.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석은 우리 둘을 한꺼번에 상대해도 숨 하나 차지 않고 처리할 실력이 있어.<end>"

## 490191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "殺す気なら、おびき寄せる必要もない。<line-break>俺たちの敵じゃないと思っていい。<end>"
EN: "If he wanted us dead, he wouldn't need to lure us in. We can safely assume he's not our enemy.<end>"
KO: "죽일 생각이었다면 유인할 필요도 없어. 우리 적은 아니라고 봐도 돼.<end>"

## 490192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…うーん。　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>実は俺たちを捕まえて、ゆっくり<line-break>いたぶって殺すのがシュミな奴、<line-break>という可能性も捨てがたいな。<end>"
EN: "Hmm... Though we can't entirely rule out the possibility that he enjoys capturing people, torturing them at leisure, and then killing them.<end>"

## 490193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談だ。あいつの噂なら知っている。<line-break>あいつはそんな奴じゃない。<line-break>　<line-break>それにな。俺のカンが<line-break>大丈夫だって言っている。<line-break>だから、絶対大丈夫だ。<end>"
EN: "I'm joking. I've heard the stories about him, and he's not like that. Besides, my instincts say it's safe. So it's definitely safe.<end>"

## 490194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者にとって、カンってのは<line-break>結構、大事なんだぜ。<line-break>　<line-break>それに、冒険者は自分を一番に<line-break>信用できないと、な。<line-break>いくぞ。<end>"
EN: "Instinct counts for a lot when you're an adventurer. And an adventurer has to trust himself above anyone else. Come on.<end>"

## 490195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、ルルアンタを追って<line-break>こんな所まで来てくれたんだ…。<end>"

## 490198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタこそ、謝らなきゃ。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ごめんね、<value:$28>。<line-break>フリントさんが死んで、<line-break>一番つらいときにいなくなっちゃって。<end>"

## 490200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490201

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490205
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっちもいろいろ事情があってな。<end>"

## 490209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そっちもいろいろあるんだろうが、<line-break>悪いが、明日にしてくれないか。<end>"

KO: "그쪽도 여러 사정이 있겠지만 미안하다. 내일로 해 주지 않겠나.<end>"

## 490210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、そのようですね。<line-break>わかりました。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>今日はお疲れのようですし、<line-break>久々の再会で話すことも<line-break>たくさんあるでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Heh. It would seem so. Very well. You look tired, and after so long apart, I'm sure you have much to discuss.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그런 것 같군요. 알겠습니다. 오늘은 피곤해 보이시고, 오랜만의 재회라 할 이야기도 많으시겠죠.<end>"

## 490211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I'll cook you the best dinner I can, so stay and get some rest.<end>"
KO: "솜씨를 발휘해서 저녁을 만들 테니 푹 쉬다 가세요.<end>"

## 490212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "오르파우스 씨, 좋은 아침입니다!!<end>"

## 490213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おはよう、　　　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>、ルルアンタ。<line-break>　<line-break>ルルアンタは本当に元気ですね。<end>"

KO: "좋은 아침, <value:$28>, 루루안타. 루루안타는 정말 활기차네요.<end>"

## 490214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ。ありがとうございます！　　　<line-break>あ、ねえねえ、<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>ルルアンタがこの森で<line-break>悪い人たちから逃げてね。道に迷って、<line-break>モンスターに襲われて困ってたとき、<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Thank you! Oh, listen, <value:$28>. Lulluantha was running away from some bad people in this forest, and then she got lost and monsters attacked her, and she didn't know what to do, but―<end>"
KO: "후후. 감사합니다! 아, 있잖아 <value:$28>. 릴비가 이 숲에서 나쁜 사람들에게서 도망치다가 길을 잃고 몬스터에게 습격당해 곤란해했을 때,<end>"

## 490215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "오르파우스 씨가 구해 줬어. <value:$28>도 고맙다고 해 줘.<end>"

## 490216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml

## 490217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml

## 490218

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスとゼネテスと<line-break>朝まで飲んでて、このザマだ。<end>"
EN: "I was drinking with Orphaus and Xenetes until dawn, and now look at me.<end>"
KO: "오르파우스랑 제네테스랑 아침까지 마시다가 이 꼴이 됐어.<end>"

## 490225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、心配は要りません。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>昔、勇者ネメアに倒されてからは<line-break>猫の姿に封印されて、<line-break>今はなんの力も残っていませんから。<end>"
EN: "Heh. There's no need to worry. Ever since the Hero Nemea defeated him long ago, he's been sealed in the form of a cat. He has no power left now.<end>"
KO: "후후, 걱정할 필요 없습니다. 예전에 용자 네메아에게 쓰러진 뒤 고양이 모습에 봉인되어 지금은 아무 힘도 남지 않았으니까요.<end>"

## 490226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、さすがは<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士ですよね。<line-break>　<line-break>猫の姿になってなお<line-break>人語を操るんですから。<end>"
EN: "Still, he is a Knight of the Round Table of the God of Destruction. Even in the form of a cat, he can speak the human tongue.<end>"
KO: "그래도 역시 파괴신의 원탁기사답네요. 고양이 모습이 되고도 사람 말을 하니까요.<end>"

## 490227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、このバカ猫はほっといて、　　<line-break>説明したいものがあります。<line-break>こちらに来てください。<end>"
EN: "Now, never mind this stupid cat. There's something I'd like to explain. Come this way.<end>"
KO: "자, 이 바보 고양이는 내버려 두고 설명할 게 있습니다. 이쪽으로 오세요.<end>"

## 490228

## 490229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれだけ飲めば、仕方ないですかね。<line-break>私ですら多少厳しいんですから…。<line-break>　<line-break>ま、こんな感じで、<line-break>これは遠くの人を呼び寄せたり…。<end>"
EN: "After that much drinking, I suppose this is only to be expected. Even I'm feeling it a little... Anyway, as you can see, this can summon people from far away...<end>"
KO: "그만큼 마셨으니 어쩔 수 없겠죠. 저도 조금 버거우니까요…. 뭐, 이런 식으로 이건 멀리 있는 사람을 불러오기도 하고….<end>"

## 490231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな感じで、ここにいる人を<line-break>遠くに送り込んだりできます。<end>"
EN: "And it can send someone here to a distant location, like so.<end>"
KO: "이런 식으로 여기 있는 사람을 먼 곳으로 보내기도 할 수 있습니다.<end>"

## 490232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごいすごーい！<end>"
EN: "Amazing!<end>"
KO: "대단해, 대단해~!<end>"

## 490233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが将来、旅で人と出会い、<line-break>仲間を増やしたとき、<line-break>　<line-break>一緒に旅する仲間を<line-break>変更をしたいと思ったとき<line-break>ここに来てください。<end>"
EN: "In the future, as you meet people on your travels and gain more companions, come here whenever you wish to change who travels with you.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 여행 중 사람을 만나 동료가 늘고, 함께 여행하는 동료를 바꾸고 싶어지면 여기로 오세요.<end>"

## 490234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、今後は、パーティーメンバーの<line-break>了解を取ってから呼び出しますから<line-break>こんな無様なことはありませんよ。<line-break>それより、<line-break>用意してあった朝ごはんは<line-break>食べていただけましたか。<end>"
EN: "In future, I'll obtain your party members' consent before summoning them, so there will be no repeat of this embarrassing spectacle. More importantly, did you eat the breakfast I had prepared?<end>"
KO: "아, 앞으로는 파티 멤버들의 동의를 받은 다음에 불러낼 테니 이런 꼴은 없을 겁니다. 그보다 준비해 둔 아침 식사는 드셨습니까?<end>"

## 490235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490236

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490239

## 490241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朝まで飲んで寝てるところを<line-break>転送機の実演に使われたんだからな。<end>"

## 490242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml

## 490243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 490245

## 490246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがは、剣狼のゼネテスですね。<line-break>噂通りの冒険者だ。耳が早い。<line-break>　<line-break>そうです。<line-break>ことはミイスを焼き払った魔人<line-break>アーギルシャイアに関わります。<end>"
EN: "Just what I'd expect from Xenetes the Sword Wolf. You're every bit the adventurer the rumors say, and you have a keen ear for news. Yes. This concerns Argyleshire, the demon who burned Miis.<end>"
KO: "과연 검랑 제네테스군요. 소문대로의 모험자입니다. 귀가 빠르군요. 그렇습니다. 미이스를 불태운 마인 아르길샤이어와 관련된 일입니다.<end>"

## 490247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Argyleshire...?<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어…?<end>"

## 490248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士と呼ばれる　<line-break>強力な魔人の１人です。<line-break>　<line-break>妖艶な美女で、残酷で気まぐれです。<line-break>闇の神器をひとつ手に入れるために<line-break>ミイスを焼き払ってしまいました。<end>"
EN: "She is one of the powerful demons known as the Knights of the Round Table of Ulugh, the God of Destruction. A bewitching beauty, she is cruel and capricious. She burned Miis to the ground to obtain one of the Artifacts of Darkness.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사라 불리는 강력한 마인 중 한 명입니다. 요염한 미녀이며 잔혹하고 변덕스럽습니다. 어둠의 신기 하나를 얻기 위해 미이스를 불태웠습니다.<end>"

## 490249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器？<end>"
EN: "An Artifact of Darkness?<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기?<end>"

## 490250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強大で凶々しい力を持つ　　<line-break>１２の魔道器だ。<line-break>かつて、破壊神ウルグの<line-break>復活に使われたことがある。<line-break>…ってな感じだっけ？<end>"
EN: "Twelve magical artifacts possessing immense and sinister power. They were once used to resurrect Ulugh, the God of Destruction. Something like that, right?<end>"
KO: "강대하고 흉악한 힘을 지닌 12개의 마도기다. 예전에 파괴신 울그를 부활시키는 데 쓰였다고 했던가?<end>"

## 490251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "正解です。<line-break>よく、ご存じですね。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>ただ、彼女は<line-break>その本来の使い方以外のことを<line-break>しようとしています。<end>"
EN: "Correct. You are well informed. But she intends to use one for something other than its original purpose.<end>"
KO: "정답입니다. 잘 알고 계시네요. 다만 그녀는 그 본래의 쓰임새 이외의 일을 하려 하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 490252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女は究極の合成怪物を作り<line-break>その力でおもしろ半分に世界を　　　<line-break>破壊しようと考えています。<line-break>その合成怪物を作るための知識を<line-break>闇の神器のひとつ、禁断の聖杯から<line-break>得ようとしているのです。<end>"
EN: "She plans to create the ultimate composite monster and use its power to destroy the world for her own amusement. She hopes to obtain the knowledge needed to create it from one of the Artifacts of Darkness: the Forbidden Grail.<end>"
KO: "그녀는 궁극의 합성괴물을 만들어 그 힘으로 재미 삼아 세계를 파괴하려 하고 있습니다. 그 합성괴물을 만들 지식을 어둠의 신기 중 하나인 금단의 성배에서 얻으려 하는 겁니다.<end>"

## 490253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど、ね。　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>それで、その禁断の聖杯が<line-break>アーギルシャイアの手に落ちる前に<line-break>俺たちに取ってきてほしいってわけか。<end>"
EN: "Now I understand. You want us to retrieve the Forbidden Grail before it falls into Argyleshire's hands.<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 그래서 그 금단의 성배가 아르길샤이어 손에 들어가기 전에 우리가 가져와 달라는 거구나.<end>"

## 490254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、聖杯って名前の魔道器が<line-break>盗み出される事件が<line-break>最近ロストールであったんだが…　　　<line-break>もし、それが禁断の聖杯だったら、<line-break>もう、聖杯はアーギルシャイアの<line-break>手に渡ってるんじゃないか。<end>"
EN: "But a magical artifact called a Grail was recently stolen in Rostorl. If that was the Forbidden Grail, wouldn't it already be in Argyleshire's hands?<end>"
KO: "그런데 최근 로스톨에서 성배라는 이름의 마도기가 도난당하는 사건이 있었어…. 만약 그게 금단의 성배였다면 벌써 아르길샤이어 손에 들어간 거 아닐까.<end>"

## 490255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがに鋭いですね。<line-break>その通り。<line-break>その聖杯こそ禁断の聖杯です。<line-break>ですが、それを盗み出させたのは<line-break>魔人アーギルシャイアではない<line-break>別の魔人なのです。<end>"
EN: "Very perceptive. You're right: that Grail is the Forbidden Grail. However, it was not Argyleshire who arranged the theft, but another demon.<end>"
KO: "역시 날카롭군요. 맞습니다. 그 성배가 바로 금단의 성배입니다. 하지만 그걸 훔치게 한 건 마인 아르길샤이어가 아니라 다른 마인입니다.<end>"

## 490256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、誰なのぉ？<end>"
EN: "Then who was it?<end>"
KO: "그럼, 누구야아?<end>"

## 490257
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の円卓騎士ネモ。<line-break>…彼ですよ。　<line-break>もともと聖杯は彼のものなのです。　　<line-break>アーギルシャイアの企みを知った彼は<line-break>自分の神器を奪われたくないと考え、<line-break>この際、神器の力でもとの姿に戻ろうと<end>"
EN: "Nemo, Knight of the Round Table of the God of Destruction. That cat. The Grail was originally his. Once he learned of Argyleshire's scheme, he decided he wouldn't let her take his artifact―and that he might as well use its power to restore his true form.<end>"
KO: "파괴신의 원탁기사 네모…. 그입니다. 원래 성배는 그의 것이죠. 아르길샤이어의 계획을 안 그는 자기 신기를 빼앗기기 싫어했고, 이참에 신기의 힘으로 원래 모습으로 돌아가려고….<end>"

## 490258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "３匹のゴブリンたちを使って<line-break>ロストールから神器を盗み出させ、<line-break>自分の所に持ってこさせたのです。　　<end>"
EN: "So he had three Goblins steal the artifact from Rostorl and bring it to him.<end>"
KO: "세 마리 고블린을 이용해 로스톨에서 신기를 훔쳐 자기에게 가져오게 한 겁니다.<end>"

## 490259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、ネモは猫のままだったよぉ？<end>"
EN: "But Nemo was still a cat!<end>"
KO: "그런데 네모는 고양이 그대로였어?<end>"

## 490260
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖杯は持ち主に英知を与えます。<end>"
EN: "The Forbidden Grail grants wisdom to whoever possesses it.<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배는 소유자에게 지혜를 줍니다.<end>"

## 490261
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知恵を身につけたゴブリンたちは　　　<line-break>ネモを見限ってしまったのです。<end>"
EN: "Once the Goblins gained wisdom, they abandoned Nemo.<end>"
KO: "지혜를 얻은 고블린들은 네모를 버리고 말았습니다.<end>"

## 490262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かわいそぉ！<end>"
EN: "Poor Nemo!<end>"
KO: "불쌍해애!<end>"

## 490263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、おかげで、ロストールは<line-break>魔人に焼き払われずにすんだわけだ。<end>"
EN: "Well, thanks to that, Rostorl escaped being burned to the ground by a demon.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 덕분에 로스톨은 마인에게 불타 없어지는 일을 피했지.<end>"

## 490264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、アーギルシャイアは、<line-break>聖杯を手に入れようとしてるんでしょ？<line-break>　<line-break>聖杯を持ってるゴブリンがいる町ごと<line-break>ミイスみたいに焼き払っちゃうかも<line-break>しれないんだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "But Argyleshire is still after the Grail, right? She might burn down the whole town where the Goblins have it, just like she did Miis.<end>"
KO: "그런데 아르길샤이어는 성배를 손에 넣으려는 거지? 성배를 가진 고블린이 있는 마을째로 미이스처럼 불태워 버릴지도 몰라.<end>"

## 490265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急がないとな。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "We'd better hurry, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "서둘러야겠군. <value:$28>.<end>"

## 490266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっと、そのゴブリンの特徴とか<line-break>行き先の心当たりとかないんですかぁ？<end>"
EN: "Um, do you know what those Goblins look like, or where they might have gone?<end>"
KO: "저기, 그 고블린의 특징이나 행선지로 짐작되는 곳은 없나요?<end>"

## 490267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブリンは聖杯の力で言葉を話し、　<line-break>ゴブゴブ団を名乗ってます。<line-break>　<line-break>あと、ネモの話だと<line-break>エンシャントにいるという話でしたが<line-break>急がないと、移動してしまうでしょう。<end>"
EN: "The Grail gave the Goblins the power of speech, and they call themselves the Gob-Gob Gang. According to Nemo, they were in Ancient, but if we don't hurry, they'll move on.<end>"
KO: "고블린들은 성배의 힘으로 말을 하고 고브고브단을 자칭하고 있습니다. 그리고 네모의 말로는 엔샨트에 있다고 했지만, 서두르지 않으면 이동할 겁니다.<end>"

## 490268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "んじゃ、行くか、<line-break><value:$28>、ルルアンタ。<end>"
EN: "Then let's go, <value:$28>, Lulluantha.<end>"
KO: "그럼 갈까, <value:$28>, 루루안타.<end>"

## 490269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何か質問ですか？<end>"
EN: "Do you have a question?<end>"
KO: "뭔가 질문 있으신가요?<end>"

## 490270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無限のソウルとは<end>"
EN: "What is the Infinite Soul?<end>"
KO: "무한의 소울이란<end>"

## 490271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "転送機について<end>"
EN: "About the teleportation device<end>"
KO: "전송기에 대해<end>"

## 490272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器について<end>"
EN: "About the divine artifact of darkness<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기에 대하여<end>"

## 490273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネモについて<end>"
EN: "About Nemo<end>"
KO: "네모에 대해서<end>"

## 490274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイアについて<end>"
EN: "About Argyleshire<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어에 대해서<end>"

## 490275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "質問はない<end>"
EN: "No questions<end>"
KO: "질문은 없다<end>"

## 490276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、それは秘密です。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ま、おいおいわかりますよ。<line-break>とりあえずはギルドで<line-break>仕事をしていってください。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh, that's a secret. You'll understand in time. For now, take on some work at the Guild.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그건 비밀입니다. 뭐, 차차 알게 될 겁니다. 일단 길드에서 일을 해 주세요.<end>"

## 490277
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他人のために働くことで、<line-break>魂を成長させていけば<line-break>いずれ、わかってきますよ。<end>"
EN: "By working for others and allowing your soul to grow, you'll come to understand in time.<end>"
KO: "다른 사람을 위해 일하며 영혼을 성장시켜 가면 언젠가 알게 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 490278
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将来、旅の途中で出会った仲間を<line-break>呼び出すための装置です。<line-break>　<line-break>一緒に旅する仲間を<line-break>変更したくなったら<line-break>この猫屋敷に来てください。<end>"
EN: "This device will allow you to summon companions you meet on your travels. If you ever want to change who travels with you, come to Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 여행 중 만난 동료를 불러내기 위한 장치입니다. 함께 여행할 동료를 바꾸고 싶어지면 이 고양이 저택으로 와 주세요.<end>"

## 490279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に旅をしたい仲間を呼び出したり、<line-break>別行動を取ることを決めた仲間を<line-break>送り出したりできますよ。<end>"
EN: "You can summon companions you wish to travel with, or send off those who have decided to go their separate ways.<end>"
KO: "함께 여행하고 싶은 동료를 불러오거나 따로 행동하기로 한 동료를 돌려보낼 수 있어요.<end>"

## 490280
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの復活に<line-break>用いられた強力な魔道器です。　<line-break>　<line-break>１２種類あるといいます。<line-break>禁断の聖杯は<line-break>その中のひとつですね。<end>"
EN: "It is a powerful magical artifact used to resurrect Ulugh, the God of Destruction. There are said to be twelve types of magical artifact, and the Forbidden Grail is one of them.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 부활에 쓰인 강력한 마도기입니다. 12종류가 있다고 하지요. 금단의 성배도 그중 하나입니다.<end>"

## 490281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖杯については<line-break>今、ゴブゴブ団という<line-break>３匹のゴブリンが持っています。<line-break>とりあえずは、<line-break>エンシャントを探してみると<line-break>彼らに会えると思いますよ。<end>"
EN: "The Forbidden Grail is currently in the possession of three Goblins known as the Gob-Gob Gang. For now, try looking for Ancient. I think that will lead you to them.<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배라면 지금 고브고브단이라는 고블린 셋이 가지고 있습니다. 일단 엔샨트를 찾아보면 그들을 만날 수 있을 거예요.<end>"

## 490282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにいるブサイクな猫です。　　　　<line-break>口は悪いけど、無害ですよ。<line-break>もともとは老人星の精霊で<line-break>天空の賢者とも呼ばれた者でしたが<line-break>地上に堕ちて、魔人になりました。<end>"
EN: "It's the ugly cat right here. It has a foul mouth, but it's harmless. It was originally the spirit of the Old Man Star and was also known as the Sage of the Heavens, but after falling to earth, it became a demon.<end>"
KO: "여기 있는 못생긴 고양이입니다. 입은 험하지만 해는 없어요. 원래는 노인성의 정령이자 천공의 현자라 불리던 자였지만, 지상에 떨어져 마인이 되었습니다.<end>"

## 490283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の強力な魔人、<line-break>１２人の円卓騎士のひとりで、<line-break>名前を知るもの、の異名を持ちます。<line-break>そのあだ名の通り<line-break>金を出せば名前を付けてくれますが<line-break>ま、あんまりおすすめはしません。<end>"
EN: "This is one of the God of Destruction's powerful demons, one of the twelve Knights of the Round Table, and bears the epithet “the One Who Knows Names.” True to that epithet, a name will be assigned for a price, though I cannot really recommend it.<end>"
KO: "파괴신의 강력한 마인, 12인의 원탁기사 중 하나로 '이름을 아는 자'라는 이명이 있습니다. 별명대로 돈을 내면 이름을 지어 주지만, 별로 추천하진 않습니다.<end>"

## 490284
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "円卓騎士には他に<line-break>アーギルシャイアなどがいますね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Other Knights of the Round Table include Argyleshire.<end>"
KO: "원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 아르길샤이어 등이 있습니다.<end>"

## 490285
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士の１人です。<line-break>妖艶な美女の姿をしています。<line-break>　<line-break>きわめて、気まぐれで残酷です。<line-break>すでに神器を手に入れるため<line-break>ミイスの村を焼き払っています。<end>"
EN: "She is one of Ulugh the God of Destruction's Knights of the Round Table and takes the form of a bewitchingly beautiful woman. She is extremely capricious and cruel. She has already burned Miis Village to the ground in pursuit of a divine artifact.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 중 한 명입니다. 요염한 미녀의 모습을 하고 있습니다. 극도로 변덕스럽고 잔혹합니다. 이미 신기를 얻기 위해 미이스 마을을 불태웠습니다.<end>"

## 490286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "究極生物を作ろうとして、<line-break>その製法を神器、禁断の聖杯から<line-break>得ようと、聖杯をねらっています。<line-break>円卓騎士には他に<line-break>うちのネモなどがいますね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "She is after the Forbidden Grail, a divine artifact, hoping to learn from it how to create an Ultimate Life-Form. The other Knights of the Round Table include our own Nemo.<end>"
KO: "궁극 생물을 만들려고, 그 제조법을 신기, 금단의 성배에서 얻기 위해 성배를 노리고 있습니다. 원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 저희 네모 같은 이들이 있죠.<end>"

## 490287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<end>"
EN: "Is that so?<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까.<end>"

## 490288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、こんな感じですかね。<line-break>わかっていただけましたか？<end>"
EN: "Well, that about covers it. Does that make sense?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이런 느낌입니다. 이해하셨나요?<end>"

## 490289
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯をねらってる悪い魔人は　<line-break>アーギルシャイア。<line-break>　<line-break>聖杯を持ってるゴブリンは<line-break>ゴブゴブ団、っていって<line-break>今はエンシャントにいる…と。<end>"
EN: "The evil demon after the Grail is Argyleshire. The Goblins who have the Grail are called the Gob-Gob Gang, and they're in Ancient now... Right.<end>"
KO: "성배를 노리는 나쁜 마인은 아르길샤이어. 성배를 가진 고블린은 고브고브단이라고 하고 지금은 엔샨트에 있다… 이거지.<end>"

## 490290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "忘れないように<line-break>覚えておかなくちゃ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I'd better remember that so I don't forget.<end>"
KO: "잊지 않도록 기억해 둬야겠어.<end>"

## 490291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺も冒険者だ。<line-break>それこそ、世界中をまわった。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>賢者の森にも何度も来た。だが、<line-break>ここに入ったことは一度もなかった。<line-break>運命に選ばれていなかったんだな。<end>"
EN: "I'm an adventurer too. I've traveled all over the world. I've been to Sage's Forest many times, but I'd never once set foot in this place. Fate hadn't chosen me, I suppose.<end>"
KO: "나도 모험자야. 정말 세계 곳곳을 돌았지. 현자의 숲에도 몇 번이나 왔어. 하지만 여기에 들어온 적은 한 번도 없었지. 운명에 선택받지 못했던 거야.<end>"

## 490292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その俺がここにいる。<line-break>これはどういうことだって思う？<end>"
EN: "And yet here I am. What do you think that means?<end>"
KO: "그런 내가 여기 있어. 이게 무슨 뜻이라고 생각해?<end>"

## 490293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんが<line-break>運命に選ばれた人間だってことさ。<end>"
EN: "It means you're someone fate has chosen.<end>"
KO: "자네가 운명에 선택받은 인간이라는 뜻이지.<end>"

## 490294
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別に、お前さんが特別な人間で、<line-break>他人より上等だ下等だってんじゃない。<line-break>安心してくれ。<line-break>俺が言いたいのは<line-break>お前さんと旅をしてると面白いことに<line-break>たくさん出会えそうだってことだ。<end>"
EN: "That doesn't mean you're special, or any better or worse than anyone else. Don't worry. All I'm saying is, traveling with you, I have a feeling we'll come across all sorts of interesting things.<end>"
KO: "네가 특별한 인간이라서 남보다 우월하다거나 열등하다는 이야기가 아니야. 안심해. 내가 말하고 싶은 건 너와 여행하면 재미있는 일을 많이 만날 것 같다는 거지.<end>"

## 490295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってよぉ！<end>"
EN: "Wait!<end>"
KO: "기다려어!<end>"

## 490296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、ちゃんとオルファウスさんに<line-break>ごあいさつしてよぉ。<line-break>　<line-break>そういうことって、いっつも<line-break>ルルアンタしか<line-break>気づかないんだからぁ！<end>"
EN: "Come on, say hello to Orphaus properly! Lulluantha's always the only one who notices things like that!<end>"
KO: "정말, 오르파우스 씨께 제대로 인사해 줘. 그런 건 늘 루루안타만 알아챈다니까!<end>"

## 490297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、そうだな。これから、<line-break>ちょくちょくお世話になるんだからな。<end>"
EN: "Heh, you're right. We'll be relying on him now and then.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그렇군. 앞으로 자주 신세를 지게 될 테니까.<end>"

## 490298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーい、じゃ、行きますか。<end>"
EN: "All right, shall we get going?<end>"
KO: "좋아, 그럼 가볼까.<end>"

## 490321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（酒場に行かなかないと･･･。）<end>"
EN: "(I need to get to the tavern...)<end>"
KO: "(술집에 가야 해….)<end>"

## 490323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスさんを待たせちゃ<line-break>いけないよ。<end>"
EN: "Can't keep Xenetes waiting.<end>"
KO: "제네테스 씨를 기다리게 하면 안 돼.<end>"

## 490324
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "反対側に何か見えるぞ<line-break>行ってみよう、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "I can see something on the other side. Let's go take a look, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "반대편에 뭔가 보인다. 가 보자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 490325
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあ、<value:$28><line-break>俺の我がままにつきあってくれないか<line-break>もう少しこの森を調べてみたいんだ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Would you humor me? I'd like to investigate this forest a little more.<end>"
KO: "있잖아, <value:$28>. 내 고집에 조금만 더 어울려 주지 않을래? 이 숲을 좀 더 조사하고 싶어.<end>"

## 490326
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec049-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "船に乗りたいのか？<line-break>あいにくだがしばらく出港予定はない。<line-break>目処が立ったころにまた来るんだな。<end>"
EN: "Looking to take a ship? Sorry, but none will be departing for a while. Come back when we have a better idea.<end>"
KO: "배를 타고 싶나? 유감이지만 당분간 출항 예정은 없다. 전망이 설 때 다시 와.<end>"

## 500001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 500002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の自由なところがうらやましい？<line-break>変なことを言うもんだな。<line-break>　<line-break>お前さんだって、<line-break>自由気ままな冒険者だぜ？<line-break>違うかい？<end>"
EN: "You're jealous of how free I am? That's a strange thing to say. You're a freewheeling adventurer yourself, aren't you?<end>"
KO: "내 자유로운 점이 부럽다고? 이상한 말을 하는군. 자네도 자유로운 모험자잖아? 아닌가?<end>"

## 500003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、自由な雰囲気ね。<line-break>ま、お前さんの言いたいことは　　<line-break>見当がつくけどな。<line-break>ひとつだけ教えてやるよ。<line-break>自由ってのは、当然にあるものでも<line-break>人から与えられるものでもない。<end>"
EN: "Heh. So it's my air of freedom, is that it? Well, I think I know what you mean. But let me tell you one thing: freedom isn't something you can take for granted, or something anyone else can give you.<end>"
KO: "후후, 자유로운 분위기네. 뭐, 네가 무슨 말을 하려는지는 짐작 가지만. 하나만 알려 줄게. 자유라는 건 당연히 존재하는 것도, 남이 주는 것도 아니야.<end>"

## 500004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自由は自分の力で<line-break>つかみ取るものなんだよ。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>それが、わからないうちは、<line-break>自由なんて<line-break>手に入らないんじゃねぇのかな。<end>"
EN: "You have to seize freedom with your own two hands. Until you understand that, I don't think you'll ever have it.<end>"
KO: "자유는 자기 힘으로 움켜쥐는 거야. 그걸 모르는 동안은 자유 같은 건 손에 넣지 못할 거라고.<end>"

## 500005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いい冒険者になるコツだって？<line-break>フフ、お前さん、冒険者になって<line-break>そんなに経ってないんだろ？<line-break>いきなり模範解答ってのも<line-break>おもしろくないと思うぜ。<line-break>ま、そんなにあせりなさんなって。<end>"
EN: "You want to know the secret to becoming a good adventurer? Heh. You haven't been at this long, have you? It'd be no fun if I handed you the perfect answer right away. Don't be in such a hurry.<end>"
KO: "좋은 모험자가 되는 비결? 후후, 자네 모험자가 된 지 얼마 안 됐지? 처음부터 정답만 듣는 것도 재미없잖아. 너무 조급해하지 말라고.<end>"

## 500006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、お前さんがなれそうなもんに<line-break>なればいいんだよ。<line-break>いい冒険者なんて肩肘はることないさ。<end>"
EN: "Just become the kind of adventurer you think you can be. No need to get all worked up about being a “good adventurer.”<end>"
KO: "뭐, 네가 될 수 있을 것 같은 게 되면 돼. 좋은 모험자가 되겠다고 너무 힘줄 필요 없어.<end>"

## 500007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうすれば、俺みたいに強くなれるか？<line-break>謙遜するわけじゃないが、世界には<line-break>俺より強い奴なんていくらでもいるぜ？<line-break>それに、仮に俺が世界最強の戦士<line-break>だとしても、俺の戦法を身につけたって<line-break>お前さんが強くなる保証はない。<end>"
EN: "How can you get as strong as me? I'm not being modest, but there are plenty of people in the world stronger than I am. And even if I were the greatest warrior alive, learning to fight the way I do wouldn't guarantee you'd become strong.<end>"
KO: "어떻게 하면 나처럼 강해질 수 있냐고? 겸손 떠는 건 아니지만 세상엔 나보다 강한 놈이 얼마든지 있어. 게다가 내가 세계 최강의 전사라고 해도 내 전법을 익힌다고 네가 강해진다는 보장은 없어.<end>"

## 500008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんと俺とじゃ、<line-break>体格も筋力も俊敏さも違う。<line-break>お前さんが俺のまねをすることはない。<line-break>俺には俺に適した戦い方があるし、<line-break>お前さんにもお前さんに適した<line-break>戦い方があるってことだな。<end>"
EN: "You and I are built differently. Our strength and speed aren't the same. There's no need for you to imitate me. I have a way of fighting that suits me, and there's a way that suits you.<end>"
KO: "너와 나는 체격도 근력도 민첩함도 달라. 네가 날 흉내 낼 필요는 없어. 나한텐 나에게 맞는 싸움법이 있고 너한텐 너에게 맞는 싸움법이 있다는 거지.<end>"

## 500009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、何にしたって、<line-break>生き残った者が勝ち<line-break>ってのが世の常道だからな。　　　　　<line-break>お前さんは、今こうして<line-break>生きているんだから、とりあえずは<line-break>そのまま努力すればいい。<end>"
EN: "Whatever the case, the one who survives is the one who wins. That's the way of the world. You're still alive, so for now, just keep at it.<end>"
KO: "뭐든 결국 살아남은 자가 이기는 게 세상 이치니까. 넌 지금 이렇게 살아 있으니 일단 그대로 노력하면 돼.<end>"

## 500010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "痛テテテテテテテ！<end>"
EN: "Ow-ow-ow-ow-ow!<end>"
KO: "아야야야야야야!<end>"

## 500011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "がらにもないご託を並べたもんだから<line-break>舌がツっちまったよ。<line-break>分を越えることはするもんじゃねえな。<end>"
EN: "All that out-of-character preaching made my tongue cramp up. I should know better than to overreach.<end>"
KO: "나답지 않게 어려운 소리를 늘어놨더니 혀가 꼬였네. 분수에 넘는 짓은 하는 게 아니군.<end>"

## 500012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…てなわけで、ま<line-break>テキトーにな、テキトーに。<end>"
EN: "So there you have it. Just take it easy. Nice and easy.<end>"
KO: "…그런 거니까, 뭐 적당히 해. 적당히.<end>"

## 500013

## 500030

## 500031

KO: "…소용없습니다. 움직일 수 없을 겁니다.<end>"

## 500032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "酒場の中で暴れるつもり？<line-break>しばらく、マヒしてもらってるわ。<end>"
EN: "Were you planning to cause a scene in the tavern? I've paralyzed you for the time being.<end>"
KO: "술집 안에서 날뛸 생각이야? 잠시 마비돼 있어 줘.<end>"

## 500033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仲間の子を傷つけたくはないのです。<line-break>一緒に来ていただきます。<end>"
EN: "I have no wish to hurt your companion. You will come with me.<end>"
KO: "동료 아이를 다치게 하고 싶지는 않습니다. 함께 가 주셔야겠습니다.<end>"

## 500035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今回は、ノーブルの代官ボルボラから<line-break>エリエナイ公レムオンの出生の秘密に<line-break>関わる手紙を運んでくる途中だった。<end>"
EN: "This time, he was on his way here carrying a letter from Bolbora, the governor of Noble, concerning the secret surrounding the birth of Duke Lemghon of Elienai.<end>"
KO: "이번에는 노블의 대관 볼보라에게서 에리에나이 공 레무온의 출생 비밀과 관련된 편지를 운반하던 중이었다.<end>"

## 500036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうだ。<line-break>ガルドランは手紙を奪還するため<line-break>レムオンが差し向けた冒険者だ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Girldlan is the adventurer Lemghon sent to recover the letter.<end>"
KO: "…그래. 갈드란은 편지를 되찾기 위해 레무온이 보낸 모험자다.<end>"

## 500037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナが生まれるころ、<line-break>私をテジャワの変で救ってくれて以来<line-break>フリントは私によく仕えてくれた。<line-break>フリントのことは<line-break>心から残念に思っている。<line-break>そなたには気を落とすなとも言えぬ。<end>"
EN: "Flint saved me during the Tejava Incident, around the time Tiana was born, and served me faithfully from that day on. What happened to Flint grieves me deeply. I cannot even ask you not to lose heart.<end>"
KO: "티아나가 태어날 무렵, 테자와의 변에서 나를 구해 준 이후 플린트는 나를 충실히 섬겨 주었다. 플린트의 일은 진심으로 안타깝게 생각한다. 네게 낙심하지 말라는 말조차 할 수 없구나.<end>"

## 500038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなたは目の輝きが他人とは違う。<line-break>目の輝きは魂の輝きを示す。<line-break>そなたにはよい魂があるのだ。<line-break>それを、今回のことで<line-break>曇らせぬようにしてほしい。<line-break>ただ、それを願うばかりだ。<end>"
EN: "Your eyes shine as no other's do. The light in them reveals the light of your soul, and yours is a good soul. I hope what has happened does not dim it. That is my only wish.<end>"
KO: "그대의 눈빛은 다른 사람과 다르다. 눈의 빛은 영혼의 빛을 보여 주지. 그대에게는 좋은 영혼이 있다. 이번 일로 그것이 흐려지지 않기를 바란다. 그저 그것뿐이다.<end>"

## 500039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さがってよい。<line-break>フリントの子<value:$28>よ。<end>"
EN: "You may go, <value:$28>, child of Flint.<end>"
KO: "물러가도 좋다. 플린트의 아이 <value:$28>여.<end>"

## 500040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec050-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントの子<value:$28>よ。<line-break>父のように私に仕えろ、とは言わぬ。<line-break>が、たまに私の所に来るとよい。<line-break>門衛には、そなたのことは<line-break>申し伝えておく。<line-break>門衛に話をすれば通れるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, child of Flint. I will not ask you to serve me as your father did. But come and see me from time to time. I will leave word at the gate. Speak to whoever is on guard, and you should be allowed through.<end>"
KO: "플린트의 자식 <value:$28>여. 아버지처럼 나를 섬기라고 하진 않겠다. 다만 가끔 내게 오너라. 문지기에게 네 이야기는 해 두겠다. 문지기에게 말하면 통과할 수 있을 것이다.<end>"
