## 510001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス様、<line-break>私たちとともにいらしてください。<end>"
EN: "Lord Xenetes, please come with us.<end>"
KO: "제네테스 님, 저희와 함께 가 주십시오.<end>"

## 510002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅん、デートのお誘い？<end>"
EN: "Oh? Asking me on a date?<end>"
KO: "흐응, 데이트 신청?<end>"

## 510003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうね。私たちじゃないけど<line-break>叔母様がお会いしたいって。<end>"
EN: "That's right. Not with us, though. Our aunt wants to see you.<end>"
KO: "그래. 우리가 아니라 숙모님께서 만나고 싶으시대.<end>"

## 510004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "叔母貴ねえ…。<line-break>美人だけど旦那持ちだからねえ。　　　<line-break>ちょっと、つまらねえかな。<line-break>悪いけど、叔母貴には、<line-break>今、でっかいものを追いかけてるから、<line-break>今回見送らせてもらうって伝えてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Your aunt, huh? She's beautiful, but she's already married. Takes some of the fun out of it. Sorry, but tell her I'm chasing something big right now, so I'll have to pass this time.<end>"
KO: "숙모라…. 미인이긴 하지만 유부녀잖아. 좀 재미없달까. 미안하지만 숙모에겐 지금 더 큰 걸 쫓고 있어서 이번엔 사양한다고 전해 줘.<end>"

## 510005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…んなこと突然、言われてもなあ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>俺は今、とーってもでっかいものを<line-break>追っかけてるんでな。<line-break>悪いが戻れんと、叔母貴に伝えてくれ。<end>"
EN: "You can't just spring this on me. I'm chasing something very, very big right now. Sorry, but tell your aunt I can't go back.<end>"
KO: "…그런 말을 갑자기 해도 말이지. 나는 지금 엄청나게 큰 걸 쫓고 있어서. 미안하지만 못 돌아간다고 숙모에게 전해 줘.<end>"

## 510006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうはまいりません。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>…兄が申しておりました。<line-break>あれは無限のソウルを持つ者だと。<line-break>興味をひかれることはわかります。<end>"
EN: "That will not do. My brother told me that one is the Bearer of the Infinite Soul. I understand why your interest was piqued.<end>"
KO: "그럴 수는 없습니다. …형님께서 말씀하셨습니다. 저 사람은 무한의 소울을 지닌 자라고. 흥미가 끌리는 건 이해합니다.<end>"

## 510007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですが、ゼネテス様がお戻りに<line-break>なられませんと、民や兵の生命を<line-break>お父上がすべて握ることになるのです。<end>"
EN: "But unless you return, Lord Xenetes, the lives of the people and soldiers will all be in your father's hands.<end>"
KO: "하지만 제네테스 님께서 돌아오시지 않으면 백성과 병사들의 목숨을 아버님께서 모두 쥐게 되는 겁니다.<end>"

## 510008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "相手はあのアンギルダン将軍。<line-break>残念ながらノヴィンでは、<line-break>勝ち目はないに等しいですわ。<end>"
EN: "They face General Angeerdan himself. Regrettably, Novin has virtually no hope of victory.<end>"
KO: "상대는 그 앙길단 장군입니다. 유감이지만 노빈으로는 승산이 거의 없어요.<end>"

## 510010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来ていただけるかしら、<line-break>ゼネテス様？<end>"
EN: "Will you come with us, Lord Xenetes?<end>"
KO: "와 주시겠어요, 제네테스 님?<end>"

## 510011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "悪いな、話の調子を変えてくれ。<line-break>友達が来た。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, but let's change the subject. A friend just arrived.<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 이야기 분위기를 좀 바꿔 줘. 친구가 왔어.<end>"

## 510012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>。<line-break>ヒマに任せてひとりで冒険に出てたら、<line-break>美人姉妹にデートに誘われちゃってさ。<line-break>結構、強引なのよ。<line-break>どうしようかね、これ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. I got bored and went adventuring on my own, and now two beautiful sisters are asking me on a date. They're being awfully pushy about it, too. What do you think I should do?<end>"
KO: "여, <value:$28>. 심심해서 혼자 모험을 나왔더니 미인 자매한테 데이트 신청을 받았어. 꽤 강압적인데. 어쩌면 좋을까, 이거.<end>"

## 510013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力ずくでも、来ていただくわ、<line-break>ゼネテス様。<end>"
EN: "You will come with us, Lord Xenetes, even if we must use force.<end>"
KO: "힘으로라도 모셔 가겠습니다, 제네테스 님.<end>"

## 510015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "けど、あんたら相手に<line-break>いつもの調子じゃ負けるんでな。<line-break>本気を出させてもらうぜ。<end>"
EN: "But if I take it easy against you two, I'll lose. So I'm afraid I'll have to get serious.<end>"
KO: "하지만 너희를 상대로 평소처럼 해선 지니까. 진심으로 해 주지.<end>"

## 510016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴァイ、ヴィア、やめろ！<end>"
EN: "Vai, Via, stop!<end>"
KO: "바이, 비아, 그만해!<end>"

## 510017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくらお前たちでも、<line-break>奴相手だと無事ではすまん。<end>"
EN: "Even you two won't come away unscathed against him.<end>"
KO: "아무리 너희라도 녀석을 상대하면 무사하진 못한다.<end>"

## 510018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヴィアだな、<line-break>雌狐に踊らされたのは。<line-break>　<line-break>血の気の多いお前のことだ。<line-break>強い奴がいると聞いて、<line-break>ふたつ返事で来たんだろう。<end>"
EN: "Via, I take it you let that vixen play you for a fool. Hot-blooded as ever. You heard there was someone strong here and agreed to come without a second thought, didn't you?<end>"
KO: "비아였군, 암여우에게 놀아난 건. 피가 뜨거운 너니까 강한 녀석이 있다는 말에 두말없이 왔겠지.<end>"

## 510019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、兄さん！<end>"
EN: "But, brother!<end>"
KO: "하지만, 형!<end>"

## 510020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前たちも気づいたはずだ。<line-break>奴は強い。<line-break>剣狼の異名はダテじゃない。<line-break>下がれ。<end>"
EN: "You must have noticed it yourselves. He's strong. They don't call him the Sword Wolf for nothing. Stand down.<end>"
KO: "너희도 알았겠지. 녀석은 강하다. 검랑이라는 별명은 허명이 아니야. 물러서라.<end>"

## 510021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前も相変わらずだな。<line-break>状況はわかってるはずだ。<end>"
EN: "You haven't changed. You know what's at stake.<end>"
KO: "너도 여전하군. 상황은 알고 있을 텐데.<end>"

## 510022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかってるさ。<end>"
EN: "Of course I do.<end>"
KO: "알고 있어.<end>"

## 510023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なら、一緒に来てくれや。<line-break>妹たちも失敗者の汚名を着せられちゃ、<line-break>商売上がったりなんでな。<end>"
EN: "Then come with us. If my sisters get branded as failures, it'll ruin their livelihood.<end>"
KO: "그럼 같이 가 줘. 동생들까지 실패자라는 오명을 쓰면 장사가 끝장이거든.<end>"

## 510024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イヤだ。俺が追いかけてる奴は<line-break>いずれ、世界に関わることになる。<end>"
EN: "No. The one I'm chasing will eventually have a bearing on the whole world.<end>"
KO: "싫어. 내가 쫓는 녀석은 결국 세계와 관련된 존재가 될 거야.<end>"

## 510025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前が出ていかなければ、<line-break>ロストールの民や兵が<line-break>大勢死ぬだろうな。<line-break>彼らを放っておくなんてマネ、<line-break>お前にできるのか？<end>"
EN: "If you don't go, many of Rostorl's people and soldiers will die. Can you really abandon them?<end>"
KO: "네가 나가지 않으면 로스톨의 백성과 병사들이 많이 죽을 거다. 그들을 내버려 둘 수 있겠나?<end>"

## 510026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺がのこのこ出ていっても、<line-break>総合的な死人の数は変わらない。<end>"
EN: "Even if I went trotting back, the overall death toll wouldn't change.<end>"
KO: "…내가 어슬렁어슬렁 나가 봐야 전체 사망자 수는 달라지지 않아.<end>"

## 510027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "総合的な死人ってことは、<line-break>お前、敵軍の死人増加まで勘定に<line-break>入れてるのか？<line-break>いつも以上に自信家だな。<end>"
EN: "The total death toll? Are you counting the additional enemy casualties too? You're even more confident than usual.<end>"
KO: "전체 사망자라니, 너 적군 사망자 증가까지 계산에 넣은 거야? 평소보다 더 자신만만하네.<end>"

## 510028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いずれにしても、お前に戻って<line-break>もらわないと、ヴァイはともかく、<line-break>ヴィアが問題なんでな。<line-break>あいつは、自分の誇りにかけて、<line-break>抜け駆けしてでもお前を襲うだろう。<line-break>絶対にな。<end>"
EN: "Either way, you have to come back with us. Vai aside, Via is the problem. Pride won't let her leave this alone. She'll slip away and attack you herself if she has to. I guarantee it.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 네가 돌아와 주지 않으면 바이야 그렇다 쳐도 비아가 문제야. 그 녀석은 자기 자존심을 걸고 독단적으로라도 너를 습격할 거야. 틀림없이.<end>"

## 510029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、お前とやり合えば、<line-break>ヴィアの実力でも<line-break>無事じゃすまないだろう。<end>"
EN: "But even someone as skilled as Via won't come away unscathed from a fight with you.<end>"
KO: "하지만 너와 맞붙으면 비아의 실력으로도 무사하진 못할 거다.<end>"

## 510030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、お前を腕ずくで連れていく。<end>"
EN: "So I'll take you back by force.<end>"
KO: "그러니 널 힘으로라도 데려가겠다.<end>"

## 510032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "妹を守るためなら<line-break>なんだってするさ。<end>"
EN: "I'd do anything to protect my sister.<end>"
KO: "여동생을 지키기 위해서라면 뭐든 할 거야.<end>"

## 510034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お前さんの勝ちだ。<line-break>わかった。行くよ。<end>"
EN: "You win. All right, I'll go.<end>"
KO: "…네가 이겼다. 알았어. 갈게.<end>"

## 510035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>悪いが、急用ができた。<line-break>俺はここで降ろさせてもらう。<line-break>厄介なことに、<line-break>俺にしかできない用事でな。<line-break>だから、しばらくはお別れだ。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, <value:$28>, but something urgent has come up. This is where I leave you. Unfortunately, it's something only I can do. So we'll have to say goodbye for a while.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 미안하지만 급한 일이 생겼다. 난 여기서 내릴게. 성가시게도 나밖에 할 수 없는 일이거든. 그러니 한동안 작별이다.<end>"

## 510036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き留めてくれるのかい？　　　　<line-break>うれしいねえ。<line-break>　<line-break>だけど、俺なしじゃやって<line-break>いけないのかい？<line-break>自分ひとりじゃ何もできないのか？<end>"
EN: "You want me to stay? I'm touched. But can you really not manage without me? Can't you do anything on your own?<end>"
KO: "붙잡아 주는 건가? 기쁘군. 하지만 나 없이는 못 하는 거야? 혼자서는 아무것도 못 하나?<end>"

## 510037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん…<end>"
EN: "Yeah...<end>"
KO: "응…<end>"

## 510038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ！<end>"
EN: "No!<end>"
KO: "아니!<end>"

## 510039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…。そうだ。いいんだよ、それで。<line-break>未熟だから、人に頼る、<line-break>それは悪いことじゃない。<line-break>未熟だから、お前は一生懸命だ。<line-break>未熟ゆえに、お前の目は<line-break>純粋な見方ができる。<end>"
EN: "Heh... Yes. That's just fine. There's nothing wrong with relying on others when you still have growing to do. That's why you try so hard, and why you can still see things with unclouded eyes.<end>"
KO: "후후…. 그래. 그걸로 됐어. 미숙하니까 남에게 의지하는 건 나쁜 일이 아니야. 미숙하니까 넌 최선을 다하지. 미숙하기 때문에 네 눈은 순수하게 볼 수 있어.<end>"

## 510040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひとりでは、できないことがあっても<line-break>お前は仲間を頼ることができる。　　　<line-break>それこそがお前の力なんだよ。<line-break>何を頼ってもいい。誰に聞いてもいい。<line-break>…だがひとつ、最後に決める役割だけは<line-break>自分が持って放すな。<end>"
EN: "When you can't do something alone, you can turn to your companions. That is your strength. Rely on whatever you need. Ask whoever you like. But never surrender one thing: the final decision must always be yours.<end>"
KO: "혼자서는 못 하는 일이 있어도 너는 동료에게 의지할 수 있다. 그것이 바로 네 힘이야. 무엇에 기대도 좋고 누구에게 물어도 좋아. …하지만 마지막에 결정하는 역할 하나만큼은 네가 쥐고 절대 놓지 마라.<end>"

## 510041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…、そうだ、その調子だ。<line-break>頼もしくなったな、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Heh... Yes, that's the spirit. You've become quite dependable, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "후후… 그래, 그 기세다. 믿음직해졌군, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 510042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分の道を選ぶときは、　　<line-break>必ず自分で考え、<line-break>自分で決めるんだ。<line-break>俺は俺の道を行く。<line-break>お前はお前の道を行くんだ。<line-break>お前にしか歩めない道をな。<end>"
EN: "When the time comes to choose your path, think for yourself and make your own decision. I'll follow my path, and you follow yours―the path that only you can walk.<end>"
KO: "자기 길을 고를 때는 반드시 스스로 생각하고 스스로 결정해. 나는 내 길을 간다. 너는 네 길을 가는 거야. 너만 걸을 수 있는 길을.<end>"

## 510043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0今度会ったときは、朝まで飲もう。<line-break>お前さんに輝くものが残っていれば、<line-break>それを目印に、俺は必ず戻ってくる。<end>今度会ったときは、<line-break>もっともっとイイ女に<line-break>なってるんだぜ。<line-break>お前さんに輝くものが残っていれば、<line-break>それを目印に、俺は必ず戻ってくる。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Next time we meet, let's drink until dawn. As long as that light in you still shines, I'll use it to find my way back. I promise.<end>Next time we meet, you'd better have become an even finer woman. As long as that light in you still shines, I'll use it to find my way back. I promise.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0다음에 만나면 아침까지 마시자. 네게 빛나는 것이 남아 있다면 그걸 표식 삼아 나는 반드시 돌아올게.<end>다음에 만날 땐 훨씬 더 멋진 여자가 되어 있어라. 네게 빛나는 것이 남아 있다면 그걸 표식 삼아 나는 반드시 돌아올게.<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあな。<end>"
EN: "See you around.<end>"
KO: "그럼 잘 있어.<end>"

## 510048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうこなくちゃ。<line-break>よしよし、今、登録して　　　　　<line-break>紹介状を送るからな。<line-break>…って、あんたディンガルの傭兵も<line-break>引き受けてるじゃないか。<line-break>困るなぁ。<end>"
EN: "That's the spirit. All right, I'll register you now and send over a letter of introduction. Wait―you've signed on as a mercenary for Dyneskal too. That's a problem.<end>"

## 510049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Didn't you know? Tensions are running high between Rostorl and Dyneskal. You'll have to choose one.<end>"
KO: "모르나? 로스톨과 딩갈은 긴장 상태야. 둘 중 하나로 해 줘.<end>"

## 510052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガルの傭兵はやめて<line-break>ロストールの傭兵になるんだね？<line-break>じゃあ、そういうふうに登録しとくよ。<line-break>ロストール王宮へ行ってくれ。<line-break>一応、話はこれで通ってるはずだから。<end>"
EN: "So you're leaving Dyneskal's service to become a mercenary for Rostorl, right? I'll update your registration. Head to Rostorl's royal palace. They should be expecting you.<end>"
KO: "딩갈 용병은 그만두고 로스톨 용병이 되는 거지? 그럼 그렇게 등록해 둘게. 로스톨 왕궁으로 가. 일단 이야기는 전달되어 있을 거야.<end>"

## 510053

## 510054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 510055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうこなくちゃ。<line-break>よしよし、今、登録して　　　　<line-break>紹介状を送るからな。<line-break>あんたはロストールの王宮へ<line-break>行けばいい。<line-break>話はそれで通るはずだからな。<end>"

## 510056

## 510057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 510058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Here to sign on as a mercenary? You're in luck―we've got work for you already. Ready for battle?<end>"

## 510067

## 510068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ。そのとおりさ。<line-break>俺がゼネテス・ファーロス。<line-break>ロストール王国軍副司令閣下様だ。　<line-break>生まれてみれば、親父がたまたま<line-break>お偉い貴族様をやってたんでな。<line-break>ま、こういう商売に就かされたわけだ。<end>"
EN: "That's right. I'm Xenetes Pharos―His Excellency, Deputy Commander of the Royal Army of Rostorl. I happened to be born with a high-and-mighty noble for a father, so I got pushed into this line of work.<end>"
KO: "그래. 맞아. 내가 제네테스 파로스. 로스톨 왕국군 부사령관 각하님이다. 태어나 보니 아버지가 우연히 높은 귀족이어서 말이지. 뭐, 이런 일을 하게 된 거야.<end>"

## 510069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれ、言わなかったけか？<line-break>ま、俺のあふれ出る気品みたいなもんで<line-break>貴族だとわかってると思ってたんでな。<end>"
EN: "What, I never told you? I thought my unmistakable air of refinement would've made it obvious I was a noble.<end>"
KO: "어라, 말 안 했던가? 뭐, 내게서 흘러넘치는 품격만 봐도 귀족인 줄 알았을 거라고 생각했지.<end>"

## 510070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ハハ、お前さんなら、俺が<line-break>名家ファーロス家の跡取りだと知っても<line-break>変わりなく接してくれるって信じてた。<line-break>だから、別に言い出す必要もないと<line-break>思ってたんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Ha! I knew you'd treat me no differently even if you found out I was heir to the illustrious House Pharos. So I never saw any need to mention it.<end>"
KO: "하하, 자네라면 내가 명문 파로스 가문의 후계자란 걸 알아도 변함없이 대해 줄 거라 믿었어. 그래서 굳이 말할 필요도 없다고 생각했지.<end>"

## 510071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前さんたち傭兵は、<line-break>詳しい事の成り行きを<line-break>知らないんだろ？<end>"
EN: "You mercenaries haven't been told exactly how all this came about, have you?<end>"
KO: "자네들 용병은 자세한 사정의 흐름을 모르고 있지?<end>"

## 510072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメアのライオンちゃんの<line-break>ディンガル帝国に動きがあったんだ。<line-break>どうやら攻めてくるらしい。<line-break>俺の親父…ロストール軍総司令官<line-break>ノヴィン・ファーロスは<line-break>大量の兵士と物資を輸送する必要から…<end>"
EN: "That little lion Nemea's Dyneskal Empire is on the move. Looks like they're preparing to invade. My father, Novin Pharos, Commander-in-Chief of the Rostorl army, reasoned that moving so many troops and supplies would require...<end>"
KO: "네메아의 사자, 딘갈 제국에 움직임이 있어. 아무래도 쳐들어오는 모양이야. 내 아버지… 로스톨군 총사령관 노빈 파로스는 대량의 병사와 물자를 수송할 필요 때문에…<end>"

## 510073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帝国軍が海路から攻めてくると予想し<line-break>上陸時に敵を叩く作戦に出た。<line-break>…だが、俺にはそうくるとは思えない。<line-break>根拠はいろいろあるんだが、何よりも<line-break>敵の将軍が、南方攻略軍司令・朱雀将軍<line-break>…つまりアンギルダンてのが大きいな。<end>"
EN: "He expects the imperial army to come by sea and plans to strike as they land. But I don't think that's what they'll do. I've got plenty of reasons, but the biggest is the enemy commander: Angeerdan, commander of the Southern Campaign Army―the Vermilion Bird General.<end>"
KO: "제국군이 해로로 쳐들어올 거라고 보고 상륙할 때 적을 치는 작전을 세웠다. …하지만 난 그렇게 올 것 같지 않아. 근거는 여러 가지지만, 무엇보다 적장이 남방공략군 사령관 주작장군… 즉 앙길단이라는 점이 커.<end>"

## 510074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺ならこうする…。まず<line-break>大量の兵を動員するという情報を流し、<line-break>海路で攻めてくると俺らに思わせる。<line-break>そうして、海の方へ俺らをおびき寄せて<line-break>自分は精兵を率い、分断の山脈を越えて<line-break>大街道沿いの都市を占領する。<end>"
EN: "Here's what I'd do. First, leak word that a vast army is being mobilized and make us think the attack will come by sea. Once we've been lured toward the coast, I'd lead an elite force across the Dividing Mountains and seize the cities along the Great Highway.<end>"
KO: "나라면 이렇게 한다…. 먼저 대병력을 동원한다는 정보를 흘려 우리에게 해로로 공격할 거라 믿게 한다. 그렇게 바다 쪽으로 우리를 유인하고 자신은 정예병을 이끌고 분단의 산맥을 넘어 대가도 연선 도시를 점령하는 거지.<end>"

## 510075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうやって橋頭堡を築いたあと<line-break>ゆっくり、大量に集めた兵と物資を<line-break>運び込ませ、俺らと決戦をする。<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁんと<line-break>対等の条件で戦争したら、勝てる奴は<line-break>世界にほとんどいないからな。<end>"
EN: "After establishing a bridgehead, I'd slowly bring in all those troops and supplies, then fight us in a decisive battle. Almost no one in the world could beat old Angeerdan in a war fought on equal terms.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 교두보를 만든 뒤 천천히 대량으로 모은 병력과 물자를 들여와 우리와 결전을 벌이겠지. 앙길단 영감과 대등한 조건으로 전쟁해서 이길 수 있는 사람은 세계에도 거의 없으니까.<end>"

## 510076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのあとは、王都ロストールまでは<line-break>大街道が通っていて、障害はゼロだ。<line-break>簡単に攻撃できる。<line-break>ま、この意見を親父に言ったんだが<line-break>聞く耳持たずで、却下されたよ。<end>"
EN: "From there, the Great Highway runs straight to the capital, Rostorl, with nothing in the way. It'd be an easy attack. I told my father all this, but he wouldn't listen. Rejected it outright.<end>"
KO: "그 뒤에는 왕도 로스톨까지 대가도가 이어져 있어 장애물은 제로야. 쉽게 공격할 수 있지. 이 의견을 아버지께 말했지만 들은 척도 않고 기각됐어.<end>"

## 510077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺の予測が外れていてくれると<line-break>いいんだがな。<end>"
EN: "I only hope I'm wrong.<end>"
KO: "내 예상이 빗나가면 좋겠는데.<end>"

## 510078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、いざってときに<line-break>頼りになる奴が欲しかったんでね。<line-break>　<line-break>お前さんが傭兵の中にいるのを<line-break>見つけたんで、来てもらった。<line-break>よろしく頼むぜ。<end>"
EN: "Anyway, I wanted someone dependable around in case things went bad. When I spotted you among the mercenaries, I had you brought here. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 정작 필요할 때믿을 만한 녀석이 필요했거든. 네가 용병들 사이에 있는 걸발견해서, 와 달라고 했어. 잘 부탁하지.<end>"

## 510079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール軍は、海路からの<line-break>侵攻に<line-break><end>"
EN: "The Rostorl army, in response to an invasion by sea...<end>"
KO: "로스톨군은 해로에서의 침공에<end>"

## 510080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　帝国軍は分断の山脈を越えて進攻。<line-break>　大街道沿いの都市を電撃的に占領した。<line-break>これは大陸史上、例を見ない壮挙であった。<end>"
EN: "The imperial army advanced across the Dividing Mountains and swiftly seized the cities along the Great Highway. It was an unprecedented feat in the history of the continent.<end>"
KO: "제국군은 분단의 산맥을 넘어 진격했다. 대가도 연선의 도시를 전격적으로 점령했다. 이는 대륙사상 유례없는 위업이었다.<end>"

## 510081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　王都ロストールへ迫った帝国軍に対し、　<line-break>　ロストール側は軍を大街道へ反転させ、<line-break>　　これを迎撃するべく、急行した。<end>"
EN: "As the imperial army closed in on the capital, Rostorl turned its forces back toward the Great Highway and rushed to intercept it.<end>"
KO: "왕도 로스톨로 다가오는 제국군에 맞서 로스톨 측은 군을 대가도로 돌려 이를 요격하기 위해 급행했다.<end>"

## 510082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ダメだ。ま～たボツだ。　　　　　　　<line-break>まいったな～。<line-break>　<line-break>帝国軍を牽制するために、王都守備軍と<line-break>連携して、ゆっくり進軍させるように、<line-break>親父に言ったんだが、また却下された。<end>"
EN: "No good. Rejected again. What am I going to do with him? I told my father to coordinate with the capital defense force and advance slowly, keeping the imperial army in check. He turned me down again.<end>"
KO: "안 돼. 또 기각됐어. 난감하네…. 제국군을 견제하려고 왕도 수비군과 연계해서 천천히 진군하라고 아버지께 말씀드렸는데 또 거절당했어.<end>"

## 510083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "侵入者を放っておいては<line-break>ロストール貴族の名折れだってな。<line-break>軍を急行させて決戦するんだと。<line-break>急ぐのもいいが、隊列はメチャクチャ、<line-break>兵士も疲れ切ってる軍隊なんて、<line-break>待ちかまえてる帝国軍のエジキさ。<end>"
EN: "He says letting invaders run free would disgrace the nobles of Rostorl. So he's rushing the army ahead to force a decisive battle. Speed's all well and good, but an army with its ranks in chaos and its soldiers exhausted is easy prey for imperial troops lying in wait.<end>"
KO: "침입자를 내버려두면 로스톨 귀족의 수치라나. 군을 급히 보내 결전을 벌인대. 서두르는 건 좋지만 대열은 엉망이고 병사들도 지친 군대라면 기다리고 있는 제국군의 먹잇감이지.<end>"

## 510084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく、親父の説得は続けよう。<line-break>あとは、臨機応変に、つまり<line-break>行き当たりばったりってとこだな。<end>"
EN: "For now, I'll keep trying to persuade my father. After that, we'll adapt to circumstances―which is to say, make it up as we go.<end>"
KO: "어쨌든 아버지 설득은 계속하자. 나머지는 임기응변으로, 그러니까 닥치는 대로 해 보는 수밖에 없겠군.<end>"

## 510085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、ゼネテスの説得は聞き入れられず、<line-break>隊列が乱れたまま到着したロストール軍は　　<line-break>　アンギルダンの攻撃を受け、壊滅した。<line-break><line-break>総司令ノヴィン・ファーロスをはじめ<line-break>多くのロストールの名だたる貴族が、<line-break>この戦いの中で、命を落とした。<end>"
EN: "But Xenetes's counsel went unheeded. The Rostorl army arrived with its ranks still in disarray and was annihilated by Angeerdan's attack. Commander-in-Chief Novin Pharos and many other renowned nobles of Rostorl lost their lives in the battle.<end>"
KO: "하지만 제네테스의 설득은 받아들여지지 않았고, 대열이 흐트러진 채 도착한 로스톨군은 앙길단의 공격을 받아 괴멸했다. 총사령 노빈 파로스를 비롯해 많은 로스톨의 이름난 귀족들이 이 전투에서 목숨을 잃었다.<end>"

## 510086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルブルグ伯タルテュバ・リューガは<line-break>自分の軍を捨て、ひとりだけ極秘に逃走。　　<line-break>命は助かったが、その評判は地に落ちた。<end>"
EN: "Count Tartuba Ryuga of Ruburg abandoned his army and secretly fled on his own. He survived, but his reputation was ruined.<end>"
KO: "루브르그 백작 타르튜바 류가는 자신의 군대를 버리고 홀로 극비리에 도주했다. 목숨은 건졌지만 평판은 땅에 떨어졌다.<end>"

## 510087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル帝国軍アンギルダンは、<line-break>ロストール軍を壊滅させると、深追いを禁じ、<line-break>全軍に物資の現地調達を命じた。<end>"
EN: "After annihilating the Rostorl army, Angeerdan forbade the Dyneskal imperial forces from pursuing the enemy and ordered the entire army to procure supplies locally.<end>"
KO: "딘갈 제국군의 앙길단은 로스톨군을 괴멸시키자 추격을 금하고 전군에 현지에서 물자를 조달하라고 명했다.<end>"

## 510088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…と、総司令閣下は自刃なされ、<line-break>ロストール貴族にふさわしい<line-break>見事な最期を遂げられました。<end>"
EN: "And so the Commander-in-Chief took his own life, meeting a splendid end worthy of a Rostorl noble.<end>"
KO: "…그렇게 총사령관 각하께서는 자결하시어 로스톨 귀족다운 훌륭한 최후를 맞으셨습니다.<end>"

## 510089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、これだけの人数を<line-break>こんな状況に落とし込んでおいて<line-break>ご自分は貴族の誇りと心中か。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. He got all these people into this mess, then chose to die with his noble pride.<end>"
KO: "원 참, 이렇게 많은 사람을 이런 상황에 빠뜨려 놓고 본인은 귀족의 긍지와 함께 죽은 건가.<end>"

## 510090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かくなる上は、ゼネテス様にも<line-break>誇りを汚さず、潔く自刃なさるようにと<line-break>閣下はおっしゃっておいででした。<end>"
EN: "The Commander also said that, now matters had come to this, Lord Xenetes should take his own life with dignity rather than stain his honor.<end>"
KO: "이렇게 된 이상 제네테스 님께서도 긍지를 더럽히지 말고 깨끗이 자결하시라고 각하께서 말씀하셨습니다.<end>"

## 510091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなロマンチズムに<line-break>つきあう気はないな。<end>"
EN: "I've no intention of indulging that sort of romanticism.<end>"
KO: "그런 낭만주의에 어울려 줄 생각은 없어.<end>"

## 510092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "閣下はこうもおっしゃっておいででした。<line-break>ゼネテス様には、やや貴族としての<line-break>常識にかけるところがある。<line-break>命を惜しみ、おめおめと生き残ろうと<line-break>するならば、ゼネテス様の介錯を<line-break>してさしあげるようにと…！<end>"
EN: "The Commander said something else as well. Lord Xenetes is somewhat lacking in the common sense expected of a nobleman. If you should cling to life and shamelessly try to survive, I was to act as your second and see to your death myself!<end>"
KO: "각하께서는 이런 말씀도 하셨습니다. 제네테스 님은 귀족으로서의 상식이 조금 부족하니, 목숨을 아까워하며 구차하게 살아남으려 한다면 제네테스 님을 직접 편히 보내 드리라고…!<end>"

## 510093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ～てと。<line-break>生き残る方法を考えなきゃ、だな。　　<line-break>　<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁんは<line-break>俺らを叩きつぶすのよりも、<line-break>物資調達の方を優先させた。<end>"
EN: "Well, then. Time to work out how we're going to survive. Old Angeerdan put procuring supplies ahead of finishing us off.<end>"
KO: "자아. 살아남을 방법을 생각해야겠군. 앙길단 아저씨는 우리를 짓밟는 것보다 물자 조달을 우선했어.<end>"

## 510094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンのとっつぁんは正しい。<line-break>レムオンの王都守備軍が<line-break>攻めてくる可能性を考えるなら…<line-break>戦意をなくし、無力化した<line-break>俺らを無視して、自分たちの防備を<line-break>固める準備を急ぐのは、理の当然だ。<end>"
EN: "Old Angeerdan's reasoning is sound. With the possibility that Lemghon's capital defense force may attack, it makes perfect sense to ignore us as a demoralized, neutralized force and hurry to strengthen his own defenses.<end>"
KO: "앙길단 영감의 판단은 맞아. 레무온의 왕도 수비군이 공격해 올 가능성을 생각한다면… 전의를 잃고 무력화된 우리를 무시하고 자기들 방비를 굳히는 준비를 서두르는 건 당연하지.<end>"

## 510095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、それが実は間違いでな。<line-break>俺たちはまだ、<line-break>戦意はなくしちゃいなかったりする。　<end>"
EN: "But that's where he's wrong. As it happens, we haven't quite lost our will to fight.<end>"
KO: "하지만 사실 그게 틀렸어. 우리는 아직 전의를 잃지 않았거든.<end>"

## 510096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はあきらめの悪い男でな。　　　<line-break>生き残るためのガッツなら<line-break>売るほどある。<line-break>今なら向こうさんも攻めてくるとは<line-break>思ってない。徴発隊も出払っていて<line-break>敵中枢部に直でなぐり込む好機だ！<end>"
EN: "I'm a stubborn man. When it comes to the will to survive, I've got plenty to spare. They won't expect us to attack now, and their requisitioning parties are all away. It's the perfect chance to strike straight at their command!<end>"
KO: "난 포기가 느린 남자라서 말이야. 살아남는 근성이라면 남아돌아. 지금이라면 저쪽도 우리가 쳐들어올 거라 생각하지 않을 거야. 징발대도 나가 있으니 적 중추부를 곧장 때릴 절호의 기회다!<end>"

## 510097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無傷な俺の部隊を中心に<line-break>ロストール軍の敗残兵をまとめ、<line-break>敵ディンガル軍中枢を叩く！　　　　　<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁんを倒せば<line-break>あとのディンガル軍は浮き足だって<line-break>無力化する！　ねらうは朱雀将軍のみ！<end>"
EN: "We'll rally the remnants of the Rostorl army around my unscathed unit and strike the heart of the Dyneskal Army! Take down old Angeerdan, and the rest of the Dyneskal Army will panic and cease to be a threat. Our only target is the Vermilion Bird General!<end>"
KO: "멀쩡한 내 부대를 중심으로 로스톨군 패잔병을 모아 적 딩갈군의 중추를 친다! 앙길단 아저씨를 쓰러뜨리면 나머지 딩갈군은 동요해서 무력화돼! 노릴 건 주작장군 하나뿐이다!<end>"

## 510098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は、俺が軍をまとめる間、<line-break>先鋒を率い、先に帝国軍に突っ込んで、<line-break>大暴れしてくれ。<line-break>お前の活躍を聞けば、<line-break>敗残兵の集まりもよくなる。<line-break>危険な任務だが、お前しか頼れん。<end>"
EN: "While I rally the army, take command of the vanguard, charge into the imperial forces ahead of us, and raise hell. Once word of your exploits spreads, the stragglers will be more willing to rally. It's dangerous, but you're the only one I can trust with it.<end>"
KO: "내가 군을 정비하는 동안 넌 선봉을 이끌고 먼저 제국군에 돌진해서 크게 날뛰어 줘. 네 활약 소식을 들으면 패잔병들도 더 모일 거야. 위험한 임무지만 너밖에 믿을 사람이 없어.<end>"

## 510099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "５分でいい。<line-break>頼むぜ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Just give me five minutes. I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "5분이면 돼. 부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 510100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカな、ロストール軍だと！<end>"
EN: "Impossible―the Rostorl army?!<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼, 로스톨군이라고!<end>"

## 510101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "徴発のため、ほとんど<line-break>軍が出払っているときに！<end>"
EN: "Now, when nearly all our forces are out requisitioning supplies!<end>"
KO: "징발 때문에 군이 거의 다 나가 있는 때에!<end>"

## 510102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奇襲だ！<line-break>とにかく、もみつぶせ！<end>"
EN: "It's a surprise attack! Crush them at once!<end>"
KO: "기습이다! 어쨌든 짓뭉개 버려!<end>"

## 510103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、どうしたことだ！<line-break>ロストール軍が復活したとは！<end>"
EN: "Damn! How can this be? The Rostorl army has rallied!<end>"
KO: "큭, 이게 어떻게 된 일이냐! 로스톨군이 부활했다니!<end>"

## 510104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス…、<line-break>そうか、お主がおったのか！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お主ならば、あの敗軍を<line-break>まとめる器量がある！<line-break>くっ、ぬかったわ！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes... So you were here! You have what it takes to rally a defeated army like that. Damn it, I miscalculated!<end>"
KO: "제네테스…. 그렇군, 네놈이 있었나! 네놈이라면 그 패잔군을 수습할 역량이 있다! 큭, 방심했군!<end>"

## 510105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主がおるとわかっておれば、<line-break>敗残兵の殲滅を優先させたものを！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Had I known you were here, I would have made exterminating the remnants my first priority!<end>"
KO: "네놈이 있는 줄 알았다면 패잔병 섬멸을 우선했을 것을!<end>"

## 510106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…とっつぁん、<line-break>悪いが、命をもらう。　　<line-break>　<line-break>…ロストールの兵の<line-break>命がかかってるんでな。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, old man, but I'm taking your life. The lives of Rostorl's soldiers depend on it.<end>"
KO: "…아저씨, 미안하지만 목숨을 받겠다. …로스톨 병사들의 목숨이 걸려 있어서 말이야.<end>"

## 510107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい、そうですかと<line-break>くれてやるわけにはいかんな。<line-break>　<line-break>こっちも<line-break>ディンガルの兵の命が<line-break>かかっておるのでな！<end>"
EN: "I cannot simply hand it over because you ask. The lives of Dyneskal's soldiers depend on me as well!<end>"
KO: "네, 그렇습니까 하고 순순히 내줄 수는 없지. 이쪽도 딘갈 병사들의 목숨이 걸려 있으니까!<end>"

## 510108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…、<line-break>運命の女神は残酷なものよ！　　　<line-break>ここまで来て、わしを見限るとはな…。<line-break>ゼネテスよ…。<line-break>お主ほどの男が…ファーロスの…<line-break>跡取りだったとは…、うぐっ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh... The Goddess of Fate is cruel indeed! To abandon me now, after I've come this far... Xenetes... To think a man of your caliber was the Pharos heir... Ngh...<end>"
KO: "후후… 운명의 여신은 잔혹하구나! 여기까지 와서 나를 버리다니…. 제네테스여…. 자네 같은 사내가… 파로스의… 후계자였다니… 윽…<end>"

## 510109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退け！<line-break>バラバラに地下王国を通るのだ！<line-break>みな…生き延びるの…じゃ…！<end>"
EN: "Retreat! Split up and make your way through the Underground Kingdom! All of you... survive...!<end>"
KO: "물러나라! 뿔뿔이 지하왕국을 통과해라! 모두… 살아남아라…!<end>"

## 510110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朱雀将軍が討ち取られたぞ！<end>"
EN: "The Vermilion Bird General has been slain!<end>"
KO: "주작장군이 쓰러졌다!<end>"

## 510111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退け！　退けええい！<end>"
EN: "Retreat! Retreeeat!<end>"
KO: "물러나라! 물러나아아!<end>"

## 510112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敗残兵を驚くべき早さで立て直した<line-break>ゼネテスの奇襲により、アンギルダンが<line-break>討ち取られると、帝国軍は潰走した。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes rallied the defeated troops with astonishing speed and launched a surprise attack. Once Angeerdan was slain, the Imperial forces were routed.<end>"
KO: "패잔병을 놀라운 속도로 재정비한 제네테스의 기습으로 앙길단이 전사하자 제국군은 무너져 도주했다.<end>"

## 510113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この戦いで、ゼネテスの声望は<line-break>一気に上がり、奇跡の名将と呼ばれたが<line-break>ロストールの被害も甚大なものがあった。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes's renown soared after this battle, and he became known as a general who worked miracles. Rostorl, however, had suffered devastating losses.<end>"
KO: "이 전투로 제네테스의 명성은 단숨에 높아져 기적의 명장이라 불렸지만 로스톨의 피해도 막대했다.<end>"

## 510114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…千年樹に刻まれし竜綱の盟約に基づき<line-break>我、ロストール王<line-break>セルモノー・リューは…<line-break>汝、ゼネテス・ファーロスを<line-break>ファーロス家当主と認め、その功により<line-break>ロストール王国軍総司令に任ずる…。<end>"
EN: "By the Covenant of the Dragon Cord, graven upon the Thousand-Year Tree, I, Selmono Ryu, King of Rostorl, hereby recognize you, Xenetes Pharos, as head of House Pharos and, in honor of your deeds, appoint you commander-in-chief of the armies of the Kingdom of Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "…천년수에 새겨진 용강의 맹약에 따라 나 로스톨 왕 셀모노 류는… 그대 제네테스 파로스를 파로스 가문의 당주로 인정하고, 그 공적에 따라 로스톨 왕국군 총사령관으로 임명한다….<end>"

## 510115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、最高の殊勲を<line-break>ファーロスのドラ息子に<line-break>奪われるとはな。<end>"
EN: "Damn it. To think that worthless Pharos brat would claim the highest honor.<end>"
KO: "큭, 최고의 공을 파로스의 망나니 아들에게 빼앗기다니.<end>"

## 510116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "第２の殊勲、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "The second-highest honor goes to <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "제2의 공훈, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 510117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかも、第２の殊勲も<line-break>ファーロスのゆかりの者か…！<end>"
EN: "And even the second honor goes to someone connected with Pharos...!<end>"
KO: "게다가 두 번째 공로자도 파로스와 연이 있는 자인가…!<end>"

## 510118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、行け。<end>"
EN: "Go on, then.<end>"
KO: "자, 가.<end>"

## 510119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0…千年樹に刻まれし竜綱の盟約に基づき<line-break>我、ロストール王<line-break>セルモノー・リューは…<line-break>その功により、汝、<value:$28>に<line-break>騎士の証である盾を与え、<line-break>白竜騎士、竜字将軍に叙任する。<end>…千年樹に刻まれし竜綱の盟約に基づき<line-break>ロストール王セルモノー・リューは…<line-break>女人の身である汝に、特例をもって<line-break>騎士の証である盾を与え、<line-break>白竜騎士、竜字将軍に叙任する。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0By the Covenant of the Dragon Cord, graven upon the Thousand-Year Tree, I, Selmono Ryu, King of Rostorl, hereby grant you, <value:$28>, the shield that marks a knight and, in honor of your deeds, invest you as a White Dragon Knight and Dragon-Sigil General.<end>By the Covenant of the Dragon Cord, graven upon the Thousand-Year Tree, Selmono Ryu, King of Rostorl, hereby makes a special exception for you as a woman, granting you the shield that marks a knight and investing you as a White Dragon Knight and Dragon-Sigil General.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0…천년수에 새겨진 용강의 맹약에 따라, 나 로스톨 왕 셀모노 류는… 그 공적을 기려 그대 <value:$28>에게 기사임을 증명하는 방패를 내리고 백룡기사, 용자 장군에 임명한다.<end>…천년수에 새겨진 용강의 맹약에 따라 로스톨 왕 셀모노 류는… 여인의 몸인 그대에게 특별히 기사임을 증명하는 방패를 내리고 백룡기사, 용자 장군에 임명한다.<end>"

## 510120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールを支える将軍として<line-break>ゼネテスとともに活躍するがよい。<end>"
EN: "Serve well beside Xenetes as a general upon whom Rostorl may rely.<end>"
KO: "로스톨을 떠받치는 장군으로서 제네테스와 함께 활약하도록 해라.<end>"

## 510121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、肩が凝った。<line-break>大げさな式典だったな。<line-break>　<line-break>お互い、これから<line-break>めんどくさいことになるな。<line-break>それじゃあ。<end>"
EN: "Whew, my shoulders are stiff. That ceremony was a bit much. Looks like things are going to be a pain for both of us from now on. See you around.<end>"
KO: "어휴, 어깨 뻐근하네. 거창한 의식이었군. 우리 둘 다 앞으로 귀찮아지겠어. 그럼 이만.<end>"

## 510122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>報酬としてＸギア手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> received Ｘ Gea as a reward.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 보상으로 X기어를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 510123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>の<line-break>名声値が上がった。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>'s Fame increased.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>의 명성치가 올랐다.<end>"

## 510124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おらぬのか…。<end>"
EN: "Not here...?<end>"
KO: "없는 건가….<end>"

## 510125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "皇帝ネメアの世界統一構想のもと<line-break>南方攻略軍司令、朱雀将軍アンギルダンが　<line-break>ついにロストール攻略に動き出した。<end>"
EN: "Under Emperor Nemea's plan to unite the world, Angeerdan―the Vermilion Bird General and commander of the Southern Campaign Army―finally launched the Rostorl Campaign.<end>"
KO: "황제 네메아의 세계 통일 구상 아래 남방 공략군 사령관, 주작장군 앙길단이 마침내 로스톨 공략에 나섰다.<end>"

## 510126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大兵力の動員を予想したロストール側は<line-break>帝国軍が大量輸送の必要から、海路を採ると<line-break>予想し、その上陸地点を叩く作戦に出た。<end>"
EN: "Expecting a vast army to be mobilized, Rostorl's forces reasoned that the need for large-scale transport would force the Imperial Army to come by sea. They therefore planned to strike at its landing site.<end>"
KO: "대병력 동원을 예상한 로스톨 측은 제국군이 대량 수송을 위해 해로를 택할 것으로 보고 상륙 지점을 공격하는 작전을 세웠다.<end>"

## 510127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、アンギルダンは、少数の精兵で<line-break>分断の山脈を越え、大街道沿いの都市を<line-break>電撃的に次々と占領した。<line-break><line-break>分断の山脈を軍隊が越えたのは歴史上<line-break>いまだかつてない壮挙である。こうして、<line-break>帝国軍は王都ロストール攻略に王手をかけた。<end>"
EN: "Instead, Angeerdan led a small force of elite troops across the Dividing Mountains and seized one city after another along the Great Highway with lightning speed. Never before in history had an army crossed the Dividing Mountains. The Imperial Army was now poised to capture the royal capital and complete the Rostorl Campaign.<end>"
KO: "하지만 앙길단은 소수 정예로 분단의 산맥을 넘어 대가도 연선의 도시들을 전격적으로 잇달아 점령했다. 분단의 산맥을 군대가 넘은 것은 역사상 전례 없는 위업이다. 이렇게 제국군은 왕도 로스톨 공략에 장군을 걸었다.<end>"

## 510128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール軍総司令ノヴィン・ファーロスは、<line-break>これに対して、急遽、軍を反転させ、<line-break>アンギルダンを迎え撃つべく、急行した。<line-break><line-break>これは下策であった。急行したため<line-break>隊列も整わない疲労したロストール軍は<line-break>待ちかまえていた帝国軍に敗れ、壊滅した。<end>"
EN: "In response, Novin Pharos, commander-in-chief of Rostorl's army, hastily turned his forces around and rushed to intercept Angeerdan. It was a disastrous decision. Exhausted by the forced march and with its ranks still in disarray, the Rostorl army was crushed by the waiting Imperial forces.<end>"
KO: "로스톨군 총사령관 노빈 파로스는 이에 맞서 급히 군을 돌려 앙길단을 요격하려 서둘렀다. 그러나 이는 하책이었다. 급행한 탓에 대열조차 정비되지 않은 채 지친 로스톨군은 기다리고 있던 제국군에게 패해 괴멸했다.<end>"

## 510129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主…<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>戦場をひとりで死守する<line-break>ロストールの傭兵がおると聞いて<line-break>来てみれば、お主じゃったのか！<end>"
EN: "You... <value:$28>. I came after hearing of a Rostorl mercenary holding the battlefield alone, only to find it was you!<end>"
KO: "너… <value:$28>…. 전장을 혼자 지키는 로스톨 용병이 있다기에 와 봤더니, 너였나!<end>"

## 510130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これも戦場の習いじゃ。<line-break>いくぞ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Such is the way of the battlefield. Come, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "이것도 전장의 법칙이지. 간다, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 510131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦場をひとりで守る<line-break>ロストールの傭兵とはお主か！<end>"
EN: "So you are the Rostorl mercenary holding this battlefield alone!<end>"
KO: "전장을 혼자 지키는 로스톨의 용병이 자네인가!<end>"

## 510132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ抵抗をやめぬか…天晴れなり！<line-break>勇士よ！　名を名乗られよ！<end>"
EN: "Still you refuse to yield? Magnificent! Brave warrior, state your name!<end>"
KO: "아직 저항을 멈추지 않는가… 훌륭하도다! 용사여! 이름을 밝혀라!<end>"

## 510133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我はディンガル帝国の<line-break>朱雀将軍アンギルダン！<line-break>この遠征軍の総司令官である！　　　　<line-break><value:$28>とやら。<line-break>お主の名はロストールが滅びようとも<line-break>永遠に語りつがれようぞ。<end>"
EN: "I am Angeerdan, Vermilion Bird General of the Dyneskal Empire and supreme commander of this expeditionary force! <value:$28>, is it? Even should Rostorl fall, your name shall be remembered forever.<end>"
KO: "나는 딘갈 제국의 주작장군 앙길단! 이 원정군의 총사령관이다! <value:$28>라고 했나. 네 이름은 로스톨이 멸망해도 영원히 전해질 것이다.<end>"

## 510134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、まいるぞ！<line-break><value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Now, have at you, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "그럼 간다! <value:$28>!<end>"

## 510135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ガアアアアッ！<end>"
EN: "Graaaargh!<end>"
KO: "…가아아아악!<end>"

## 510136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅ…、ふぅ…わ、わしも<line-break>…ここで、死ぬわけにはゆかぬ。　　　<line-break>兵どもの命を背負っておるのでな。<line-break>手強き敵であった…。<line-break>わしの手で、介錯しよう。<line-break>奮戦へのせめてもの手向けじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Hah... Hah... I-I cannot die here. I bear the lives of my soldiers upon my shoulders. You were a formidable foe. I shall grant you a merciful death with my own hand―the least tribute I can offer for such a valiant fight.<end>"
KO: "후우… 후우… 나, 나도 여기서 죽을 수는 없다. 병사들의 목숨을 짊어지고 있으니. 강한 적이었다…. 내 손으로 편히 보내 주마. 분전한 데 대한 최소한의 전별이다.<end>"

## 510137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵襲！<end>"
EN: "Enemy attack!<end>"
KO: "적습!<end>"

## 510138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと！？　<line-break>味方が徴発に出払っているすきを<line-break>ついてくるとは！<end>"
EN: "What?! They struck while our troops were away carrying out requisitions!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고!? 아군이 징발하러 나간 틈을 노리다니!<end>"

## 510139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、あそこまで壊乱した<line-break>敗残兵をまとめられる者など<line-break>いるわけがない。誤報ではないのか？<end>"
EN: "But no one could possibly rally remnants routed that badly. Could the report be false?<end>"
KO: "하지만 그렇게까지 무너진 패잔병을 수습할 자가 있을 리 없다. 오보 아닌가?<end>"

## 510140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頭だけで考えると<line-break>現実が見えなくなるぜ！<end>"
EN: "Think with your head alone, and you'll lose sight of reality!<end>"
KO: "머리로만 생각하면 현실이 안 보인다고!<end>"

## 510141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテス。<line-break>まさか、お主が…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>いや、まさかなどということがあるか！<line-break>あの崩れ去った敗残兵をまとめる器量、<line-break>意表をつく、奇襲をやる才覚と度胸、<line-break>お主でなくて誰にある！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes... You? Impossible... No, why should it be? Who else could rally those shattered remnants? Who else has the cunning and nerve to launch an attack no one saw coming?<end>"
KO: "…제네테스. 설마 네가…. 아니, 설마라는 게 있을 리 있나! 무너져버린 패잔병을 규합할 그릇, 허를 찌르는 기습을 해낼 재능과 배짱, 네가 아니면 누가 있겠느냐!<end>"

## 510142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ！<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁん。<line-break>俺がファーロスのドラ息子だ！<line-break>悪いが、死んでもらう。<line-break>俺は今、ロストールの兵と<line-break>民を背負ってるんでな。<end>"
EN: "That's right, old man Angeerdan. I'm the worthless Pharos brat! Sorry, but you have to die. The soldiers and people of Rostorl are depending on me now.<end>"
KO: "그래! 앙길단 아저씨. 내가 파로스의 망나니 아들이다! 미안하지만 죽어 줘야겠어. 지금 나는 로스톨의 병사와 백성을 짊어지고 있으니까.<end>"

## 510143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしとて同じじゃ。<line-break>ディンガルの兵のために<line-break>お主を倒す！　ゆくぞ！<end>"
EN: "And I am no different. For the soldiers of Dyneskal, I shall defeat you! Come!<end>"
KO: "나 역시 마찬가지다. 딘갈의 병사들을 위해 네놈을 쓰러뜨리겠다! 간다!<end>"

## 510144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…、<line-break>運命の女神は残酷なものよ！<line-break>ここまで来て、わしを見限るとはな…。<line-break>ゼネテスよ…。<line-break>お主ほどの男が…ファーロスの…<line-break>跡取りだったとは…、うぐっ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh... The Goddess of Fate is cruel indeed! To forsake me now, after I have come so far... Xenetes... To think a man such as you was heir to House Pharos... Urgh...<end>"
KO: "후후… 운명의 여신은 잔혹하구나! 여기까지 와서 나를 버리다니…. 제네테스여…. 자네 같은 사내가… 파로스의… 후계자였다니… 윽…<end>"

## 510145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退け！<line-break>バラバラに地下王国を通るのだ！<line-break>みな…生き延びるの…じゃ…！<end>"
EN: "Retreat! Make your way through the Underground Kingdom separately! All of you... survive...!<end>"
KO: "물러나라! 뿔뿔이 지하왕국을 통과해라! 모두… 살아남아라…!<end>"

## 510146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朱雀将軍が討ち取られたぞ！<end>"
EN: "The Vermilion Bird General has fallen!<end>"
KO: "주작장군이 쓰러졌다!<end>"

## 510147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退け！　退けええい！<end>"
EN: "Retreat! Retreeeat!<end>"
KO: "물러나라! 물러나아아!<end>"

## 510148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくやったな。<line-break>やるじゃねえか。<end>"
EN: "Well done. Not bad at all.<end>"
KO: "잘했군. 제법인데.<end>"

## 510149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誇りのために軍を動かし、兵を死なせた<line-break>親父ノヴィン・ファーロスの尻拭いを<line-break>息子の俺がしてるってわけだ。<line-break>ロストールの大貴族にして総司令<line-break>ノヴィン・ファーロスの不肖の息子が<line-break>この俺、ゼネテス・ファーロスだ。<end>"
EN: "So here I am, cleaning up after my father, Novin Pharos, who marched an army off to satisfy his pride and got his soldiers killed. I'm Xenetes Pharos, the unworthy son of Novin Pharos, one of Rostorl's great nobles and its commander-in-chief.<end>"
KO: "자존심 때문에 군대를 움직여 병사를 죽게 한 아버지 노빈 파로스의 뒤치다꺼리를 아들인 내가 하는 셈이지. 로스톨의 대귀족이자 총사령관 노빈 파로스의 못난 아들이 바로 나, 제네테스 파로스다.<end>"

## 510150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はゼネテス・ファーロス。<line-break>ロストールの大貴族にして総司令<line-break>ノヴィン・ファーロスの不肖の息子だ。<line-break>誇りのために軍を動かし、兵を死なせた<line-break>親父の尻拭いをしてるのさ。<end>"
EN: "I'm Xenetes Pharos, the unworthy son of Novin Pharos, one of Rostorl's great nobles and its commander-in-chief. I'm cleaning up after my father, who marched an army off to satisfy his pride and got his soldiers killed.<end>"
KO: "난 제네테스 파로스. 로스톨의 대귀족이자 총사령 노빈 파로스의 못난 아들이다. 자존심 때문에 군을 움직이고 병사들을 죽게 한 아버지의 뒷수습을 하고 있지.<end>"

## 510151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less>50あんたのことは<line-break>聞いたことがある。<line-break>冒険者だな。<line-break>…<value:$28>だったっけ？<line-break>これから、よろしくな。<end>あんたのことは<line-break>聞いたことがある。<line-break>冒険者だな。<line-break>…<value:$2B>の<line-break><value:$28>だったっけ？<line-break>これから、よろしくな。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less>50I've heard about you. You're an adventurer, right? <value:$28>, wasn't it? Well, good to meet you.<end>I've heard about you. You're an adventurer, right? <value:$2B> <value:$28>, wasn't it? Well, good to meet you.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less>50네 얘기는 들어 본 적 있어. 모험가지. …<value:$28>이라고 했던가? 앞으로 잘 부탁해.<end>네 얘기는 들어 본 적 있어. 모험가지. …<value:$2B>의 <value:$28>이라고 했던가? 앞으로 잘 부탁해.<end>"

## 510152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンは万一の王都守備軍の速攻に備え、<line-break>付近の町に強制徴発の軍を差し向けた。<line-break>そのため中枢部が手薄になった。<end>"
EN: "To guard against a sudden attack by the royal capital's garrison, Angeerdan sent troops to carry out forced requisitions in nearby towns. This left the heart of his army thinly defended.<end>"
KO: "앙길단은 만일 왕도 수비군이 속공해 올 경우에 대비해 인근 마을에 강제 징발 부대를 보냈다. 그 때문에 중추부의 수비가 허술해졌다.<end>"

## 510153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その手薄になった中枢部を、<line-break>驚くべき早さで敗残兵を立て直した<line-break>ゼネテス・ファーロスが奇襲したのである。<line-break><line-break>ゼネテスの奇襲により、<line-break>朱雀将軍アンギルダンが討ち取られると、<line-break>帝国軍は壊乱し、敗走した。<end>"
EN: "After rallying the routed remnants with astonishing speed, Xenetes Pharos launched a surprise attack on the weakened heart of the army. When the Vermilion Bird General Angeerdan fell in Xenetes's assault, the Imperial Army collapsed into disorder and fled.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 허술해진 중추부를 놀라운 속도로 패잔병을 수습한 제네테스 파로스가 기습했다. 제네테스의 기습으로 주작장군 앙길단이 전사하자 제국군은 붕괴해 패주했다.<end>"

## 510154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この戦いで、ゼネテスの声望は<line-break>一気に上がり、奇跡の名将と呼ばれたが<line-break>ロストールの被害も甚大なものがあった。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes's renown soared after this battle, and he became known as a general who worked miracles. Rostorl, however, had suffered devastating losses.<end>"
KO: "이 전투로 제네테스의 명성은 단숨에 높아져 기적의 명장이라 불렸지만 로스톨의 피해도 막대했다.<end>"

## 510155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>は<line-break>報酬としてＸギア手に入れた。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28> received Ｘ Gea as a reward.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>은 보상으로 X기어를 손에 넣었다.<end>"

## 510156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>の<line-break>名声値が上がった。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>'s Fame increased.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>의 명성치가 올랐다.<end>"

## 510157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば、用意をすませてから、<line-break>もう一度ここに来るがよい。<line-break>傭兵の集合場所に案内してやる。<end>"
EN: "Then finish your preparations and return here. I will take you to the mercenaries' assembly point.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 준비를 마친 뒤 다시 여기로 와라. 용병 집결지로 안내해 주지.<end>"

## 510160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec051-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この戦いで総司令ノヴィン・ファーロスを<line-break>はじめとする貴族たちも、多く命を落とした。<line-break>　<line-break>それは、ロストールの兵たちにとって<line-break>さらなる地獄の始まりを意味した。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Many nobles also lost their lives in the battle, among them Commander-in-Chief Novin Pharos. For Rostorl's soldiers, it marked the beginning of yet another hell.<end>"
KO: "이 전투로 총사령 노빈 파로스를 비롯한 귀족들도 많이 목숨을 잃었다. 그것은 로스톨 병사들에게 더한 지옥의 시작을 의미했다.<end>"
