## 520001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おい、放浪者！<line-break>よいところへ来た。来い！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>エリスが緊急召集をかけてきた。<line-break>…どうやら、<line-break>ディンガルが動き出すらしい。<end>"
EN: "Hey, wanderer! Good timing. Come here! Eris has called an emergency meeting. It sounds like Dyneskal is making its move.<end>"
KO: "어이, 방랑자! 잘 왔다. 이리 와! 에리스가 긴급 소집을 걸었다. …아무래도 딘갈이 움직이기 시작한 모양이야.<end>"

## 520002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリエナイ公レムオン様と<line-break>ノーブル伯<value:$28>様が<line-break>おいでです、総司令閣下。<end>"
EN: "Duke Lemghon of Elienai and Count <value:$28> of Noble have arrived, Commander-in-Chief.<end>"
KO: "엘리에나이 공 레무온 님과 노블 백작 <value:$28>님께서 오셨습니다, 총사령관 각하.<end>"

## 520003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…仕方ない、では<line-break>始めるとしよう。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Let us begin.<end>"
KO: "…어쩔 수 없군. 그럼 시작하지.<end>"

## 520004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうやら、近く、<line-break>ディンガルが攻めてくるらしい。<end>"
EN: "It appears Dyneskal will attack soon.<end>"
KO: "아무래도 곧 딘갈이 쳐들어올 모양이야.<end>"

## 520005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "根拠は？<end>"
EN: "What evidence do we have?<end>"
KO: "근거는?<end>"

## 520006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "間者の報告。<line-break>兵、兵糧の備蓄。<end>"
EN: "Reports from our spies. Troops are being assembled, and provisions stockpiled.<end>"
KO: "첩자의 보고. 병력과 군량 비축.<end>"

## 520007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なるほど。<end>"
EN: "I see.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군.<end>"

## 520008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵将はディンガル帝国南方攻略軍総司令官、<line-break>朱雀将軍アンギルダンだ。<line-break>　<line-break>残念ながら、作戦、進軍路などは不明である。<line-break>だが、敵もこの大国ロストールに挑むからには<line-break>並々ならぬ決意で攻め来ることが予想される。<end>"
EN: "The enemy commander is Angeerdan, the Vermilion Bird General and commander-in-chief of the Dyneskal Empire's southern campaign army. Unfortunately, we know nothing of the enemy's plans or route of advance. But in challenging a great power such as Rostorl, the enemy will surely come with extraordinary resolve.<end>"
KO: "적장은 딘갈 제국 남방공략군 총사령관, 주작장군 앙길단이다. 유감스럽게도 작전과 진군로 등은 불명이다. 하지만 적도 이 대국 로스톨에 도전하는 이상 보통 각오로 공격해 오지는 않을 것이다.<end>"

## 520009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おそらく大軍で押し寄せるであろう<line-break>ディンガル帝国軍に対し、我らロストールも<line-break>全力をもって対抗せねばならない。<end>"
EN: "The Dyneskal imperial forces will likely descend upon us in great numbers. Rostorl must meet them with all the strength at our command.<end>"
KO: "아마 대군으로 밀려올 딘갈 제국군에 맞서 우리 로스톨도 전력을 다해 대항해야 한다.<end>"

## 520010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なるほど…、わかりました。<line-break>陛下、迎撃軍の指揮は私めにお任せを。<end>"
EN: "Understood. Your Majesty, entrust me with command of the force sent to meet them.<end>"
KO: "과연… 알겠습니다. 폐하, 요격군의 지휘는 제게 맡겨 주십시오.<end>"

## 520011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "経験のない若造は引っ込んでいるがよい。<line-break>ロストール軍総司令であるわしが指揮をとる。<end>"
EN: "An inexperienced whelp should stand aside. I am commander-in-chief of Rostorl's army, and I shall take command.<end>"
KO: "경험 없는 애송이는 빠져 있어라. 로스톨군 총사령인 내가 지휘한다.<end>"

## 520012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実戦経験のないのは閣下も同じ。<line-break>ロストールはずっと平和でしたから。　<line-break>　<line-break>先の戦は先王フェロヒア様が<line-break>魔王バロル治めるディンガルに進攻した<line-break>２０年も前になろうという負け戦だ。<end>"
EN: "You have no experience in battle either, Commander-in-Chief. Rostorl has long been at peace. Our last war was the failed invasion of Dyneskal, then ruled by Balor, Demon King, led by the late King Ferohia nearly twenty years ago.<end>"
KO: "실전 경험이 없는 건 각하도 마찬가지입니다. 로스톨은 계속 평화로웠으니까요. 마지막 전쟁은 선왕 페로히아 님께서 마왕 발로르가 다스리던 딩갈을 침공했다가 패한, 벌써 20년 전의 일입니다.<end>"

## 520013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたはそれに参加なさらなかった。<line-break>だからこそ、先王が亡くなられたほどの<line-break>戦のあとでも生きておられる。<end>"
EN: "You did not take part. That is precisely why you survived a war that claimed even the late king.<end>"
KO: "당신은 거기에 참가하지 않았습니다. 그렇기에 선왕이 돌아가실 정도의 전쟁 뒤에도 살아 있는 것이지요.<end>"

## 520014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…エリエナイ公、無礼であろう。<line-break>こ、言葉を慎まれよ。<end>"
EN: "Duke of Elienai, that was insolent. M-mind your words.<end>"
KO: "…에리에나이 공, 무례하오. 마, 말을 삼가시오.<end>"

## 520015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この作戦の指揮は総司令であるわしがとり、<line-break>我が息子が副司令となる。<end>"
EN: "As commander-in-chief, I shall command this operation. My son will serve as deputy commander.<end>"
KO: "이 작전의 지휘는 총사령인 내가 맡고, 내 아들이 부사령이 된다.<end>"

## 520016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、何かと思えば！　　　　　<line-break>まったく、あきれかえる。<line-break>　<line-break>今回の戦はファーロスのドラ息子殿に<line-break>手柄を立てさせるための<line-break>戦だったのですか？<end>"
EN: "Good grief, so that is what this is about! How utterly absurd. Is this war merely an opportunity for the spoiled son of Pharos to win himself some glory?<end>"
KO: "이런, 뭔가 했더니! 정말 어이가 없군. 이번 전쟁은 파로스의 망나니 아드님께 공을 세워 주기 위한 전쟁이었습니까?<end>"

## 520017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙れ！　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Silence!<end>"
KO: "닥쳐!<end>"

## 520018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そういえば、ファーロスの御曹司には<line-break>めったに宮廷に来られぬゆえ、<line-break>まだ一度もお目にかかったことがない。<line-break>おられるなら、教えていただきたい。<line-break>総司令閣下、ご子息は、いったい<line-break>どこにおられるのです？<end>"
EN: "Come to think of it, the young heir of Pharos appears at court so rarely that I have never met him. If he is here, perhaps you could point him out. Commander-in-Chief, just where is your son?<end>"
KO: "그러고 보니 파로스 가문의 도련님은 좀처럼 궁정에 오지 않으셔서 아직 한 번도 뵌 적이 없습니다. 계신다면 알려 주십시오. 총사령관 각하, 아드님은 대체 어디에 계십니까?<end>"

## 520019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まだ…来ておらぬ。　　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "He has not... arrived yet.<end>"
KO: "…아직… 오지 않았다.<end>"

## 520020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール王国の大事に<line-break>副司令殿は遅刻か！？　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>フッ、あきれはてて<line-break>ものも言えませんな。<line-break>ノヴィン・ファーロス総司令閣下？<end>"
EN: "The deputy commander is late to a council concerning the very fate of the Kingdom of Rostorl? Hmph. I am quite lost for words, Commander-in-Chief Novin Pharos.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 왕국의 중대한 일에 부사령관께서는 지각입니까!? 훗, 어이가 없어서 말도 안 나오는군요. 노빈 파로스 총사령관 각하?<end>"

## 520021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "総司令閣下、ご令息である副司令閣下<line-break>ゼネテス・ファーロス閣下、お越しです。<end>"
EN: "Commander-in-Chief, your son, Deputy Commander Xenetes Pharos, has arrived.<end>"
KO: "총사령관 각하, 영식이신 부사령관 각하 제네테스 파로스 각하께서 오셨습니다.<end>"

## 520022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ。…ヒック。<line-break>皆さんおそろいで。へへ…。<end>"
EN: "Hey. Hic. Everyone's here, I see. Heh...<end>"
KO: "여어. …딸꾹. 다들 모였네. 헤헤….<end>"

## 520023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、まさか…、<line-break>…泥酔して…おいでなのか？<end>"
EN: "S-surely not... Are you completely drunk?<end>"
KO: "서, 설마… 완전히 취하신 겁니까?<end>"

## 520024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ。奇遇だな。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Hey. Fancy meeting you here, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "여어. 우연이군, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 520025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへ…へへ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh... heh...<end>"
KO: "헤헤… 헤헤….<end>"

## 520026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール王国存亡に関わる会議に<line-break>遅刻するだけではなく<line-break>あまつさえ、泥酔して現れるとは…！<line-break>このあきれはてた所行！<line-break>ファーロス家は王国をないがしろに<line-break>しているのではないかと思われますが？<end>"
EN: "Not only do you arrive late to a council upon which Rostorl's very survival depends, you appear blind drunk! Such disgraceful conduct suggests that House Pharos holds the kingdom itself in contempt. Would you not agree?<end>"
KO: "로스톨 왕국의 존망이 걸린 회의에 늦은 것도 모자라 만취해서 나타나다니…! 이 어처구니없는 행동! 파로스 가문은 왕국을 가볍게 여기는 것 아닙니까?<end>"

## 520027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ。お初にお目にかかる。<end>"
EN: "Hey. Pleased to meet you.<end>"
KO: "여. 처음 뵙겠습니다.<end>"

## 520028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less>50俺はゼネテス・ファーロス、<line-break>ドラ息子だ。<line-break>よろしくな。<line-break>あんたが<value:$28>だな。<line-break>評判は聞いてるぜ。<end>俺はゼネテス・ファーロス<line-break>ドラ息子だ。<line-break>よろしくな。<line-break>あんたが<value:$28>だな。<line-break>評判は聞いてるぜ、<line-break><value:$2B>さん。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less>50I'm Xenetes Pharos, the spoiled son. Good to meet you. You're <value:$28>, right? I've heard about you.<end>I'm Xenetes Pharos, the spoiled son. Good to meet you. You're <value:$28>, right? I've heard about you, <value:$2B>.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less>50나는 제네테스 파로스, 망나니 아들이야. 잘 부탁해. 네가 <value:$28>구나. 소문은 들었어.<end>나는 제네테스 파로스, 망나니 아들이야. 잘 부탁해. 네가 <value:$28>구나. 소문은 들었어, <value:$2B>씨.<end>"

## 520029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "と、とにかく、ロストール王国の危機に<line-break>このていたらくとは！　総司令閣下！<line-break>あまりと言えばあまりではないですか？<end>"
EN: "I-in any event, for him to behave this way while the Kingdom of Rostorl is in peril! Commander-in-Chief, surely this goes beyond all bounds?<end>"
KO: "아, 아무튼 로스톨 왕국의 위기에 이런 꼴이라니! 총사령관 각하! 아무리 그래도 너무한 것 아닙니까?<end>"

## 520030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帰れ、ゼネテス！<line-break>体面が悪い！　帰るのだ。<end>"
EN: "Go home, Xenetes! You are disgracing us. Go!<end>"
KO: "돌아가라, 제네테스! 체면이 서지 않는다! 돌아가는 거다.<end>"

## 520031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "副司令だからって<line-break>無理矢理連れてきておいて…　　　<line-break>　<line-break>来てみれば、いきなり帰れじゃ<line-break>帰れませんよ、父上。<line-break>会議に加えさせてもらいましょう。<end>"
EN: "You dragged me here because I'm deputy commander, and now that I've come, you immediately tell me to leave? I can't do that, Father. I'll be joining the council.<end>"
KO: "부사령관이라고 억지로 데려와 놓고… 와 보니 갑자기 돌아가라니 못 돌아갑니다, 아버지. 회의에 끼게 해 주십시오.<end>"

## 520032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、あきれ果てた茶番だ。　　<line-break>私も忙しい身なので、<line-break>本日は退出させていただきたい。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. What an appalling farce. I am a busy man, so I believe I shall take my leave for today.<end>"
KO: "흥, 정말 어처구니없는 연극이군. 나도 바쁜 몸이라 오늘은 이만 물러나고 싶네.<end>"

## 520033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリエナイ公、そなたには王都守備軍の<line-break>指揮をとってもらわねばならぬ。<line-break>作戦会議には参加していただこう。<end>"
EN: "Duke of Elienai, you are to command the capital's defense force. You must remain for the council.<end>"
KO: "에리에나이 공, 자네에게는 왕도 수비군을 지휘해 줘야 하네. 작전회의에는 참가해 주게.<end>"

## 520034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、総司令閣下は<line-break>必勝の策をもって、<line-break>出撃されるのではないのですかな？　　<line-break>閣下が負けるのでなければ<line-break>王都守備など不要なはず。…それとも、<line-break>敗北の公算の方が高いのですかな？<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Surely the Commander-in-Chief marches with an infallible plan for victory? If you do not lose, there should be no need to defend the capital. Or do you consider defeat the likelier outcome?<end>"
KO: "후, 총사령관 각하는 필승의 계책을 가지고 출격하시는 것 아닙니까? 각하가 패하지 않는다면 왕도 수비 따윈 필요 없을 텐데…. 아니면 패배할 가능성이 더 높습니까?<end>"

## 520035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何をバカな！　貴殿の手など借りぬ。<line-break>さっさと帰られるといい、<line-break>エリエナイ公！<end>"
EN: "What nonsense! I need no help from you. You may leave at once, Duke of Elienai!<end>"
KO: "무슨 바보 같은 소리냐! 귀공의 손 따윈 빌리지 않겠다. 얼른 돌아가시오, 엘리에나이 공!<end>"

## 520036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、閣下がそうおっしゃるなら、<line-break>戻るとしましょう。<line-break>　<line-break>さて、ノーブル伯、<line-break>卿はどうなされるのかな？<line-break>愚者の会議から一緒に戻られるか？<end>"
EN: "Hmph. If that is the Commander's wish, I shall go. Now then, Count of Noble, what will you do? Will you leave this council of fools with me?<end>"
KO: "훗, 각하께서 그렇게 말씀하신다면 돌아가도록 하지요. 자, 노블 백작. 경은 어찌하시겠습니까? 어리석은 자들의 회의에서 함께 돌아가시겠습니까?<end>"

## 520037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戻る<end>"
EN: "Leave<end>"
KO: "돌아가기<end>"

## 520038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戻らない<end>"
EN: "Stay<end>"
KO: "돌아가지 않는다<end>"

## 520039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、私と<value:$28>は<line-break>これにて退出いたします。<line-break>失礼。<end>"
EN: "Then <value:$28> and I shall take our leave. Excuse us.<end>"
KO: "그럼 저와 <value:$28>은 이만 물러가겠습니다. 실례합니다.<end>"

## 520040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、国の大事だというのに<line-break>ファーロス家のごり押しには<line-break>まいったものだ。<line-break>まったくの茶番だった。<line-break>それではな。<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. The nation faces a crisis, yet House Pharos still forces its own way. The entire affair was a farce. Farewell, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "흥, 나라의 중대사인데 파로스 가문의 억지가 대단하군. 완전히 촌극이었어. 그럼 이만, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 520041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…勝手にするがいい！<end>"
EN: "Do as you please!<end>"
KO: "…마음대로 해라!<end>"

## 520042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "余も疲れた。<line-break>余も退出させてもらおう。<end>"
EN: "I too am weary. I shall take my leave.<end>"
KO: "짐도 피곤하구나. 짐도 물러나겠다.<end>"

## 520043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "陛下、お供いたします。<end>"
EN: "Your Majesty, I shall accompany you.<end>"
KO: "폐하, 모시겠습니다.<end>"

## 520044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃がおらねば、らちがあくまい。<line-break>余のような用なしとはわけが違う。<end>"
EN: "Nothing will be settled without the queen. Unlike me, you are not superfluous here.<end>"
KO: "왕비가 없으면 일이 풀리지 않겠군. 짐 같은 쓸모없는 자와는 다르다.<end>"

## 520045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…夫を気遣うのは<line-break>妻の役目でございます。<end>"
EN: "It is a wife's duty to care for her husband.<end>"
KO: "…남편을 걱정하는 것은 아내의 역할입니다.<end>"

## 520046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王国の実権を握るような<line-break>お偉い王妃様に気遣ってもらえるとは<line-break>余も大した幸せ者じゃ。<line-break>フフフ…そうではないか？<line-break>余には過ぎたことよ。<line-break>…余にかまうな、エリス。<end>"
EN: "What a fortunate man I am, to receive the concern of so exalted a queen―the woman who holds the kingdom's true power. Heh... Would you not agree? It is more than I deserve. Leave me be, Eris.<end>"
KO: "왕국의 실권을 쥔 높으신 왕비께서 신경 써 주시다니 짐도 참 행복한 사람이구나. 후후후… 그렇지 않느냐? 짐에게는 과분한 일이다. …짐에게 신경 쓰지 마라, 에리스.<end>"

## 520047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…陛下。<end>"
EN: "Your Majesty...<end>"
KO: "…폐하.<end>"

## 520048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うぉほん、では作戦の説明をする。<line-break>おい、副官！　説明せよ！<end>"
EN: "Ahem. Now for the explanation of our plan. Adjutant! Explain it!<end>"
KO: "크흠, 그럼 작전을 설명하겠다. 어이, 부관! 설명해라!<end>"

## 520049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はっ、では説明いたします。　　　<line-break>地図をご覧ください。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Yes, sir. Allow me to explain. Please look at the map.<end>"
KO: "예, 그럼 설명드리겠습니다. 지도를 봐 주십시오.<end>"

## 520050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帝国軍は大量の兵員と兵糧、物資を<line-break>輸送する必要から、<line-break>海路を利用すると考えられます。<end>"
EN: "The imperial army must transport vast numbers of troops, provisions, and supplies. We therefore believe it will travel by sea.<end>"
KO: "제국군은 대량의 병력과 군량, 물자를 수송해야 하므로 해로를 이용할 것으로 보입니다.<end>"

## 520051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我々は南岸地帯に主軍を駐屯させ、<line-break>敵が上陸に使用可能地点を重点的に警戒し、<line-break>敵を上陸時に急襲、殲滅します。<end>"
EN: "We will station our main force along the southern coast and closely watch every viable landing point. We will strike as the enemy comes ashore and annihilate it.<end>"
KO: "우리는 남안 지역에 주력군을 주둔시키고 적이 상륙할 수 있는 지점을 중점적으로 경계하여 상륙 시 급습해 섬멸합니다.<end>"

## 520052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "以上です。　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>何か質問は？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "That concludes the briefing. Any questions?<end>"
KO: "이상입니다. 질문 있습니까?<end>"

## 520053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帝国軍が内海を縦断するという<line-break>その根拠は？<end>"
EN: "What is your basis for assuming the imperial army will cross the Inland Sea?<end>"
KO: "제국군이 내해를 종단한다는 근거는 무엇이지?<end>"

## 520054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何を言い出すのだ、ゼネテス。<line-break>常識だ！　他に考えられまい！<end>"
EN: "W-what are you talking about, Xenetes? It is obvious! There can be no other route!<end>"
KO: "무, 무슨 말을 하는 거냐, 제네테스. 상식이다! 달리 생각할 수 없지!<end>"

## 520055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦争は常識でするもんじゃない。<line-break>説明を求める。<end>"
EN: "You don't wage war on common sense. I want an explanation.<end>"
KO: "전쟁은 상식으로 하는 게 아니야. 설명을 요구한다.<end>"

## 520056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大量の兵士、兵糧、物資を輸送するためには<line-break>海路でなければ、ドワーフの地下王国を<line-break>通る方法しか考えられませんが…<line-break>ドワーフの地下王国は人間の勢力争いには<line-break>まったくの中立を表明しております。<line-break>軍隊が通行することを許しておりません。<end>"
EN: "To transport so many soldiers, provisions, and supplies, the only alternative to the sea would be passage through the Dwarven Underground Kingdom. But the Dwarves have declared complete neutrality in human struggles for power. They permit no armies to pass.<end>"
KO: "대량의 병사와 식량, 물자를 수송하려면 해로가 아니면 드워프의 지하왕국을 통과하는 방법밖에 생각할 수 없습니다만… 드워프 지하왕국은 인간 세력의 다툼에 완전한 중립을 표명하고 있습니다. 군대의 통행을 허용하지 않습니다.<end>"

## 520057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そのため、海路しか<line-break>大量の兵士、兵糧、物資を輸送する　　　　<line-break>手段はなく…<end>"
EN: "Thus, the sea is the only means of transporting so many soldiers, provisions, and supplies...<end>"
KO: "그래서 대량의 병사와 군량, 물자를 운송할 수단은 해로밖에 없고…<end>"

## 520058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺が聞きたいのは、<line-break>なんで大軍で攻めてくると<line-break>思ったのか、だ。<end>"
EN: "What I'm asking is why you think they're coming with a huge army.<end>"
KO: "…내가 묻고 싶은 건 왜 대군으로 공격해 올 거라 생각했냐는 거다.<end>"

## 520059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は何を言いたいのだ？<line-break>もういい！<line-break>やる気がないなら、口を出すな！<end>"
EN: "What are you trying to say? Enough! If you have no intention of helping, keep out of this!<end>"
KO: "넌 대체 무슨 말을 하고 싶은 거냐? 됐다! 할 생각이 없다면 입 다물어!<end>"

## 520060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺がアンギルダンのとっつぁんなら<line-break>こうは動かない。<line-break>　<line-break>ロストールには十分な国力がある。<line-break>上陸しそうなところを見張ってて<line-break>叩くのが可能なのはすぐわかるからな。<end>"
EN: "If I were old Angeerdan, I wouldn't do it this way. Rostorl has ample strength. He'll know we can watch the likely landing sites and hit him there.<end>"
KO: "내가 앙길단 영감이라면 이렇게 움직이진 않아. 로스톨에는 충분한 국력이 있어. 상륙할 법한 곳을 지켜보다가 치는 게 가능하다는 건 금방 알 수 있으니까.<end>"

## 520061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、敵はどう出ると？<end>"
EN: "Then what do you believe the enemy will do?<end>"
KO: "그럼 적은 어떻게 나올까요?<end>"

## 520062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それほど大軍でない精兵で<line-break>分断の山脈の上を越える！<end>"
EN: "They'll cross the Dividing Mountains with a smaller force of elite troops!<end>"
KO: "그리 대군이 아닌 정예병으로 분단의 산맥 위를 넘는다!<end>"

## 520063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカな！<line-break>第一、そんなこと、前例がない！<end>"
EN: "Absurd! There is no precedent for such a thing!<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼! 애초에 그런 일은 전례가 없어!<end>"

## 520064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なら、前例になればいい。<line-break>説明する。<end>"
EN: "Then they'll set the precedent. Let me explain.<end>"
KO: "그럼 전례가 되면 된다. 설명하지.<end>"

## 520065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まずは、これ見よがしに<line-break>兵糧を用意したり、情報を流し、<line-break>俺らを南岸へ出撃させる。<end>"
EN: "First, they'll make a show of stockpiling provisions and leak information to lure us down to the southern coast.<end>"
KO: "우선 일부러 보란 듯이 군량을 준비하고 정보를 흘려서 우리를 남쪽 해안으로 출격하게 만든다.<end>"

## 520066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "帝国軍はそのスキに、<line-break>分断の山脈を越えて、ドワーフの<line-break>大街道沿いの都市を占領してしまう。　<end>"
EN: "While we're occupied there, the imperial army will cross the Dividing Mountains and seize the cities along the great Dwarven highway.<end>"
KO: "제국군은 그 틈에 분단의 산맥을 넘어 드워프 대로변의 도시를 점령해 버린다.<end>"

## 520067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうすれば、王都まで、<line-break>ドワーフが作ったご立派な大街道で<line-break>なんの障害もなく一直線だ。<end>"
EN: "From there, that magnificent Dwarven highway gives them a straight, unobstructed road to the capital.<end>"
KO: "그러면 왕도까지 드워프가 만든 훌륭한 대로를 따라 아무 장애도 없이 곧장 갈 수 있어.<end>"

## 520068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これで、王都ロストールは、<line-break>のどもとに剣を突きつけられたも同然、<line-break>チェックメイトだ。<line-break>さっきそろえた兵員も物資も兵糧も<line-break>そのあとで、ドワーフの地下王国を<line-break>ゆっくりと、通しゃいい。<end>"
EN: "That puts a sword to the throat of the capital, Rostorl. Checkmate. Afterward, they can take all those troops, supplies, and provisions they've assembled and move them leisurely through the Dwarven Underground Kingdom.<end>"
KO: "이걸로 왕도 로스톨은 목에 칼을 들이댄 거나 마찬가지, 체크메이트다. 아까 모은 병력과 물자와 군량은 그 뒤에 드워프 지하왕국을 천천히 통과시키면 돼.<end>"

## 520069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兵隊が隊列そろえてこそ軍隊だ。<line-break>連中がバラバラに通行するのを<line-break>とめはしないだろうぜ。<line-break>俺たちが一杯食わされたって<line-break>戻ってきたときには、俺たちを迎え撃つ<line-break>準備は万端整ってるって寸法だ。<end>"
EN: "Soldiers are only an army when they march in formation. The Dwarves probably won't stop them passing through separately. By the time we realize we've been fooled and make it back, the enemy will be fully prepared to meet us.<end>"
KO: "병사들이 대열을 맞춰야 군대지. 녀석들이 뿔뿔이 지나가는 걸 막진 않을 거야. 우리가 당했다는 걸 알고 돌아왔을 때는 우리를 맞아칠 준비가 완벽하게 끝나 있다는 계산이지.<end>"

## 520070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺らが戻ってくる気配がないんなら、<line-break>そのまま、ロストールを落とせばいい。<line-break>王都さえ落ちれば向こう様のもんだ。<line-break>ロストールは貴族制の国だ。<line-break>王を失った貴族たちが分裂して、<line-break>たくさんの小国が乱立することになる。<end>"
EN: "If we show no sign of returning, they can simply take Rostorl. Once the capital falls, they've won. Rostorl is an aristocratic state. Without the king, the nobles will split apart and leave the land divided into a host of petty kingdoms.<end>"
KO: "우리가 돌아올 기미가 없으면 그대로 로스톨을 함락시키면 돼. 왕도만 떨어지면 저쪽 승리야. 로스톨은 귀족제 국가다. 왕을 잃은 귀족들이 갈라져 수많은 소국으로 난립하게 되겠지.<end>"

## 520071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとは、それをひとつずつ<line-break>たんねんに各個撃破していけばいい。　<line-break>ま、そういう筋書きだな。<end>"
EN: "Then they can carefully destroy those kingdoms one by one. That's how the plan would go.<end>"
KO: "그다음엔 그것들을 하나씩 차근차근 각개격파하면 돼. 뭐, 그런 시나리오지.<end>"

## 520072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんな…、考えられない…！<end>"
EN: "That's... unthinkable!<end>"
KO: "그럴 수가…, 믿을 수 없어…!<end>"

## 520073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "考えられないところを突くから<line-break>一番、効果があるんだよ。<end>"
EN: "That's precisely why it would work so well.<end>"
KO: "생각지도 못한 곳을 찌르니까 가장 효과적인 거야.<end>"

## 520074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、大軍でないにしろ、<line-break>都市をひとつ落とせるだけの軍隊が<line-break>兵糧を持って山脈を越えられるものか！<end>"
EN: "Even if it is not a vast army, could a force large enough to capture a city carry its provisions across the mountains?<end>"
KO: "하지만 대군이 아니라고 해도 도시 하나를 함락시킬 병력이 식량까지 들고 산맥을 넘을 수 있겠나!<end>"

## 520075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "必要最低限の兵糧だけ持たせて<line-break>危機感をつのらせ、<line-break>兵に全力を出させるんです。<line-break>そのあとは、現地調達、<line-break>もっと洗練された言葉で言えば<line-break>略奪でまかなうことになる…。<end>"
EN: "Give the troops only the bare minimum of provisions. The danger will force them to give everything they have. After that, they live off the land―or, to use the more refined term, they pillage.<end>"
KO: "최소한의 식량만 주어 위기감을 높이고 병사들이 전력을 다하게 하는 겁니다. 그 다음은 현지 조달, 더 세련된 말로 하면 약탈로 충당하게 되죠….<end>"

## 520076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "略奪？<line-break>そんな下劣で非道なことを！<end>"
EN: "Pillage? Such depravity! Such inhumanity!<end>"
KO: "약탈? 그런 비열하고 잔혹한 짓을!<end>"

## 520077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦争は手段を選びません。<end>"
EN: "War does not allow us to pick and choose our methods.<end>"
KO: "전쟁은 수단을 가리지 않습니다.<end>"

## 520078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス、お前は…、<line-break>なんと恐ろしく、不愉快なことを　　<line-break>言うのだ！<line-break>わしには、お前が言うような<line-break>悪魔のごとき作戦など思いもつかん。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes, what a horrifying and repugnant thing to say! I could never conceive of a devilish plan like the one you describe.<end>"
KO: "제네테스, 넌… 어찌 그리 무섭고 불쾌한 말을 하는 것이냐! 내겐 네가 말하는 악마 같은 작전은 생각조차 나지 않는다.<end>"

## 520079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵もそんな下劣なことをするような<line-break>連中とは考えられない。<line-break>当初の作戦で上陸する敵を叩く！<end>"
EN: "Nor can I believe the enemy capable of anything so vile. We will proceed with the original plan and strike them as they land!<end>"
KO: "적도 그런 비열한 짓을 할 놈들이라고는 생각되지 않아. 당초 작전대로 상륙하는 적을 친다!<end>"

## 520080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分が見たいような世界しか　　　<line-break>見る気がないようだな…。<line-break>やれやれ。<line-break>みすみす、自分が殺されるやり方で<line-break>攻めてくる奴がいるわけがない。<end>"
EN: "It seems you'll only see the world the way you want it to be. Good grief. No one is going to attack in a way that plainly gets them killed.<end>"
KO: "자기가 보고 싶은 세상만 보려는 모양이군…. 이런. 뻔히 죽을 방식으로 공격해 오는 녀석이 있을 리 없잖아.<end>"

## 520081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "親父は自分の世界観のためなら、<line-break>いくら人が死んでも<line-break>かまわないようだな。<end>"
EN: "Seems Father doesn't care how many people die, so long as his view of the world stays intact.<end>"
KO: "아버지는 자기 세계관을 위해서라면 사람이 얼마나 죽어도 상관없다는 모양이군.<end>"

## 520082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けてくれてありがとよ。<line-break><value:$28>…。<line-break>　<line-break>…さて、このまま、<line-break>親父の策で行くことになると<line-break>兵や民がかなり死ぬことになる。<end>"
EN: "Thanks for backing me up, <value:$28>. Now, if we go ahead with Father's plan, a great many soldiers and civilians are going to die.<end>"
KO: "구해 줘서 고맙다, <value:$28>…. …자, 이대로 아버지의 계책대로 가면 병사와 백성이 상당히 죽게 된다.<end>"

## 520083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それだけは避けなきゃならん。<line-break>面倒だが、なんか別の策を　　　　<line-break>練っておかなきゃな。<end>"
EN: "We have to prevent that. Troublesome as it is, I'll have to come up with another plan.<end>"
KO: "그것만은 피해야 해. 귀찮지만 다른 방책을 좀 마련해 둬야겠군.<end>"

## 520084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、お前さんが<line-break>味方にいてくれたってのが　　<line-break>せめてもの救いだな。<line-break><value:$28>、<line-break>戦の時には、俺の陣に来てくれ。<line-break>考えがある。<end>"
EN: "At least I've got you on my side. That's some comfort. <value:$28>, come to my camp when the war begins. I have an idea.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 네가 우리 편이라는 게 그나마 다행이군. <value:$28>, 전쟁 때는 내 진영으로 와 줘. 생각이 있어.<end>"

## 520085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストールの総司令ノヴィン・ファーロスは、　<line-break>ディンガル軍が海路を進軍すると予測し、<line-break>これを迎え撃つべく軍を進めた。<end>"
EN: "Novin Pharos, commander-in-chief of Rostorl, predicted that the Dyneskal Army would advance by sea and marched out to intercept it.<end>"
KO: "로스톨의 총사령관 노빈 파로스는 딩갈군이 해로로 진군할 것이라 예측하고 이를 맞아 싸우기 위해 군을 움직였다.<end>"

## 520086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、ディンガルの将アンギルダンは<line-break>海路を進軍する方法を採らず、<line-break>精兵を率いて、分断の山脈を越える道を選んだ。<end>"
EN: "But Angeerdan, the Dyneskal commander, rejected the sea route. Leading a force of elite troops, he chose instead to cross the Dividing Mountains.<end>"
KO: "하지만 딩갈의 장군 앙길단은 해로로 진군하는 방법을 택하지 않고 정예병을 이끌어 분단의 산맥을 넘는 길을 선택했다.<end>"

## 520087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "分断の山脈を越えて、<line-break>大陸の別の側を攻める戦法は、<line-break>いまだかつてない壮挙であった。　　　　　　　<line-break><line-break>アンギルダンは、電撃的に<line-break>大街道沿いの都市を攻撃、次々と占領。<line-break>王都ロストール攻略に王手をかけた。<end>"
EN: "Crossing the Dividing Mountains to attack the far side of the continent was an unprecedented feat. Angeerdan launched a lightning offensive against the cities along the great highway, capturing them one after another. The Rostorl Campaign was now one move from victory.<end>"
KO: "분단의 산맥을 넘어 대륙 반대편을 공격하는 전법은 전례 없는 장거였다. 앙길단은 전격적으로 대가도 연선의 도시들을 공격해 차례로 점령했고, 왕도 로스톨 공략을 눈앞에 두었다.<end>"

## 520088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "対して、ノヴィン率いるロストール軍は　　　　<line-break>大街道のアンギルダンを迎え撃つべく<line-break>その大軍を急行させた。<end>"
EN: "In response, the vast Rostorl army under Novin hurried to intercept Angeerdan on the great highway.<end>"
KO: "이에 노빈이 이끄는 로스톨군은 대가도의 앙길단을 맞아 싸우기 위해 대군을 급히 이동시켰다.<end>"

## 520089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ。<line-break>お待たせ。<end>"
EN: "Hey. Sorry to keep you waiting.<end>"
KO: "왔어. 기다렸지.<end>"

## 520090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、俺が貴族の息子だとは　　　　　<line-break>お前さんには<line-break>言ってなかったかもな。<line-break>だが、お前さん、<line-break>俺が貴族の…ファーロス家の跡取りだと<line-break>知ったら、態度を変えたのか？<end>"
EN: "Maybe I never told you I was a nobleman's son. But if you'd known I was a noble―and heir to House Pharos―would you have treated me any differently?<end>"
KO: "아, 내가 귀족의 아들이란 걸 네겐 말하지 않았나 보군. 하지만 내가 귀족, 파로스 가문의 후계자라는 걸 알았으면 태도를 바꿨겠나?<end>"

## 520091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違うだろう？<line-break>俺は、少なくともそう信じてたんでな。<line-break>それなら言わなくてもいいだろ？<end>"
EN: "You wouldn't have, would you? At least, that's what I believed. So there was no need to tell you.<end>"
KO: "그건 아니잖아? 적어도 나는 그렇게 믿었으니까. 그렇다면 굳이 말 안 해도 되겠지?<end>"

## 520092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、親父にも困ったもんだ。　　　<line-break>今から軍を急がせても<line-break>間に合わないって。<line-break>急ぐもんだから隊列がバラバラだ。<line-break>アンギルダンのエジキになりに<line-break>行くようなもんだな、こりゃ。<end>"
EN: "Father really is hopeless. Even if he forces the army onward now, it won't arrive in time. The rush has already broken the formation apart. At this rate, we're marching straight into Angeerdan's jaws.<end>"
KO: "하지만 아버지도 곤란하군. 지금부터 군을 서둘러도 늦는다니까. 급하게 움직이니 대열이 엉망이야. 이건 앙길단의 먹잇감이 되러 가는 거나 마찬가지군.<end>"

## 520093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さっき、親父にはそう言ってきたさ。<line-break>そうしたら、不吉なことは言うなって　<line-break>殴られたよ。<line-break>現状の方がよっぽど不吉なんだがな。　<line-break>困ったもんだ。<end>"
EN: "I told Father as much a moment ago. He hit me and said not to speak of ill omens. The situation itself is a far worse omen, if you ask me. He's hopeless.<end>"
KO: "아까 아버지한테도 그렇게 말했지. 그랬더니 재수 없는 소리 하지 말라며 한 대 맞았어. 지금 상황이 훨씬 더 불길한데 말이야. 참 곤란하군.<end>"

## 520094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここは動かずに、<line-break>レムオンの王都守備軍と連携して、<line-break>挟み撃ちの構えで敵を牽制すべきだ…<line-break>そう言って、火に油だ。<line-break>親父は、レムオンがよほど嫌いらしい。<end>"
EN: "I told him we should hold our position, coordinate with Lemghon's capital defense force, and keep the enemy in check with the threat of a pincer attack. That only made him angrier. Father must truly hate Lemghon.<end>"
KO: "여기서 움직이지 말고 레무온의 왕도 수비군과 연계해서 협공 태세로 적을 견제해야 한다… 그렇게 말해서 불에 기름을 부은 셈이지. 아버지는 레무온을 어지간히 싫어하는 모양이야.<end>"

## 520095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール貴族の名誉にかけて<line-break>敵の侵入を、黙っ見ているわけには<line-break>いかないってさ。<line-break>巻き込まれる兵にとっちゃいい迷惑だ。<line-break>とにかく説得は続けよう。<line-break>…兵の命がかかってる。<end>"
EN: "He says the honor of Rostorl's nobility won't allow him to stand by while the enemy invades. The soldiers dragged into it are the ones who'll suffer. I'll keep trying to persuade him. Their lives depend on it.<end>"
KO: "로스톨 귀족의 명예를 걸고 적의 침입을 가만히 보고 있을 수는 없대. 휘말리는 병사들만 죽을 맛이지. 어쨌든 설득은 계속하자. …병사들 목숨이 걸렸어.<end>"

## 520096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、ゼネテスの説得は聞き入れられず、<line-break>隊列が乱れたまま到着したロストール軍は　<line-break>アンギルダンの攻撃を受け、壊滅した。<line-break><line-break>総司令ノヴィン・ファーロスをはじめ<line-break>ロストールの名だたる貴族が、<line-break>この戦いの中で命を落とした。<end>"
EN: "But Xenetes's pleas went unheeded. The Rostorl army arrived with its formation still in disarray, and Angeerdan's attack annihilated it. Commander-in-Chief Novin Pharos and many of Rostorl's most renowned nobles lost their lives in the battle.<end>"
KO: "하지만 제네테스의 설득은 받아들여지지 않았고, 대열이 흐트러진 채 도착한 로스톨군은 앙길단의 공격을 받아 괴멸했다. 총사령 노빈 파로스를 비롯해 로스톨의 이름난 귀족들이 이 전투에서 목숨을 잃었다.<end>"

## 520097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルブルグ伯タルテュバ・リューガは<line-break>自分の軍を捨て、ひとりだけ極秘に逃走。　　<line-break>命は助かったが、その評判は地に落ちた。<end>"
EN: "Count Tartuba Ryuga of Ruburg abandoned his army and secretly fled alone. He survived, but his reputation was ruined.<end>"
KO: "루브르그 백작 타르튜바 류가는 자신의 군대를 버리고 홀로 극비리에 도주했다. 목숨은 건졌지만 평판은 땅에 떨어졌다.<end>"

## 520098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル帝国軍アンギルダンは、<line-break>ロストール軍を壊滅させると、深追いを禁じ、<line-break>全軍に物資の現地調達を命じた。<end>"
EN: "After annihilating the Rostorl army, Angeerdan forbade pursuit and ordered the entire Dyneskal imperial army to procure supplies locally.<end>"
KO: "딘갈 제국군의 앙길단은 로스톨군을 괴멸시키자 추격을 금하고 전군에 현지에서 물자를 조달하라고 명했다.<end>"

## 520099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…と、総司令閣下は自刃なされ、<line-break>ロストール貴族にふさわしい<line-break>見事な最期を遂げられました。<end>"
EN: "And so the Commander-in-Chief took his own life, meeting a splendid end worthy of a Rostorl noble.<end>"
KO: "…그렇게 총사령관 각하께서는 자결하시어 로스톨 귀족다운 훌륭한 최후를 맞으셨습니다.<end>"

## 520100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、これだけの人数を<line-break>こんな状況に落とし込んでおいて、<line-break>ご自分は貴族の誇りと心中、か。<end>"
EN: "Good grief. After landing all these people in this mess, he chose to die with his noble pride.<end>"
KO: "이런 이런, 이 많은 사람을 이런 상황에 몰아넣고 본인은 귀족의 긍지와 함께 죽겠다는 건가.<end>"

## 520101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かくなる上は、ゼネテス様にも<line-break>誇りを汚さず、潔く自刃なさるようにと<line-break>閣下はおっしゃっておいででした。<end>"
EN: "The Commander also said that, matters having come to this, Lord Xenetes should preserve his honor and take his own life with dignity.<end>"
KO: "이렇게 된 이상 제네테스 님께서도 긍지를 더럽히지 말고 깨끗이 자결하시라고 각하께서 말씀하셨습니다.<end>"

## 520102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなロマンチズムに<line-break>つきあう気はないな。<end>"
EN: "I've no intention of indulging that sort of romanticism.<end>"
KO: "그런 낭만주의에 어울려 줄 생각은 없어.<end>"

## 520103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "閣下はこうもおっしゃっておいででした。<line-break>ゼネテス様には、やや貴族としての<line-break>常識にかけるところがある。<line-break>命を惜しみ、おめおめと生き残ろうと<line-break>するならば、ゼネテス様の介錯を<line-break>してさしあげるようにと…！<end>"
EN: "The Commander said something else as well. Lord Xenetes is somewhat lacking in the common sense expected of a nobleman. If you should cling to life and shamelessly try to survive, I was to act as your second and see to your death myself!<end>"
KO: "각하께서는 이런 말씀도 하셨습니다. 제네테스 님은 귀족으로서의 상식이 조금 부족하니, 목숨을 아까워하며 구차하게 살아남으려 한다면 제네테스 님을 직접 편히 보내 드리라고…!<end>"

## 520104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ～て、と。<line-break>生き残る方法を考えなきゃ、だな。　　<line-break>　<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁんは<line-break>俺らの殲滅よりも、<line-break>物資調達の方を優先させた。<end>"
EN: "Well then. Time to work out how to survive. Old Angeerdan has placed securing supplies ahead of finishing us off.<end>"
KO: "자~ 그럼. 살아남을 방법을 생각해야겠군. 앙길단 영감은 우리를 전멸시키는 것보다 물자 조달을 우선했어.<end>"

## 520105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダンのとっつぁんは正しい。<line-break>レムオンの王都守備軍が<line-break>攻めてくる可能性を考えるなら…<line-break>戦意をなくし、無力化した<line-break>俺らを無視して、自分たちの防備を<line-break>固める準備を急ぐのは、理の当然だ。<end>"
EN: "Old Angeerdan's reasoning is sound. With the possibility that Lemghon's capital defense force may attack, it makes perfect sense to ignore us as a demoralized, neutralized force and hurry to strengthen his own defenses.<end>"
KO: "앙길단 영감의 판단은 맞아. 레무온의 왕도 수비군이 공격해 올 가능성을 생각한다면… 전의를 잃고 무력화된 우리를 무시하고 자기들 방비를 굳히는 준비를 서두르는 건 당연하지.<end>"

## 520106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、それが今回は間違いでな。<line-break>俺たちはまだ、<line-break>戦意はなくしちゃいなかったりする。　<end>"
EN: "But this time, that happens to be a mistake. We haven't quite lost our will to fight yet.<end>"
KO: "하지만 이번에는 그게 틀렸어. 우리는 아직 전의를 잃지 않았거든.<end>"

## 520107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺はあきらめの悪い男でな。　　　<line-break>生き残るためのガッツなら<line-break>売るほどある。<line-break>今なら向こうさんも攻めてくるとは<line-break>思ってない。徴発隊も出払っていて<line-break>敵中枢部に直でなぐり込む好機だ！<end>"
EN: "I'm a stubborn man. When it comes to the will to survive, I've got plenty to spare. They won't expect us to attack now, and their requisitioning parties are all away. It's the perfect chance to strike straight at their command!<end>"
KO: "난 포기가 느린 남자라서 말이야. 살아남는 근성이라면 남아돌아. 지금이라면 저쪽도 우리가 쳐들어올 거라 생각하지 않을 거야. 징발대도 나가 있으니 적 중추부를 곧장 때릴 절호의 기회다!<end>"

## 520108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無傷な俺の部隊を中心に<line-break>ロストール軍の敗残兵をまとめ、<line-break>敵ディンガル軍中枢を叩く！　　　　　<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁんを倒せば<line-break>残りのディンガル軍は浮き足だって<line-break>無力化する！　ねらうは朱雀将軍のみ！<end>"
EN: "We'll rally the remnants of Rostorl's army around my unscathed unit and strike the heart of the Dyneskal Army! Once old Angeerdan falls, the remaining Dyneskal forces will lose their nerve and cease to be a threat. The Vermilion Bird General is our sole target!<end>"
KO: "멀쩡한 내 부대를 중심으로 로스톨군 패잔병을 모아 적 딩갈군의 중추를 친다! 앙길단 아저씨를 쓰러뜨리면 나머지 딩갈군은 동요해서 무력화돼! 노릴 건 주작장군 하나뿐이다!<end>"

## 520109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は、俺が軍をまとめる間に、<line-break>先鋒を率い、先に帝国軍に突っ込んで、<line-break>大暴れしてくれ。<line-break>お前の活躍を聞けば、<line-break>敗残兵の集まりもよくなる。<line-break>危険な任務だが、お前しか頼れん。<end>"
EN: "While I rally the army, take the vanguard and charge into the imperial forces ahead of us. Give them hell. Word of your exploits will draw more survivors to our banner. It's dangerous, but you're the only one I can trust with it.<end>"
KO: "내가 군을 수습하는 동안 넌 선봉을 이끌고 먼저 제국군에 돌진해 크게 날뛰어 줘. 네 활약이 알려지면 패잔병들도 더 모일 거다. 위험한 임무지만 너밖에 믿을 사람이 없다.<end>"

## 520110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "５分でいい。<line-break>頼むぜ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Just give me five minutes. I'm counting on you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "5분이면 돼. 부탁한다, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 520111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカな！　ロストール軍だと！<end>"
EN: "Impossible! The Rostorl army?<end>"
KO: "말도 안 돼! 로스톨군이라고!<end>"

## 520112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "徴発のため、ほとんど<line-break>軍が出払っているときに！<end>"
EN: "Now, when nearly all our forces are out requisitioning supplies!<end>"
KO: "징발 때문에 군이 거의 다 나가 있는 때에!<end>"

## 520113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奇襲だ！<line-break>とにかく、もみつぶせ！<end>"
EN: "It's a surprise attack! Crush them at once!<end>"
KO: "기습이다! 어쨌든 짓뭉개 버려!<end>"

## 520114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、どうしたことだ！<line-break>ロストール軍が復活したとは！<end>"
EN: "Damn! How can this be? The Rostorl army has rallied!<end>"
KO: "큭, 이게 어떻게 된 일이냐! 로스톨군이 부활했다니!<end>"

## 520115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス…、<line-break>そうか、お主がおったのか！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お主ならば、あの敗軍を<line-break>まとめる器量がある！<line-break>くっ、ぬかったわ！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes... Of course, you were there! You have the skill to rally that defeated army. Damn it, I miscalculated!<end>"
KO: "제네테스…. 그렇군, 네놈이 있었나! 네놈이라면 그 패잔군을 수습할 역량이 있다! 큭, 방심했군!<end>"

## 520116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主がおるとわかっておれば、<line-break>敗残兵の殲滅を優先させたものを！<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Had I known you were there, I would have made annihilating the survivors my first priority!<end>"
KO: "네놈이 있는 줄 알았다면 패잔병 섬멸을 우선했을 것을!<end>"

## 520117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…とっつぁん、<line-break>悪いが、命をもらう。　　<line-break>　<line-break>…ロストールの兵の<line-break>命がかかっているんでな。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, old man, but I'm taking your life. The lives of Rostorl's soldiers depend on it.<end>"
KO: "…아저씨, 미안하지만 목숨을 받겠다. …로스톨 병사들의 목숨이 걸려 있어서 말이야.<end>"

## 520118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい、そうですかと<line-break>くれてやるわけにはいかんな。<line-break>　<line-break>こっちも<line-break>ディンガルの兵の命が<line-break>かかっておるのでな！<end>"
EN: "I cannot simply hand it over because you ask. The lives of Dyneskal's soldiers depend on me as well!<end>"
KO: "네, 그렇습니까 하고 순순히 내줄 수는 없지. 이쪽도 딘갈 병사들의 목숨이 걸려 있으니까!<end>"

## 520119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ……、<line-break>運命の女神は残酷なものよ！　　　<line-break>ここまで来て、わしを見限るとはな…。<line-break>ゼネテスよ…。<line-break>お主ほどの男が…ファーロスの…<line-break>跡取りだったとは…、うぐっ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh... The Goddess of Fate is cruel indeed! To forsake me after I have come so far... Xenetes... To think a man such as you was heir to Pharos... Guh...<end>"
KO: "후후…… 운명의 여신은 잔혹하구나! 여기까지 와서 나를 버리다니…. 제네테스여…. 자네 같은 사내가… 파로스의… 후계자였다니… 윽….<end>"

## 520120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退け！<line-break>バラバラに地下王国を通るのだ！<line-break>みな…生き延びるの…じゃ…！<end>"
EN: "Retreat! Pass through the Underground Kingdom separately! All of you... survive...!<end>"
KO: "물러나라! 뿔뿔이 지하왕국을 통과해라! 모두… 살아남아라…!<end>"

## 520121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "朱雀将軍が討ち取られたぞ！<end>"
EN: "The Vermilion Bird General has been slain!<end>"
KO: "주작장군이 쓰러졌다!<end>"

## 520122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退け！　退けええい！<end>"
EN: "Retreat! Retreeeat!<end>"
KO: "물러나라! 물러나아아!<end>"

## 520123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敗残兵を驚くべき早さで立て直した<line-break>ゼネテスの奇襲により、アンギルダンが<line-break>討ち取られると、帝国軍は潰走した。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes rallied the defeated troops with astonishing speed and launched a surprise attack. When Angeerdan was slain, the imperial army broke and fled.<end>"
KO: "패잔병을 놀라운 속도로 재정비한 제네테스의 기습으로 앙길단이 전사하자 제국군은 무너져 도주했다.<end>"

## 520124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この戦いで、ゼネテスの声望は<line-break>一気に上がり、奇跡の名将と呼ばれたが、<line-break>ロストールの被害も甚大なものがあった。<end>"
EN: "The battle made Xenetes's reputation soar, and he became known as a miraculous general. Yet Rostorl, too, had suffered grievous losses.<end>"
KO: "이 싸움으로 제네테스의 명성은 단숨에 높아져 기적의 명장이라 불렸지만, 로스톨의 피해도 막대했다.<end>"

## 520125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…千年樹に刻まれし竜綱の盟約に基づき<line-break>我、ロストール王<line-break>セルモノー・リューは…<line-break>汝、ゼネテス・ファーロスを<line-break>ファーロス家当主と認め、その功により<line-break>ロストール王国軍総司令に任ずる。<end>"
EN: "By the Covenant of the Dragon's Bond, graven upon the Thousand-Year Tree, I, Selmono Ryu, King of Rostorl, hereby recognize you, Xenetes Pharos, as head of House Pharos and, in honor of your service, appoint you commander-in-chief of the army of the Kingdom of Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "…천년수에 새겨진 용강의 맹약에 따라 나, 로스톨 왕 셀모노 류는… 그대 제네테스 파로스를 파로스 가문의 당주로 인정하고 그 공로로 로스톨 왕국군 총사령관에 임명한다.<end>"

## 520126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最高の殊勲こそ<line-break>ファーロスのドラ息子に奪われたが、<line-break>第二の殊勲には、お前が認められた。<line-break>ファーロスの次というのが気に入らんが<line-break>よくぞ、リューガ家の面目を<line-break>施してくれた。俺もうれしい。<end>"
EN: "The highest distinction may have gone to that spoiled son of Pharos, but you were recognized for the second-highest. I dislike seeing you placed behind a Pharos, but you have done well to restore the good name of House Ryuga. That pleases me too.<end>"
KO: "최고 공훈은 파로스의 망나니 아들에게 빼앗겼지만 두 번째 공훈은 네가 인정받았다. 파로스 다음이라는 게 마음에 안 들지만 류가 가문의 체면을 세워 줬군. 나도 기쁘다.<end>"

## 520127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバのバカが、臆病風に吹かれ、<line-break>兵を捨てて、自分だけこっそり逃げて…<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "That fool Tartuba lost his nerve, abandoned his soldiers, and slipped away alone...<end>"
KO: "타르튜바 그 바보가 겁먹고 병사들을 버린 채 자기만 몰래 도망쳐서…<end>"

## 520128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分の顔とリューガ家にたっぷりと　　<line-break>泥を塗ってくれたのでな。<line-break>お前は見事に名誉を挽回してくれた。<line-break>俺はそれを喜んでいるのだ。<line-break>お前の成功を喜んでいるわけではない。<line-break>…カン違いはするな。<end>"
EN: "He thoroughly disgraced himself and House Ryuga. You have splendidly restored our honor, and that is what pleases me. It is not your success itself that I celebrate. Do not misunderstand.<end>"
KO: "내 얼굴과 류가 가문에 잔뜩 먹칠했으니 말이다. 너는 훌륭하게 명예를 회복했어. 나는 그게 기쁜 거다. 네 성공 자체를 기뻐하는 건 아니야. …착각하지 마.<end>"

## 520129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブル伯、<line-break><value:$28>・リューガ。<end>"
EN: "Count <value:$28> Ryuga of Noble.<end>"
KO: "노블 백작, <value:$28>·류가.<end>"

## 520130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、行け。<end>"
EN: "Go on, then.<end>"
KO: "자, 가.<end>"

## 520131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、肩が凝った。<line-break>大げさな式典だったな。<line-break>　<line-break>お互い、これから<line-break>めんどくさいことになるな。<line-break>それじゃあな。<end>"
EN: "Good grief, my shoulders are killing me. What an overblown ceremony. Looks like things are going to be a nuisance for both of us from now on. See you around.<end>"
KO: "이런, 어깨가 결리는군. 거창한 의식이었어. 서로 앞으로 귀찮은 일이 많아지겠군. 그럼 잘 가.<end>"

## 520132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>の<line-break>名声値が上がった。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>'s Fame increased.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>의 명성치가 올랐다.<end>"

## 520133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "総司令閣下、<line-break>ノーブル伯<value:$28>様が<line-break>おいでです。<end>"
EN: "Commander-in-Chief, Count <value:$28> of Noble has arrived.<end>"
KO: "총사령관 각하, 노블 백작 <value:$28> 님께서 오셨습니다.<end>"

## 520134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0レムオンの弟か…。<end>レムオンの妹か…。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Lemghon's brother...<end>Lemghon's sister...<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0레무온의 남동생인가….<end>레무온의 여동생인가….<end>"

## 520135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、作戦会議を始める。<end>"
EN: "Let us begin the council of war.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 작전회의를 시작한다.<end>"

## 520136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ディンガル軍は我々の予想に違い<line-break>内海を縦断する進軍路をとらず、<line-break>分断の山脈越えで王国領に進入した。<end>"
EN: "Contrary to our expectations, the Dyneskal Army did not cross the Inland Sea. It entered the kingdom by crossing the Dividing Mountains.<end>"
KO: "딘갈군은 우리의 예상과 달리 내해를 가로지르는 진군로를 택하지 않고 분단의 산맥을 넘어 왕국령으로 침입했다.<end>"

## 520137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス殿の予想通りだ。<end>"
EN: "Just as Lord Xenetes predicted.<end>"
KO: "제네테스 공의 예상대로다.<end>"

## 520138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "総司令閣下、<line-break>ご子息のゼネテス殿は？<end>"
EN: "Commander-in-Chief, where is your son, Lord Xenetes?<end>"
KO: "총사령관 각하, 아드님 제네테스 경은?<end>"

## 520139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あまり聞き分けがないのでな。　　　<line-break>後方に配置した。<line-break>　<line-break>帝国の進入に対し、<line-break>動くべきではないなどと、<line-break>愚にもつかないことばかり言うのだ。<end>"
EN: "He refused to listen, so I stationed him in the rear. He kept spouting nonsense about how we should not move against the imperial incursion.<end>"
KO: "너무 말을 안 들어서 후방에 배치했다. 제국의 침입에 움직여선 안 된다느니 하는 어리석은 소리만 하거든.<end>"

## 520140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヒャーヒャッヒャッ！<line-break>とんだ、腰抜けだな。<line-break>ファーロスのドラ息子は。　<line-break>後方に置いておいた方がいい。<line-break>危険も少ないからな。<line-break>まったくのクズの臆病者だ。<end>"
EN: "Hya-hya-hya! What a coward that spoiled son of Pharos is! Best leave him in the rear, where there is less danger. A worthless, craven wretch!<end>"
KO: "햐햐햐! 엄청난 겁쟁이로군. 파로스의 망나니 아들은. 후방에 두는 게 좋겠어. 위험도 적으니까. 정말 쓰레기 같은 겁쟁이야.<end>"

## 520141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…コホン。とにかくだ。<line-break>敵が都市を占領し、地歩を固めないうちに<line-break>早急に敵をうち破る必要がある。<line-break>そのために、我々は軍を急行させ、<line-break>敵に対し、奇襲をかける所存である。　　<line-break>何か質問は？<end>"
EN: "Ahem. In any event, we must defeat the enemy quickly, before they can occupy the cities and consolidate their position. We therefore intend to force-march our army and launch a surprise attack. Any questions?<end>"
KO: "…흠. 아무튼 그렇다. 적이 도시를 점령해 발판을 굳히기 전에 서둘러 적을 격파해야 한다. 이를 위해 우리는 군을 급파해 적에게 기습을 가할 생각이다. 질문 있나?<end>"

## 520142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵はこちらが来るのを予測しているのでは？<end>"
EN: "Could the enemy be expecting us?<end>"
KO: "적이 우리가 오는 걸 예상하고 있는 게 아닐까요?<end>"

## 520143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "軍を急行させては、隊列が乱れるのでは？<end>"
EN: "Will a forced march not throw our formation into disarray?<end>"
KO: "군을 급히 움직이면 대열이 흐트러지지 않겠습니까?<end>"

## 520144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何もない<end>"
EN: "No questions<end>"
KO: "아무것도 없다<end>"

## 520145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だからこそ、急ぐのだ。<line-break>敵がこちらを待ちかまえる用意を<line-break>する前に、敵を叩かねばならない！<end>"
EN: "That is precisely why we must hurry. We must strike before the enemy can prepare to meet us!<end>"
KO: "그러니까 서두르는 거다. 적이 우리를 기다릴 준비를 하기 전에 쳐야 해!<end>"

## 520146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブル伯、<line-break>では、どうしろというのだ、<line-break>侵略者が攻めてきているのに。<line-break>放っておくつもりか？<line-break>そのようなことができるのか？<line-break>それでもロストール貴族か？<end>"
EN: "Then what would you have us do, Count of Noble? Invaders are advancing upon us. Do you propose that we ignore them? Could you truly do such a thing? Are you not a noble of Rostorl?<end>"
KO: "노블 백작, 그럼 어쩌라는 건가. 침략자가 쳐들어오고 있는데 내버려 두자는 건가? 그런 짓이 가능한가? 그래도 로스톨 귀족인가?<end>"

## 520147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。<end>"
EN: "Very well.<end>"
KO: "그런가.<end>"

## 520148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、会議は以上だ<line-break>各人の奮戦を望む。<end>"
EN: "That concludes the council. I expect every one of you to fight valiantly.<end>"
KO: "그럼 회의는 여기까지다. 각자의 분전을 기대하겠다.<end>"

## 520149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ロストール軍総司令ノヴィン・ファーロスは　　<line-break>ディンガル帝国軍の海路による進攻を<line-break>予測し、上陸地点で攻撃すべく軍を進めた。<end>"
EN: "Novin Pharos, commander-in-chief of the Rostorl army, predicted that the Dyneskal imperial forces would advance by sea. He moved his army to attack at their landing point.<end>"
KO: "로스톨군 총사령 노빈 파로스는 딘갈 제국군이 해로로 진공할 것을 예상하고 상륙 지점에서 공격하기 위해 군을 전진시켰다.<end>"

## 520150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、ディンガルの将アンギルダンが選んだ　<line-break>道は海路ではなく、分断の山脈を越えるという<line-break>歴史上、誰も取らなかった進軍路であった。<end>"
EN: "But Angeerdan, the Dyneskal commander, did not choose the sea route. Instead, he took a route unprecedented in history, crossing the Dividing Mountains.<end>"
KO: "하지만 딩갈의 장군 앙길단이 선택한 길은 해로가 아니라 분단의 산맥을 넘는, 역사상 누구도 택하지 않았던 진군로였다.<end>"

## 520151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アンギルダン率いるディンガル軍は　　　　　　<line-break>大街道沿いの都市を電撃的に占領。<line-break>王都ロストール攻略に王手をかけた。<end>"
EN: "The Dyneskal Army under Angeerdan captured the cities along the great highway in a lightning offensive. The Rostorl Campaign was now one move from victory.<end>"
KO: "앙길단이 이끄는 딘갈군은 대가도 연변 도시들을 전격적으로 점령했다. 왕도 로스톨 공략을 코앞에 두었다.<end>"

## 520152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "対して、ノヴィン率いるロストール軍は<line-break>これを迎撃すべく、軍を戻した。<line-break>しかし、これはアンギルダンに予測されていた。<line-break><line-break>急行したため隊列もバラバラなロストール軍は<line-break>待ち受けていたディンガル軍によって<line-break>完膚無きまでに叩きのめされ、壊乱した。<end>"
EN: "The Rostorl army under Novin turned back to intercept the invaders, but Angeerdan had anticipated this. Forced to hurry, the Rostorl army arrived with its formation in tatters. The waiting Dyneskal Army crushed it utterly and sent it into rout.<end>"
KO: "이에 맞서 노빈이 이끄는 로스톨군은 요격하기 위해 군을 되돌렸다. 하지만 이것은 앙길단이 예상한 일이었다. 급히 달려오느라 대열이 엉망이 된 로스톨군은 기다리고 있던 딘갈군에게 철저히 짓밟혀 붕괴했다.<end>"

## 520153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "総司令ノヴィン・ファーロスをはじめ　　　　　<line-break>ロストールの貴族が多く、乱戦の中で<line-break>命を落とした。<end>"
EN: "Commander-in-Chief Novin Pharos and many other Rostorl nobles lost their lives in the chaos of battle.<end>"
KO: "총사령 노빈 파로스를 비롯해 많은 로스톨 귀족들이 난전 속에서 목숨을 잃었다.<end>"

## 520154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "の、ノーブル伯、お逃げください！<line-break>ぐわっ！<end>"
EN: "C-Count of Noble, run! Gah!<end>"
KO: "노, 노블 백작님, 도망치십시오! 으악!<end>"

## 520155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ノーブル伯！　ぐわっ！<line-break>た、助けてください！　ぐわー！<end>"
EN: "Count of Noble! Gah! P-please, help me! Aagh!<end>"
KO: "노블 백작! 윽! 사, 살려 주세요! 으악!<end>"

## 520156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やんごとなき貴族とお見受けする。<line-break>天命は我らが皇帝ネメア様にあり！<line-break>お命頂戴！<end>"
EN: "You appear to be a noble of exalted birth. Heaven's mandate rests with our Emperor Nemea! Your life is mine!<end>"
KO: "귀한 신분의 귀족으로 보이는군. 천명은 우리 황제 네메아 님께 있다! 목을 내놓아라!<end>"

## 520157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お主…<value:$28>…。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>戦場をひとりで死守する<line-break>ロストール貴族がいると聞いて<line-break>来てみれば、お主じゃったのか！<end>"
EN: "You... <value:$28>... I heard a Rostorl noble was defending this battlefield single-handedly, so I came to see. And it was you!<end>"
KO: "자네… <value:$28>…. 전장을 혼자 사수하는로스톨 귀족이 있다기에와 봤더니, 자네였나!<end>"

## 520158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これも戦場のならい。<line-break>ゆくぞ、<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Such is the way of the battlefield. Come, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "이것도 전장의 법도다. 간다, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 520159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦場をひとりで守る<line-break>ロストール貴族とはお主か！<end>"
EN: "So you are the Rostorl noble defending this battlefield single-handedly!<end>"
KO: "전장을 혼자 지키는 로스톨 귀족이란 네놈이냐!<end>"

## 520160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ抵抗をやめぬか…天晴れなり！<line-break>勇士よ！　名を名乗られよ！<end>"
EN: "Still you refuse to yield... Splendid! Brave warrior, state your name!<end>"
KO: "아직 저항을 멈추지 않는가… 훌륭하도다! 용사여! 이름을 밝혀라!<end>"

## 520161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我はディンガル帝国の<line-break>朱雀将軍アンギルダン！<line-break>この遠征軍の総司令官である！　　　<line-break>ノーブル伯<value:$28>よ。<line-break>お主の名はロストールが滅びようとも<line-break>永遠に語りつがれようぞ。<end>"
EN: "I am Angeerdan, Vermilion Bird General of the Dyneskal Empire and supreme commander of this expeditionary force! Count <value:$28> of Noble, even if Rostorl should fall, your name will be spoken of for all eternity!<end>"
KO: "나는 딩갈 제국의 주작장군 앙길단! 이 원정군의 총사령관이다! 노블 백작 <value:$28>여. 그대의 이름은 로스톨이 멸망하더라도 영원히 전해질 것이다.<end>"

## 520162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、まいる！<line-break>ノーブル伯<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "Now, have at you, Count <value:$28> of Noble!<end>"
KO: "그럼 간다! 노블 백작 <value:$28>!<end>"

## 520163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ガアアアアッ！<end>"
EN: "Graaaargh!<end>"
KO: "…가아아아악!<end>"

## 520164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふぅ…、ふぅ…わ、わしも<line-break>…ここで、死ぬわけにはゆかぬ。　　　<line-break>兵どもの命を背負っておるのでな。<line-break>手強き敵であった…。<line-break>わしの手で、介錯しよう。<line-break>奮戦へのせめてもの手向けじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Hah... hah... I-I cannot die here. I bear the lives of my soldiers upon my shoulders. You were a formidable foe... I shall end your suffering with my own hand. Let this be my final tribute to your valor.<end>"
KO: "후우… 후우… 나, 나도 여기서 죽을 수는 없다. 병사들의 목숨을 짊어지고 있으니. 강한 적이었다…. 내 손으로 편히 보내 주마. 분전한 데 대한 최소한의 전별이다.<end>"

## 520165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "敵襲！<end>"
EN: "Enemy attack!<end>"
KO: "적습!<end>"

## 520166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだと！？　<line-break>味方が徴発に出払っているすきを<line-break>ついてきたというのか！<end>"
EN: "What?! They struck while our forces were away on requisition duty?!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고!? 아군이 징발하러 나간 틈을 노리고 온 거냐!<end>"

## 520167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、あそこまで壊乱した<line-break>敗残兵をまとめられる者など<line-break>いるわけがない。誤報ではないのか？<end>"
EN: "But no one could possibly rally remnants routed that badly. Could the report be false?<end>"
KO: "하지만 그렇게까지 무너진 패잔병을 수습할 자가 있을 리 없다. 오보 아닌가?<end>"

## 520168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頭だけで考えると<line-break>現実が見えなくなるぜ！<end>"
EN: "Use nothing but your head, and you lose sight of what's real!<end>"
KO: "머리로만 생각하면 현실이 안 보인다고!<end>"

## 520169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテス、<line-break>まさか、お主が…。　　　　　　　　<line-break>いや、まさかなどということがあるか！<line-break>あの崩れ去った敗残兵をまとめる器量、<line-break>意表をつく、奇襲をやる才覚と度胸、<line-break>お主でなくて誰にある！<end>"
EN: "Xenetes... Can it truly be you...? No. Who else could it be? Who else could rally those shattered remnants, then launch a surprise attack with such cunning and nerve?<end>"
KO: "…제네테스, 설마 네가…. 아니, 설마라는 게 있을 리 있나! 무너져버린 패잔병을 규합할 그릇, 허를 찌르는 기습을 해낼 재능과 배짱, 네가 아니면 누가 있겠느냐!<end>"

## 520170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ！<line-break>アンギルダンのとっつぁん。<line-break>俺がファーロスのドラ息子だ。<line-break>悪いが、死んでもらう。<line-break>俺は今、ロストールの兵と<line-break>民を背負ってるんでな。<end>"
EN: "That's right, old man Angeerdan. I'm Pharos's good-for-nothing son. Sorry, but you're going to die. I've got the soldiers and people of Rostorl on my shoulders now.<end>"
KO: "그래! 앙길단 아저씨. 내가 파로스의 망나니 아들이다. 미안하지만 죽어 줘야겠어. 지금 나는 로스톨의 병사와 백성을 짊어지고 있으니까.<end>"

## 520171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしとても同じじゃ。<line-break>ディンガルの兵のために<line-break>お主を倒す！　ゆくぞ！<end>"
EN: "And I am no different. For the soldiers of Dyneskal, I shall defeat you! Come!<end>"
KO: "나 또한 마찬가지다. 딘갈 병사들을 위해 너를 쓰러뜨리겠다! 간다!<end>"

## 520172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec052-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくやったな。<line-break>やるじゃねえか。<end>"
EN: "Well done. Not bad at all.<end>"
KO: "잘했군. 제법인데.<end>"

## 530001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺が部下を見捨てて何が悪い…。　　　<line-break>俺とあいつらゴミどもとは違うんだ。　<line-break>　<line-break>俺は貴族なんだ、俺は貴族なんだ、<line-break>俺は、と、と、特別なんだアアア！！！<line-break>あいつらとは価値が違うのだ！　俺は！<end>"
EN: "What's wrong with abandoning my men...? I'm not like those worthless pieces of trash. I'm a noble! I'm a noble! I'm s-s-special! I'M WORTH MORE THAN THEY ARE! I AM!<end>"
KO: "내가 부하를 버린 게 뭐가 나빠…. 나와 저 쓰레기들은 다르다고. 나는 귀족이야, 나는 귀족이야, 나는 트, 트, 특별하다고오오!!! 저놈들과는 가치가 달라! 나는!<end>"

## 530002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "同じ負け戦に出て、どうしてどうしてエ<line-break>ゼネテスはみんなの脚光を浴びて、俺は<line-break>俺は俺は俺だけは白い目で見られるウ？<line-break>ゼネテスは貴族の義務を放棄したクズ…<line-break>そう、クズクズクズクズ…。<end>"
EN: "We fought in the same losing battle, so why―why does Xenetes get all the glory while I―I'm the only one everyone looks down on? Xenetes cast aside his duty as a noble. He's scum... Yes, scum, scum, scum, scum...<end>"
KO: "똑같이 패전한 전쟁에 나갔는데 왜, 왜 제네테스는 모두의 주목을 받고 나는, 나는, 나만은 차가운 눈총을 받는 거야아? 제네테스는 귀족의 의무를 버린 쓰레기… 그래, 쓰레기쓰레기쓰레기쓰레기….<end>"

## 530003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それなのにどうして俺が白い目で？　　<line-break>俺が俺がア？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Then why do they look down on me? Why me? Meee?<end>"
KO: "그런데 왜 내가 이상한 눈초리를 받아야 해? 내가, 내가아?<end>"

## 530004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くそっくそっくそっくそっ！　クズめ！<line-break>クズめクズめクズめ！　クズのくせに！<line-break>俺をバカにしやがって！　クズがアア！<end>"
EN: "Damn it, damn it, damn it, damn it! Scum! Scum, scum, scum! You're nothing but scum! How dare you make a fool of me! You scu-u-um!<end>"
KO: "젠장젠장젠장젠장! 쓰레기 자식! 쓰레기 쓰레기 쓰레기! 쓰레기 주제에! 나를 바보 취급하고! 이 쓰레기가아아!<end>"

## 530005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おとなしくしろ！<line-break>ルブルグ伯タルテュバ！　　<end>"
EN: "Control yourself, Tartuba, Count of Ruburg!<end>"
KO: "얌전히 있어라! 루브르그 백작 타르튜바!<end>"

## 530006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王宮内での無礼は厳罰だぞ！<line-break>ロストール貴族の面汚しめ！<end>"
EN: "Any insolence in the royal palace will be severely punished! You are a disgrace to the nobility of Rostorl!<end>"
KO: "왕궁 안에서 무례를 범하면 엄벌이다! 로스톨 귀족의 망신이군!<end>"

## 530007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何をオオオオオオオオオオ！<end>"
EN: "How dare youuuuuuu!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고오오오오오오오오!<end>"

## 530008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦場で見苦しいマネをした貴様が、<line-break>王宮内で無礼を働くなど、<line-break>許されざることだ！<end>"
EN: "After your disgraceful conduct on the battlefield, you dare behave so insolently in the royal palace? That is unforgivable!<end>"
KO: "전장에서 추태를 보인 네놈이 왕궁 안에서 무례를 저지르다니 용서할 수 없다!<end>"

## 530009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "戦場で見苦しいマネをした貴様が、<line-break>戦場でご活躍なされた将軍に<line-break>無礼を働くなど許されざることだ！<end>"
EN: "After your disgraceful conduct on the battlefield, you dare insult the general who served with such distinction there? That is unforgivable!<end>"
KO: "전장에서 추태를 보인 네놈이 전장에서 활약하신 장군께 무례를 범하다니 용서할 수 없다!<end>"

## 530010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…昔、アンギルダンのとっつぁんは<line-break>ロストールで傭兵をしてたこともある。<line-break>　<line-break>俺が物心つくかつかないころだ。<line-break>…だが、俺は鮮明に覚えてる。<line-break>ホントに、でけえ人だった。<end>"
EN: "Long ago, old Angeerdan spent some time working as a mercenary in Rostorl. I was barely old enough to know what was going on, but I remember him clearly. He was a truly enormous man.<end>"
KO: "…예전에 앙길단 아저씨는 로스톨에서 용병을 한 적도 있어. 내가 철들기 전후의 일이야. …하지만 난 선명하게 기억해. 정말 큰 사람이었어.<end>"

## 530011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "肩車してもらったこともある。　　　　<line-break>とっつぁんは覚えちゃ<line-break>いなかったろうがな…。<end>"
EN: "I even got a ride on his shoulders. Doubt he remembered it, though.<end>"
KO: "목말을 타 본 적도 있어. 아저씨는 기억도 못 했겠지만….<end>"

## 530012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…剣を振ってくる。<line-break>じゃあな。<end>"
EN: "I'm off to practice with my sword. See you.<end>"
KO: "…검 좀 휘두르고 올게. 그럼.<end>"

## 530013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>か。<line-break>先のディンガルとの戦で　　　　　<line-break>兄、ノヴィンが死んだ。<line-break>気の利かぬ、察しの悪い男だった。<line-break>よく言えば、たくらみのない、<line-break>悪く言えば、ぼんくらな男だった。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, is it? My brother Novin died in the recent war with Dyneskal. He was thoughtless and slow to understand. At best, he was guileless; at worst, a fool.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>인가. 지난 딩갈과의 전쟁에서 형 노빈이 죽었다. 눈치 없고 둔한 남자였지. 좋게 말하면 꿍꿍이가 없고, 나쁘게 말하면 멍청한 남자였다.<end>"

## 530014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "自分のあり方に疑問を抱かなかった。<line-break>一切な。そういう人間は得てして<line-break>はた迷惑なものだが、兄もそうだった。<end>"
EN: "He never once questioned the way he lived. People like that often make nuisances of themselves, and my brother was no exception.<end>"
KO: "자신의 삶의 방식에 의문을 품지 않았어. 전혀. 그런 인간은 대개 주변에 민폐를 끼치는데, 형도 그랬지.<end>"

## 530015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "凡庸だったが、<line-break>死んでみると、やはり悲しいものだ。　<line-break>それが親族というものかもしれぬ。<end>"
EN: "He was an unremarkable man, yet now that he is dead, I find myself grieving. Perhaps that is simply the nature of kinship.<end>"
KO: "평범한 사람이었지만 죽고 나니 역시 슬프군. 그게 혈육이라는 것일지도 모르지.<end>"

## 530016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスは兄の息子。私の甥だ。<line-break>そして、こたびの戦の最功労者だ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>敗軍をまとめ、敵を敗走させた。<line-break>その功をもって、父のあとを継ぎ、<line-break>軍総司令、ファーロス家当主となった。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes is my brother's son, my nephew. He also distinguished himself above all others in this war. He rallied the defeated army and sent the enemy fleeing. For that achievement, he succeeded his father as commander-in-chief and head of House Pharos.<end>"
KO: "제네테스는 형의 아들, 내 조카다. 그리고 이번 전쟁의 최고 공로자지. 패잔병을 수습해 적을 패주시켰다. 그 공으로 아버지의 뒤를 이어 군 총사령관이자 파로스 가문의 당주가 되었다.<end>"

## 530017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、ゼネテスの正体を知って　　　　<line-break>驚いているのか、<line-break><value:$28>？<line-break>驚いているのは私の方だ。<line-break>私の配下がディンガル側にいて<line-break>ロストール軍と戦っているのだからな。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Surprised to learn who Xenetes really is, <value:$28>? I am the one who should be surprised. One of my own retainers was on Dyneskal's side, fighting Rostorl's army.<end>"
KO: "후후, 제네테스의 정체를 알고 놀랐느냐, <value:$28>? 놀란 건 오히려 나다. 내 부하가 딩갈 쪽에서 로스톨군과 싸우고 있으니 말이지.<end>"

## 530018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "リューガ家に売国奴の反逆者を出して<line-break>レムオンを巻き込もうとしたのか？　<line-break>　<line-break>もしそうならば<line-break>以後、そのような<line-break>愚かなマネはせぬことだ。<end>"
EN: "Were you trying to turn a member of House Ryuga into a traitorous rebel and implicate Lemghon? If so, do not attempt anything so foolish again.<end>"
KO: "류가 가문에 매국노 반역자를 만들어 레무온을 끌어들이려 한 건가? 그렇다면 앞으로는 그런 어리석은 짓은 하지 마라.<end>"

## 530019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなたの父フリントは<line-break>決して捨て身の行動はとらなかった。<line-break>なぜだかわかるか？<line-break>捨て身になれば、人は判断力を失う。<line-break>何も守るものがない者は<line-break>強くはなれぬ。<end>"
EN: "Your father Flint never acted with reckless disregard for his own life. Do you know why? Once a person throws life away, sound judgment goes with it. No one with nothing to protect can ever become strong.<end>"
KO: "그대의 아버지 플린트는 결코 목숨을 내던지는 행동을 하지 않았다. 왜인지 아느냐? 자포자기하면 사람은 판단력을 잃는다. 지킬 것이 없는 자는 강해질 수 없다.<end>"

## 530020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フリントはそなたを守ろうとしていた。<line-break>石火のごときフリントの働きは<line-break>そなたあってこその働きだったのだ。<line-break>フリントの、そなたを守ろうとした<line-break>気持ちをムダにするな。<line-break>そして、強くなれ。<end>"
EN: "Flint was trying to protect you. He could act with such lightning speed because he had you to protect. Do not let that devotion go to waste. Become strong.<end>"
KO: "플린트는 그대를 지키려 했다. 번개처럼 빠른 플린트의 활약도 그대가 있었기에 가능했던 것이다. 그대를 지키고자 했던 플린트의 마음을 헛되게 하지 마라. 그리고 강해져라.<end>"

## 530021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、愚かなマネをしてくれた。　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>フリントの子供ゆえ、<line-break>守ってやりたいが、<line-break>事が露見してはかばいきれぬぞ。<end>"
EN: "Well, you have done a foolish thing. You are Flint's child, so I want to protect you, but if this comes to light, I will not be able to shield you.<end>"
KO: "참 어리석은 짓을 했군. 플린트의 자식이라 지켜 주고 싶지만 일이 드러나면 감싸 줄 수 없다.<end>"

## 530022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスには他言無用と<line-break>言いおいたが、誰に知れるか<line-break>わからぬ。気をつけよ。<end>"
EN: "I ordered Xenetes to tell no one, but there is no knowing who may discover the truth. Be careful.<end>"
KO: "제네테스에게는 다른 사람에게 말하지 말라고 해 두었지만 누구에게 알려질지 모른다. 조심해라.<end>"

## 530023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よい。おもてを上げよ。<line-break>そなたひとりでは戦況は変わらぬ。　<line-break>　<line-break>兄、ノヴィンの死も、<line-break>多数のロストールの戦死者もすべて<line-break>兄の責任だ。そなたのせいではない。<end>"
EN: "It is all right. Raise your head. You alone could not have changed the course of the war. The death of my brother Novin, and of so many Rostorl soldiers, was entirely my brother's responsibility. None of it was your fault.<end>"
KO: "좋다. 고개를 들라. 그대 혼자서는 전황이 바뀌지 않는다. 형 노빈의 죽음도, 수많은 로스톨 전사자의 죽음도 모두 형의 책임이다. 그대 탓이 아니다.<end>"

## 530024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くそっくそっくそっくそっ！　クズめ！<line-break>クズめクズめクズめ！　クズのくせに！<line-break>俺をバカにしやがって！　クズがアア！<end>"
EN: "Damn it, damn it, damn it, damn it! Scum! Scum, scum, scum! You're nothing but scum! How dare you make a fool of me! You scu-u-um!<end>"
KO: "젠장젠장젠장젠장! 쓰레기 자식! 쓰레기 쓰레기 쓰레기! 쓰레기 주제에! 나를 바보 취급하고! 이 쓰레기가아아!<end>"

## 530025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…アンギルダンのこと、<line-break>俺もおぼえてるよ。<line-break>当時は、よくここに来てたからな…。<line-break>よく飲む、陽気な、いい奴だった…。<end>"
EN: "I remember Angeerdan too. He used to come here often in those days. He could put away his drink, and he was cheerful. A good man.<end>"
KO: "…앙길단 일은 나도 기억해. 그때는 자주 여기 왔거든…. 술 잘 마시고 유쾌한 좋은 녀석이었지….<end>"

## 530034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "兄は無能ゆえに多くの兵を死なせた。<line-break>死をもって報いるのが当然だろう。<line-break>息子のゼネテスに跡を継がせた。<end>"
EN: "My brother's incompetence cost many soldiers their lives. It was only right that he pay with his own. I appointed his son Xenetes to succeed him.<end>"
KO: "형은 무능해서 수많은 병사를 죽게 했다. 죽음으로 갚는 게 당연하겠지. 아들 제네테스에게 뒤를 잇게 했다.<end>"

## 530035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よくやった。<line-break>戦争などは本来いむべきものだが、<line-break>そなたの活躍、うれしく思うぞ。<line-break>天国のフリントとそなたの母も<line-break>やはり喜んでいよう。<end>"
EN: "You did well. War is something to be abhorred, but I am pleased by what you accomplished. Flint in heaven must be pleased, and your mother must be too.<end>"
KO: "잘했다. 전쟁은 본래 피해야 할 일이지만 네 활약은 기쁘구나. 천국의 플린트와 네 어머니도 분명 기뻐하실 거다.<end>"

## 530036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様。<line-break>七竜家の当主重鎮の方々が<line-break>会談を申し込んでおられます。<line-break>このたびの戦で受けた<line-break>損害の大きさについて、<line-break>お話があるとのことでした。<end>"
EN: "Your Majesty. The heads and senior members of the Seven Dragon Houses have requested a meeting. They wish to discuss the scale of the losses suffered in this war.<end>"
KO: "왕비님. 칠룡가의 당주와 중진들이 회담을 신청했습니다. 이번 전투에서 입은 피해가 큰 것에 대해 말씀드릴 게 있다고 합니다.<end>"

## 530037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、わかった。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>フリントの子、<value:$28>よ。<line-break>また来るとよい。<end>"
EN: "Very well. <value:$28>, child of Flint, you are welcome to return.<end>"
KO: "그래, 알겠다. 플린트의 아이 <value:$28>여. 또 오도록 해라.<end>"

## 530038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec053-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ファーロス・ボリューム３終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "파로스 볼륨 3 끝<end>"

## 540000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "パーティーの編成を変えるのですね。<end>"
EN: "You'd like to change your party lineup, then.<end>"
KO: "파티 편성을 바꾸시는군요.<end>"

## 540001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "パーティーの編成を変えるのね？<end>"
EN: "You want to change your party lineup?<end>"
KO: "파티 편성을 바꾸는 거지?<end>"

## 540002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、どうしますか？<end>"
EN: "Now then, what would you like to do?<end>"
KO: "자, 어떻게 할까요?<end>"

## 540003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうするの？<end>"
EN: "What do you want to do?<end>"
KO: "어떻게 할 거야?<end>"

## 540004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これ以上は、仲間を増やせませんね。　　<line-break>どなたかと入れ替えなければなりません。<line-break>別れる方を決めてください。<end>"
EN: "You cannot add any more companions. You will have to replace someone. Please choose who will leave.<end>"
KO: "더 이상 동료를 늘릴 수 없네요. 누군가와 교체해야 합니다. 헤어질 사람을 정해 주세요.<end>"

## 540005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これ以上、仲間にはできないわよ。<line-break>誰かと別れなければならないわ。<line-break>さあ、選んで。<end>"
EN: "You can't add any more companions. Someone will have to leave. Go on, choose.<end>"
KO: "더는 동료를 늘릴 수 없어. 누군가와 헤어져야 해. 자, 골라.<end>"

## 540006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どなたと別れますか？<end>"
EN: "Who will you part ways with?<end>"
KO: "누구와 헤어질까요?<end>"

## 540007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰と別れるの？<end>"
EN: "Who are you parting ways with?<end>"
KO: "누구와 헤어지는 거야?<end>"

## 540008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他にも別れる方はいますか？<end>"
EN: "Is there anyone else you wish to part ways with?<end>"
KO: "또 헤어질 분이 있습니까?<end>"

## 540009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他にも別れる仲間はいるのかしら？<end>"
EN: "Are there any other companions you want to part ways with?<end>"
KO: "또 헤어질 동료가 있나요?<end>"

## 540010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よろしいですか？<end>"
EN: "Are you sure?<end>"
KO: "괜찮으시겠습니까?<end>"

## 540011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいのね？<end>"
EN: "You're sure?<end>"
KO: "괜찮은 거지?<end>"

## 540012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec054-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どなたをお呼びしますか？<end>"
EN: "Who would you like to call?<end>"
KO: "누구를 부르시겠습니까?<end>"
